# Template map — working draft

> **Working draft, not a module document.** Built module by module with the product owner in the template management review (started 10 Oct 2026). When the map is complete, the final design is decided and written into SA-05 (Templates) and AG-12 (Firm templates), plus knock-ons. Starting draft for that design: the co-product owner's SA-05 v2.4 (`_archive/SA-05-global-templates_v2.4_2026-10-10.md`).
>
> Status: firm-side modules mapped and gap-reviewed after a full read (13 incl. Pathway assessment); shared items decided; **all decisions revisited 11 Oct under rule 7 (the firm owns its legal and regulatory compliance)**. **A full-read verification pass of modules 1–11 is planned before the final design** (they were mapped from targeted searches; module 12 showed that full reading finds more).

---

## Rules agreed so far

1. **Lean ready-made set.** ExpatReady provides only **Must** and **Good-to-have** templates — nothing exhaustive. Firms create whatever extra they want.
2. **Nothing hard-coded.** Every ready-made template is a default the firm can use as is or customise. Default steps (e.g. "follow up on day 3") are also changeable by the firm (Automation module).
3. **Working model — provisional, decided at the end with the full map:**
   - **ExpatReady → firms emails** — only ExpatReady edits.
   - **Automatic messages** — one per event, ExpatReady default wording; firm edits, resets to default, switches optional ones off; never deletes.
   - **Template library** — lean starter set; firms use, edit, delete and create unlimited; used for manual sends and by automation steps.
   - **Different types may need their own design** (noted per module below); a shared foundation applies to all: fill-in fields with privacy rules, English / French (French optional), preview with a real client, send-me-a-test, version history, "ExpatReady updated this" note, use as is or customise.
4. **A template used by a ready-made recipe is always a Must.**
5. **A template arrives in the same release as the feature it belongs to** (e.g. "Refund issued" comes with client refunds, Phase 2 today).
6. **Staff notifications** (the in-app bell, e.g. "New lead assigned to you") — **decided:** fixed wording, not part of template management; staff choose what they get and how.
7. **The firm owns its legal and regulatory compliance (decided 11 Oct — governs every row below).** ExpatReady is vertical SaaS for firms. It gives sensible defaults and warnings; it **never locks legal wording, never certifies content as legally correct, never cites or enforces a regulator's rule on the firm's behalf**, and never takes on the firm's professional or legal responsibility. Ready-made templates are **starting points** the firm owns once used. Already stated in the docs: AG17-D44, AG-18 ("we work for the firm, not the regulator"), AG-05 ("ExpatReady warns, it does not block"), AG-16 BR-21. **What ExpatReady still fixes** (its own business, not the firm's legal work): its own emails to firms; client and staff security emails; the unsubscribe link / STOP handling (protects ExpatReady's shared sending reputation); the e-sign certificate (a factual record of what ExpatReady's system did); facts about ExpatReady's own systems (data location, deletion dates).
   - **Starter-wording label:** firm staff (never clients) see on every ready-made template in the library and when adopting it: *"Starter wording from ExpatReady. Review it and make it your own before using it with clients."*
   - **Exception kept (decided 11 Oct):** the retainer's **"No active licensed consultant — add one" block stays** (AG-5-49, BR-4) — a departed consultant's licence number is never reused, and a new retainer cannot be created until an active licensed person is on file. *(To confirm when writing AG-05: a lawyer's law-society number counts as the licence for this check, so law firms are not blocked.)*
   - **Standard checklists (decided 11 Oct):** a firm may **hide** any of ExpatReady's standard checklist / "to collect" items in its own copy, firm-wide — recorded (who, when), reversible, never deleted; if ExpatReady later updates a hidden item the firm sees "ExpatReady updated '…', which you've hidden. Review?". Questions that feed scoring or government forms stay fixed (technical reason, not legal). "Locked legal core" / "required by law" wording is removed; pack items are "ExpatReady's standard checklist". *(Knock-ons: SA-1-34, SA1-D9, SA-1B-94 wording, AG17-D8, Corporate LMIA decision log D8.)*
   - **Client language (decided 11 Oct):** the French version is sent automatically when it exists and the client prefers French; if it is missing, staff see "Client X's preferred language is French, but this template has no French version. It will be sent in English. Send anyway?" and proceed. No firm-policy block setting. Legal claims ("legal requirement", "Law-25 / Charter compliance", "non-compliant language") removed. ExpatReady's own subscription terms and billing documents stay bilingual, French first (SA4-D19). *(Knock-ons: AG-05 lines 144, 148, 218, 220, AG-5-14, BR-6, scenario at 592; same check across other modules when writing.)*
   - **Publishing:** ExpatReady's legal-type starters (retainer, agreements, refusal messages, disclaimers) follow the **same publishing flow as every template** — no Owner legal approval, no recorded immigration-expert check. Expert input while writing is optional background quality work, never recorded or claimed.

---

8. **Build for solo practitioners up to 15–20 staff, run by non-technical owners (decided 11 Oct; target market widened 12 Oct).** Release 1 is designed for firms from solo practitioners up to 15–20 staff, run by non-technical owners. **Real features and day-to-day functionality are not reduced** (Release 1 stays full-fledged — see the Release 1 scope principle); what is sized for small firms is **customisation, configurability, access-control granularity and admin depth** — enterprise-grade versions of those are **parked** (listed, with the reason, in "Parked for later"), not built. **Park features, never data foundations** — anything that is cheap now and costly to retrofit because it lives in the data (template version on each send, purpose, firm ownership) stays. Every **material** knock-on is tagged **Keep / Simplify / Park** and approved by the product owner per module before it is written; pure wording fixes are applied directly. Large-organisation needs are handled later. **Built stays** (12 Oct): before parking or simplifying anything, check the code; if it is already built, it stays.
9. **Rule 9 — Intuitive for non-technical owners (decided 12 Oct).** ExpatReady is an intuitive vertical SaaS. Kept features must not add cognitive load:
   - **Zero setup:** a new firm can run from day one on ExpatReady's defaults without opening any settings.
   - **Defaults first, knobs hidden:** every setting has a sensible default; anything beyond the basics sits under a collapsed "Advanced" section, never on the main path.
   - **Plain language:** no permission keys or technical terms on screen; settings describe outcomes (e.g. "Send reminders 3 days before the due date").
   - **One screen, one job:** settings grouped by what the owner wants to achieve, not by module internals.
   - **Test for every screen and setting:** "Could a non-technical solo RCIC set this up alone in 15 minutes without calling support?"
   - **Applies to all kept configuration**, including: the custom-role editor (capabilities view first, Advanced view hidden), Team safeguards, appointment-type options, editable timings, discounts, billing exceptions, the starter-role editor, password rules.
   - **How it is applied:** in every SA and AG update, each kept setting gets a stated default and a placement (main screen or Advanced), and any screen that fails the 15-minute test is flagged.

---

## 1. Prospects (leads) — AG-02 · provisional

| Kind | Template | Priority | Example |
|---|---|---|---|
| Automatic message | Lead acknowledgement | Must | "Thanks Client C, we've received your enquiry. Client A will call you within the hour." |
| Library (used by default follow-up steps) | Follow-up | Must | "Just following up on your enquiry about Express Entry…" |
| Library (used by default steps) | Going-cold nudge | Must | "Are you still planning your move to Canada?" |
| Library | Invitation to book a consultation (booking link) | Must | "Book a 30-minute consultation with Client A: [link]" |
| Library | Call follow-up / next steps | Good to have | "Thanks for your time today. Next steps: …" |
| **Document** | **Proposal** (generated from the consultation: services, fees, scope) + short "Here is your proposal" email | **Must** *(full-read review — the "create proposal from consultation" step exists in Release 1)* | "Our proposal for your Express Entry application: services…, fees $3,500…" |
| Library | Nurture check-in (marketing consent needed) | Good to have | "It's been 3 months — any news on your language test?" |

Dropped (firms create if wanted): program-update newsletter; "sorry we couldn't help" for lost leads.

**Decided (full-read review):** a lead created by a **booking** does **not** get the lead acknowledgement — only the booking confirmation (the staff call task still runs).

## 2. Booking (consultations) — AG-04

| # | Moment | Template | Priority |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Booking made | Booking confirmed (calendar invite attached; reschedule / cancel links) | Must |
| 2 | Before the meeting | Appointment reminder | Must |
| 3 | Time or host changed | Booking updated | Must |
| 4 | Cancelled | Booking cancelled (rebook link) | Must |
| 5 | No-show | Missed appointment (rebook link) | Must |
| 6 | After the meeting | Thank you / next steps after consultation | **Must** *(upgraded — used by the post-consultation recipe)* |
| 7 | Paid session not yet paid | Complete your payment (slot held) | Good to have |
| 8 | Held paid booking expires unpaid | Payment expired — time released (rebook link) | Good to have *(full-read review)* |

**Decided (full-read review):**
- **Pre-consultation agreement** (accepted before paying for a paid session) — **the firm writes all of it** from an ExpatReady starter, including the legal sentences. *(Knock-on: AG-04 currently says the wording comes from the country pack — to change.)*
- One booking template covers video / phone / in-person; the meeting details fill in.

Special design: reminder **timing per appointment type** (firm setting); **each appointment type picks its template**, default ExpatReady's (decided); calendar invite attached automatically. Receipt → Invoices.

## 3. Retainer and e-signature — AG-05

**Documents (legal):**

| # | Document | Priority |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Client retainer agreement | Must |
| 2 | Employer retainer (one per LMIA file) | Must |
| 3 | Short employer retainer pointing to a master agreement (position and fee) | Must |

**Automatic messages:**

| # | Moment | Template | Priority |
|---|---|---|---|
| 4 | Sent for signature | Please sign your agreement (signing link) | Must |
| 5 | Not signed yet | Signing reminder | Must |
| 6 | All signed | Your signed agreement (signed copy + certificate) | Must |
| 7 | Withdrawn by the firm | Agreement withdrawn | Good to have |
| 8 | Expired unsigned (default 14 days) | Agreement expired (a new one will follow if the client still wants to proceed) | Good to have *(full-read review)* |

**Decided (full-read review):** signing reminders also go **automatically** (default: 3 days after sending, then every 4 days, until signed or expired; firm changes the days or switches off; manual "Remind" stays). *(Knock-on: AG-05 today has manual reminders only.)*

Special design *(revised 11 Oct — rule 7)*: the starter retainer carries the usual clauses (scope, fees, refund, dispute, confidentiality, termination) as **ordinary editable text** — the firm edits, merges or deletes any of them; **no ExpatReady "required clause" list, no "required" badges, no refusal to send**. The firm's **Owner may lock the firm's own clauses** so staff cannot change them. A signed retainer never changes (record integrity). ExpatReady's versions follow normal publishing (no expert check). Firms may keep their own variants per type of matter. E-signature *(revised 11 Oct)*: the **certificate** is ExpatReady-owned, fixed and factual (signer, time, method, document fingerprint; no claims of legal effect); the **consent text** is an ExpatReady default that the firm's Owner may replace in firm settings (versioned; new signing requests only); no recorded expert/counsel check. *(Knock-ons: AG-05 AG-5-4, AG-5-5, BR-3, clause panel, locked-clause integrity, persona line "lock the required clauses"; country pack loses the required clause set; SA-08 SA8-D22, SA-8-43, SME note field, US-8; SA-07 approval row.)*

## 4. Invoices (firm → client) — AG-06

**Documents (layout and settings, not free text):**

| # | Document | Priority |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Invoice (PDF) — logo, address, licence number, payment terms, footer; tax, numbering and amounts automatic | Must |
| 2 | Receipt (PDF) | Must |
| 2b | **Offline payment instructions** (bank, e-transfer, cheque details — a firm setting printed on every invoice and the "Invoice sent" email) | Must *(full-read review)* |
| 2c | **Client statement** (billed / paid / outstanding for a period) | Good to have *(full-read review)* |

**Automatic messages:**

| # | Moment | Template | Priority |
|---|---|---|---|
| 3 | Invoice issued (incl. each instalment) | Invoice sent (PDF + pay online link) | Must |
| 4 | Payment received | Payment received (receipt attached) | Must |
| 5 | Before / on due date | Payment reminder | Must |
| 6 | After due date | Payment overdue | Must |
| 7 | A week overdue | Final notice | **Must** *(upgraded 11 Oct — in the default sequence, rule 4)* |
| 8 | Online card payment failed | Payment didn't go through | Good to have |
| 9 | Invoice cancelled by the firm | Invoice cancelled — you don't need to pay it | Good to have *(full-read review)* |
| 10 | Instalment plan created | Your payment schedule (all dates and amounts) | Good to have *(full-read review)* |

Special design: invoices and receipts are **layout settings**; reminder **timing is a firm setting** — **default decided: 3 days before due, on the due date, 3 days after, 7 days after (final notice)** *(full-read review; AG-06 had left it open)*; reminders stop when paid. "Refund issued" comes with the refunds feature (Phase 2).

**Messages to the client:**

| # | Moment | Template | Priority |

| 5 | Any update | Case update (general) | Must |

Questionnaire link → Questionnaire; portal invitation → Client portal; document requests → Documents.

| # | Moment | Template | Priority |

| 2 | Not finished | Questionnaire reminder | Must |

Special design: reminder **timing** is a firm setting; one invitation per family member; re-send uses the invitation. The **questions** are pack content plus firm-added questions (questionnaire builder), not templates — the builder already follows the same "use the default or customise, told when the default improves" idea.

## 7. Documents — AG-09

| # | Moment | Template | Priority |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Documents requested for a case | Document request (case list and due date filled in automatically; portal link) | Must |
| 2 | Generic list, not tied to a case (e.g. to a prospect) | Document checklist (letter / PDF) | Must |
| 3 | Still missing | Upload reminder (only what is missing) | Must |
| 4 | Can't be accepted (blurry, expired, wrong, infected) | Document needs replacing (reason filled in) | Must |
| 5 | Everything received | All documents received | Good to have |
| 6 | Document about to expire (e.g. passport) | Document expiring soon | Good to have |

**Decided (full-read review):** **no** representative submission letter / cover letter template — firms upload their own. Questionnaire gaps: nothing new (existing templates cover them).

Special design: **checklist template = wording + a list**; the list is either **taken from the country pack for a chosen program** (always current) or the **firm's own fixed list** — both supported (decided). The case's document request never carries a second list. Reminder timing is a firm setting; reminders list only what is missing.

## 8. Communication — AG-10

| # | What | Template | Priority |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Every email staff send | Email signature (firm default; each person can adjust) | Must |
| 2 | Firm writes in the portal chat | New message in your portal (email nudge with link) | Must |

Automatic, not a template: the unsubscribe line and sender details on marketing emails (CASL).

Special design — **WhatsApp:** every template that may go by WhatsApp has a **short WhatsApp version**; editing it means **resubmitting to Meta for approval**; until approved, that message goes by email. Replies within the 24-hour window are free text (decided).

**Staff notifications — decided:** fixed wording written by ExpatReady (English / French), **not part of template management**; each staff member chooses which ones they get and how (bell, email, push, digest, quiet hours).

## 9. Automation and reminders — AG-11

| Ready-made recipe | Default | Template it uses | Priority |
|---|---|---|---|
| Speed-to-lead | On | Lead acknowledgement (§1) | Must |
| Case opened → onboarding *(revised 11 Oct)* | On | **Welcome — your case has started** (one email: portal set-up or login + first to-dos) · short "Your new case has started" version for returning clients | Must |
| Document received | On | **Document received** ("Thanks Client C, we got your passport") | Must — new |
| Missing-document chase | On | Upload reminder (§7) | Must |
| Government deadline countdown *(widened from Biometrics countdown, 11 Oct)* | On | **Government deadline reminder** (§5 #16) — "10 days left to send your additional documents — 2 still outstanding" | Must |
| PR card / permit expiry watch | On | **Status expiring soon** ("Your work permit expires on 12 Mar…") | Must — new |
| No-show recovery | Off | Missed appointment (§2) | Must |
| Going-cold follow-up | Off | Going-cold nudge (§1) | Must |
| Post-consultation follow-up | Off | Thank you / next steps after consultation (§2) | Must (upgraded) |

The Minder's deadline reminders go to **staff** → staff notifications (fixed wording).

**Full-read review — Communication and Automation:** nothing new; consent / unsubscribe / disclaimer / AI-notice wording goes to the shared items; the "T-7 client nudge" in firm-designed reminder chains follows that feature (Release 2).

**Decided — automatic messages at a new firm (option C):** *(narrowed 11 Oct by full-read decision 3a: only **self-firing** messages are held; direct responses to someone's action send from day one)* nothing is sent to clients automatically until the Owner confirms a one-time **"Review your automatic messages"** screen; **no copies** are made; messages the firm has not edited follow ExpatReady's improvements automatically; edited ones get the "ExpatReady updated this" note. *(Replaces AG-11's "copies adopted as firm drafts at provisioning"; knock-on to AG-11 and AG-12.)*

**New rule:** a template used by a ready-made recipe is always a **Must**, even if the recipe is off by default.

## 10. Tasks (to-do lists) — AG-13

The to-do lists are staff work. **One client message (full-read review):** **Reminder from your consultant** — the branded frame (greeting, consultant name, reply line) around a one-off reminder a staff member writes for a client (e.g. "book your medical this week") — **Must**.

**Decided — two kinds of to-do, one owner each:**
1. **ExpatReady's standard steps** (e.g. "Advertise the job for 4 weeks", "Submit within 60 days of the invitation") → **country pack** action lines; appear on every case automatically; **the firm may hide them firm-wide** (recorded, reversible — rule 7, standard checklists, revised 11 Oct); not a template.
2. **How the firm works** → **to-do list templates** under the template principle: ExpatReady starter set (normal publishing, no expert sign-off — AG-13's "SME decision per pack" reworded, 11 Oct); firms use, edit, create; each task has a due date relative to an event and a default owner *(knock-on: AG-13 data model has no per-task default owner)*.

| To-do list template | Priority | Example tasks |
|---|---|---|
| New case — file opening | Must | Conflict check · retainer filed · intake call (2 days) · questionnaire sent · document request sent |
| Pre-submission review | Must | Forms checked · documents verified · fees paid · senior review · client approval |
| After decision — closing | Good to have | Decision sent to client · after-approval guidance · file archived |

Portal chat nudge → §8. **"Document accepted"** after staff check a document: shown in the portal only, **no email** *(full-read review)*. Everything firm-branded (logo, colours, sender; custom domain with that extra).

**Decided — client security emails** (password reset, email change confirmation, sign-in code): **ExpatReady-owned, shown in the firm's branding, not editable by firms**; they sit with ExpatReady's own emails.

## 12. Corporate cases (LMIA) — AG-17 (full read)

Founder decisions that keep this lean: **AG17-D49** — nothing automatic keeps the employer informed about workers (staff do it their own way); **AG17-D44** — ExpatReady manages the process, not inboxes.

**Generated documents (pre-filled from the file's job details):**

| # | Document | Priority |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Job advertisement (Job Bank and other boards) | Must |
| 2 | Job offer letter (signed by the employer's authorised signatory) | Must |
| 3 | Recruitment report (built from the candidate log; own template type) | Must |
| 4 | Employment contract | Good to have |

**Messages to the employer:**

| # | Moment | Template | Priority |
|---|---|---|---|
| 5 | Chase what is still owed (one click, editable) | Request outstanding items | Must |
| 6 | Processing fee requested, employer pays | Government fee requested | Must |
| 7 | Approved (incl. partial, e.g. 3 of 4) | LMIA decision: approved | Must |
| 8 | Refused | LMIA decision: refused | Must |
| — | Submitted | uses Application submitted, filled with "Service Canada" | (§5) |
| 9 | Employer questionnaires | Employer questionnaire invitation | Good to have |
| 10 | Officer will contact the employer | Officer contact: how to prepare | Good to have |
| 11 | Ads live | Your job ads are live | Good to have |
| 12 | Change to an approved LMIA decided | Change to your LMIA: outcome | Good to have |
| 13 | Unused positions, approval expiring | LMIA expiring: unused positions | Good to have |
| 14 | Staff update the employer about workers (manual) | Worker update (library) | Good to have |
| 15 | New employer client | Employer welcome | Good to have |

Follow their feature (Release 2): job-applicant rejection letters and other recruitment-engine messages.

Special design: **generated documents** — wording editable; the **pre-filled job facts** (wage, hours, location) fill in from the job details and **staff can change them** *(revised 11 Oct — rule 7, AG17-D17 "nothing is ever locked")*; if a document no longer matches the job details, the existing three-way check (AG17-D33) shows a plain mismatch notice, e.g. "Wage on this ad ($34–36/hr) differs from the job details ($34/hr)". Nothing blocked. **General case templates fill in the government body** (IRCC / Service Canada), never hard-coded. Workers use the individual templates in their own cases.

**A. Firm staff account emails — ExpatReady-owned, firm-branded, not editable by firms (decided):**

| 2 | Password reset | Must |

## 16. ExpatReady → firms (category 1) — SA-2, SA-3, SA-4, SA-10, SA-11 (full read)

All **ExpatReady-owned**; only ExpatReady edits (Content author / Owner; legal sentences Owner-only — SA-7 v2.13 Appendix A). Recipients follow SA-4-53.

| Group | Emails |
|---|---|
| Joining | Owner invitation / set your password · **Sign-up link email** (Must — added) |
| Trial | Reminders 7 / 3 / 1 days · trial ended · trial extended · subscription started early |
| Billing | Invoice and receipt · refund and credit note · price change notice · payment failed (day 0) · reminder (day 7) · final reminder (day 12) · payment hold · card expiring · plan change confirmation · upgrade charge failed · lower plan booked · discount ending · cancellation confirmation |
| Account lifecycle | Deletion warnings (days 30 / 60 / 97) · data export ready · support session summary · live-plan removal notice · storage over limit · **Account security-locked** (Must — added; neutral, no details) · **Owner contact changed** (Good to have — added) |
| Support | Ticket received · reply from support · status changed · satisfaction survey |
| Compliance | Data breach notice to affected people (wording by counsel) |

Also ExpatReady-owned (decided earlier): client security emails (§11) and firm staff account emails (§15).

**Departure notices revised 11 Oct (rule 7):** cancellation confirmation, payment-hold notice, deletion warnings and the data-download screen state only ExpatReady's own facts — e.g. *"Your data will be permanently deleted on 14 Jan 2027. Download it now and keep your own copy for as long as your professional rules require."* No regulator name, number of years or section citation. *(Knock-on: SA-02 SA2-D13, SA-2-38, the US/acceptance line at :529, §13 row 5; SA2-D11's "Why" keeps or drops the citation as rationale only — not in product text.)* ExpatReady's **own** billing-record retention (6 years, SA4-D18) is ExpatReady's tax duty and stays.

**Signed-agreement keep-period revised 11 Oct (rule 7):** the firm sets its own keep-period (default: none); deleting a signed agreement inside it shows a warning the person can pass with a reason; the country pack no longer carries a retention horizon. *(Knock-on: AG-05 §3.10 retention, line ~338/348, related requirements and scenarios.)*

## 17. Shared items (decided 11 Oct)

| # | Item | Decision |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Marketing consent wording (checkbox on firm forms) | ExpatReady default, **fully editable by the firm, no must-keep parts** (the consent law is the firm's duty — rule 7) |
| 2 | Disclaimers (assessment, questionnaire, any document) | Editable starter wording inside the template; never locked, never from the pack, no expert check (rule 7) |
| 3 | AI notice (telling clients AI is used) | Library template, **Must** when the firm has the AI extra; editable like any library template |
| 4 | Unsubscribe link in marketing emails; STOP handling on WhatsApp / SMS; their confirmations | **Automatic, not removable**, fixed ExpatReady wording shown in the firm's branding — a platform function protecting ExpatReady's shared sending reputation (bulk-sender one-click unsubscribe rules), not legal advice |
| 5 | Personal-data package (client asks for their data) | Package format fixed by ExpatReady (it describes ExpatReady's own export); covering email is an editable library template |
