- **AG16-91 — Firm language: English (Canada) only at launch (option A; the product owner, 18 Oct).** Audit #13. Confirms the AG-01 17 Oct fix (`AG-01…:435`, `:476`): the firm profile offers English (Canada) only; `FirmConfig.defaultLocale` stays in the data model (`AG-01…:739`) so French can be switched on later (AG16-88(1)). Why: the firm language is only the starting value; each client has their own preferred language, set at invite and changeable (AG-15-36, `AG-15…:328`, `:408`), and templates are picked in the client's language, so a firm that writes its own French templates can still send French per client. SA-05's "firm whose main language is French" branch (SA-5-70, O14, `SA-05…:548`, `:1191`) is kept and marked "applies once French is switched on"; not deleted. Nothing built. To apply: SA-05 (three notes, done 18 Oct). AG-01 unchanged.
- **AG16-92 — Email-first: the portal is optional (standing rule, option A; the product owner, 18 Oct).** The product owner: "everything about the client could be on their client portal but must be emailed to him too … we can't force clients to log in … a lot of clients may never log in and can produce things on email and through SQR." Audit: `scratchpad/AUDIT-email-first.md`. **Standing rule:** anything the client must **do** or must **know** reaches them on email (or their preferred channel), and every action can be completed **without a portal login** (reply with attachments, or a no-login link). Routine status (document accepted, a shared note, a delivery step moving) stays on the portal and is not emailed one by one (AG16-76). Every requirement that says "in the portal" must pass: "and by email, and doable from the email?" Rules: (1) **Welcome at case opening** (C1; `SA-05:1415`, `:1436`, `:1598`, `:772`): carries, for each first to-do, the same no-login link the stand-alone message would carry (questionnaire secure link per person; document-request link with the list) and the line "or reply to this email with the documents attached"; portal set-up is one optional line. Fixes `AG-07:419`. (2) **Every action link in a client email or WhatsApp is a no-login token link** (document by-code upload, questionnaire `sqrLinkCode`, signing token, hosted pay link, booking token), working whatever the client's portal status and writing the same record as the portal; a signed-in client lands in the portal (C2). Home: new AG-10-85; amends CP-1-69 (`CP-01:415`), closes CP-01 Q12 and Q24; pointers from AG-09, SA-05. (3) **A staff message in portal chat, or a "message ask", is delivered in full** (text and attachments) on the client's preferred channel with the reply token, so a reply files into the same conversation (C3; changes A.8 #1 "New message in your portal" `SA-05:1457` from a nudge to the delivery). One staff send = one client message; no new chain. Content rules unchanged (`AG-15:690`). (4) **WhatsApp media** filed to a case gets the same one-click "Mark '[Document]' request as received? [Yes]" as email (S2; AG-9-62 `AG-09:714`). (5) **A document the firm shares** (firm-produced documents, the assessment) goes with the sharing email as an attachment, or a no-login download link if too large (S6; `AG-15:248`, `SA-05:1539`). (6) **Activation link valid 7 days**, re-sendable (S8; AG-15 BR-4 `:484` and diagram `:129` aligned to `SA-05:1509`). (7) Minor: "Things waiting in your portal" is the manual Nudge only, each item with its no-login link, title "Things we still need from you" (M1; `SA-05:1511`); AG-15-55's "Remind until they confirm" tick removed (M2; AG16-75); "Last update" is set on a successful "Tell the client" send on any channel (M3; `AG-15:454`, `:472`); AG-06 states the hosted pay link needs no login and "portal (AG-12)" → AG-15 (M4); "Document received" also follows a done-on-behalf upload (M5); questionnaire reminders carry a refreshed link, answers kept (M6; `AG-08:535`, AG-8-14). **Not in this entry:** where replies go when no mailbox is connected (S1) and the stall signal (S7) — next questions. S3, S4, S5 stay portal-only under this rule. Engine: rules; Doers: Mailer, Messenger. Not built. To apply: AG-10 (new AG-10-85 card; AG-10-16 chat), AG-15 (§3.4, §3.7, AG-15-18, -54, -55, BR-4, diagram), AG-09 (AG-9-6 link, AG-9-62 WhatsApp), AG-08 (reminder link), AG-06 (M4), AG-07 (`:419`), SA-05 (Welcome A.5 #3, A.7 document request, A.8 #1, A.11 #3, A.13 assessment, Must-have notes), CP-01 (CP-1-69, Q12, Q24 — note only; CP-01 refresh to v0.3 is separate), skill `expatready-requirements` (principle 12), WRITER-BRIEF (standing rules).
- **AG16-93 — Client replies without a connected mailbox: unchanged; connecting is the obvious step (option A; the product owner, 18 Oct).** Audit S1. The product owner: "we don't want to take their work on us but help them work efficiently." Rule: under shared sending, replies go to the firm's own address (`AG-16…:414`, built: `rail-config.resolver.ts`); ExpatReady does not run its own inbound reply address. With a connected mailbox (every plan, AG16-24; IMAP route from day one, AG16-37(3a)) replies are filed and "Mark request as received" works (AG-9-62); without one, the firm reads the reply in its own inbox and uploads by hand. Nothing is missed; filing is the firm's work. **Not built:** an ExpatReady reply-to / forwarding address. To apply: AG-10 design decision 3 (`AG-10…:265`), one line.
- **AG16-94 — Client stall = oldest open request, any channel; flag only (option A; the product owner, 18 Oct).** Audit S7; follows AG16-92; **changes AG16-86(4)** (stall flag kept, but no longer "no login for N days"). Rules: (1) **Stall** = the client's **oldest open request** (document, questionnaire, signature, payment) **7 or more calendar days old**; shown as "Waiting on client: [item] · N days · last heard from N days ago". (2) **"Last heard from"** = the client's last action on **any channel**: email or WhatsApp filed to them, upload, questionnaire progress or submit, signing, payment, portal login. Email counts only when the firm's mailbox is connected (AG16-93); the open request still shows correctly without it. (3) Shown as the flag on the client and case and in the firm-wide list sorted by oldest open request; **no pushed notification** (`client-stalled` AG-15-33 withdrawn; AG16-42, AG16-76); the automatic chases already go to the client (AG16-12). (4) **No settings:** fixed 7 calendar days (Rule 9; matches AG-2 going-cold); `PortalAccessPolicy.stallThresholdDays` and `businessHoursMode` withdrawn; closes AG-15 Q6 and Q10 (and the AG16-6 conflict). (5) "Invited, not activated" is an information label, not a risk factor. Engine: rules; Doer: Minder. Not built. To apply: AG-15 (§3.6 `:301`–`:311`, AG-15-29, -31, -33, BR-12, `PortalActivityEvent` / activity source, `PortalAccessPolicy`, US-5, edge cases, §12 summary lines, Q6, Q10), SA-06 (`agent/portal:nudge` description "send nudges"; no stall configuration).

ExpatReady is finishing its AI set-up." No provider name (Rule 7). To apply: AG-16 (AG-16-35 rule 1, Q20).

AG-16-75 data line points to them. **AG-16 review complete (18 Oct):** all AG-16 open questions closed; AG-16 v1.18 is ready to hand over with its "Decisions that change this epic" table.
- **AG16-101 — Developer API plans move to SA-03 pricing (the product owner, 18 Oct; closes AG-16 Q4).** Which plans include Developer (API and webhooks) (SA-03 #21; code tier `enterprise` marked FOUNDER-CONFIRM, `capability.ts:331-337`) is a pricing decision, listed in SA-03 next to "which plans include AI" (AG16-80). Home: SA-03. Epics affected: AG-16 (Q4 → pointer), SA-03 (pricing open item).
- **AG16-102 — Firm switches AI off: waiting AI proposals stay for review (the product owner, 18 Oct; closes AG-16 Q9).** Same as the ExpatReady pause: proposals already in Needs you stay, labelled "Made by AI before AI was switched off"; staff approve or dismiss each; nothing runs by itself (AG11-D2). Home: AG-11 (Needs you). Epics affected: AG-11, AG-16.
- **AG16-103 — "Allow support to make changes" sits in Help & support (the product owner, 18 Oct; closes AG-16 Q16).** Section 8, §3.15, as placed (AG01-16). Home: AG-16.
- **AG16-104 — Restore from Recently deleted uses the delete permission (the product owner, 18 Oct; closes AG-16 Q18).** Restoring a record uses the same key as deleting that kind of record; the deleter holds it by definition; Owner and Admin hold all delete keys (AG16-21). No new key. "Move to another client or case" stays with the AG-09 review. Home: AG-16 (Recently deleted). Epics affected: AG-16, SA-06 (note on delete keys). **AG-16 review complete (18 Oct).**
- **Group 2 complete (17 Oct):** AG16-56 – AG16-82 decided; standing rules added: AG16-60 (a duplicate beats a miss, staff visibility only), AG16-63 (design checks), AG16-76 (help people, don't replace them; do, don't overdo). Next: one fix pass across all affected documents (AG16-54 – AG16-82), then the full audit.
- **AG16-38 — Lower-volume email items (D12, option A; 16 Oct).** Checked as already covered, so nothing new: "documents received" message (AG-12 line 167 class 1; BR-28, `AG-10…:533`); bulk email to many clients (SA-03 #28, SA3-D26, always marketing); urgent client mail (government action letters by rule, AG16-27; "Waiting on us", AG16-29; Sorter tags on plans with AI, AG16-33; no keyword rule list, since it would be brittle). **Added (G4):** when an **emailed attachment** is filed to a case with an **open document request** of that type, the email shows **"Mark '[Passport]' request as received? [Yes]"**. One click marks the request received and stops the chase (AG16-12). With AI the Sorter suggests the type; without AI staff pick from the open requests. Never automatic (AG16-31). Low volume (1 emailed document in 14 days, `inbox_mix_snapshot.md`), but it stops clients being chased for something they already sent. Not built. To apply: AG-09 (request loop accepts an email-filed document), AG-10 (thread action).
- **AG16-37 — Mailbox go-live essentials (D11, option A; 16 Oct).** (1) **Deactivation warning, one line only:** the deactivate screen lists any firm mailbox the person connected with their own sign-in — "These mailboxes will stop syncing: documents@ (connected by Client G). [Reconnect as me]" — plus a pointer to their open items, which become unassigned under AG16-9. It warns and never blocks. ExpatReady deletes the person's stored mailbox access itself. Dropped: the IRCC representative-email line (the firm handles that with IRCC) and a separate reassignment step. Evidence: 23 bounces to one deleted staff alias in 14 days (`inbox_mix_snapshot.md` §4 item 9). (2) **Microsoft admin approval in setup:** since late 2025 Microsoft requires a tenant admin to approve apps that read mail (MC1163922). The Connect screen detects "admin approval needed" and offers **"Approve for your whole firm"** when the user is the admin, otherwise **"Send this link to whoever manages your Microsoft 365"**. This happens once per firm. ExpatReady completes free Microsoft publisher verification. This replaces AG-10-67's "a similar approval". (3) **Three ways to connect Gmail:** (a) from day one, **"Other provider (IMAP/SMTP)" with an app password** works for Gmail too, with no Google review; (b) **send-only from Gmail** (`gmail.send`, a sensitive scope: Google verification, no CASA) as soon as verification passes; (c) **full "Continue with Google"** (reading and filing; restricted scopes) once Google verification and the **annual CASA assessment** pass. ExpatReady starts verification and CASA **now** (the longest lead-time item; about $540–$6,000 a year per 2024–25 sources; 100-user cap until verified). A vendor with a pre-assessed Google app (Nylas, about $2 per mailbox per month, its name on the consent screen) is the fallback if the launch date can't wait. Sources: `reports/Mailbox connection design for ExpatReady.md`. To apply: AG-10 (AG-10-66, AG-10-67, §3.2a connect flow), AG-16 (Connections, deactivate screen), DEV-TASKS (new top-priority task: Google OAuth verification + CASA; Microsoft publisher verification).
- **AG16-36 — "Make task" and "Remind me if no reply" (D10, option A; 16 Oct).** Evidence: about 65% of the human email threads in 14 days asked for an action (`inbox_mix_snapshot.md` §3). (1) **"Make task"** on any email opens a task with the title pre-filled from the subject, assigned to the email's owner (AG16-29), due in 2 days and linked to the email; one click to save. With AI on, the Sorter suggests a clearer title (AG16-33). (2) **"Remind me if no reply"** on sending: 3 days (default), 7 days or a date. If no reply arrives by then, the **home screen** shows "No reply from [client] · N days". It clears itself on reply. **Not added:** automatic tasks from every email, email-triggered task templates, or a rules engine. Government letters already create their own tasks (AG16-27). Pattern: Front, HubSpot, Gmail nudges, Clio. Not built. To apply: AG-10 (thread actions, composer), AG-13 (task linked to an email), AG-11 (home screen, AG16-40).
- **AG16-35 — "Not on this case" label (D9, practical version; 16 Oct).** Evidence: in 14 days, 2 threads where a relative wrote for a client, and one where staff had to refuse information (`inbox_mix_snapshot.md` §3). Uses the people already linked to the case (`CaseApplicant` principal / spouse / dependant, `AG-07…:823`; employer contacts, AG-7-72). (1) A small label **"⚠ Not on this case"** shows on any email or draft where the sender or a recipient is not linked to the case. It is visible before replying, with no pop-up and no extra click. (2) **"Add to case"** adds them as a related contact with a relationship (brother, HR, agent), after which the label disappears for them. (3) Filing still works, so the history stays complete. **Not added:** a separate authorised-contacts list, consent records, a "Send anyway" dialog, or extra logging. The firm keeps the client's written consent in its own retainer or documents (Rule 7: remind, never enforce). Pattern: Clio, MyCase, Docketwise, Karbon (related contacts with a role, no gate); heavier proxy-consent models exist only where the law requires them (health care). Not built. To apply: AG-10 (thread header, composer), AG-07 (related contact with relationship). D9 unauthorised-sender warning; D10 email → task, remind if no reply; D11 go-live essentials (staff-departure warnings, Microsoft admin consent in onboarding, start Google CASA now); D12 lower-volume items (attachment fulfils request, "documents received" message, urgent label, bulk send — check AG-10); D13 Translate and Summarise (the product owner's release call). Completeness fixes: ~~F1 one place to start~~ (AG16-40); ~~F2 portal PDFs~~ (AG16-41); ~~F3 telling the client~~ (AG16-42); ~~F4 letter-library upkeep~~ (AG16-43); ~~F5 monthly counts~~ (skipped, the product owner 17 Oct: not day-to-day; helps ExpatReady at renewal, not firm operations; no Release 2 placeholder, can be added later from existing logs). Earlier queue still open: office timezone and office-scoped escalation, closed-days suggestions, Automatic messages grouping, WhatsApp coexistence, client quiet hours, unassigned overdue Needs-you items.

## AG-07 review — decisions (the product owner, from 18 Oct)

Numbered per epic (AG07-Dn); each states Home and Epics affected. Audit: `scratchpad/AUDIT-AG07.md`. Applied in one pass at the end of the AG-07 review.

- **AG07-D1 — One document status list, owned by AG-09 (option A; the product owner, 18 Oct).** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-26). Epics affected: AG-07 (AG-7-15 shows AG-09's status, stores none), AG-09, AG-15 (client view labels), SA-05 (template wording already matches). Conflict: AG-7-15 (`AG-07…:698`, 7 values) vs AG-9-26 (`AG-09…:611`, 10 values). Rule: staff see **5 statuses**: **Needed → Received → Accepted / Needs replacing**, plus **Not needed**. "Superseded" and "Quarantined" (virus check) are states of a file, kept on the file record, not request statuses; "Under review" = Received not yet checked. Why: one home (AG16-11); small firms (Rule 9); same words as the client emails ("Document received", "Document needs replacing", `SA-05…:1448-1449`). Status: approved, not yet applied.
- **AG07-D2 — One rules pin per filing (option A; the product owner, 18 Oct).** Home: AG-07 (case and filing model). Epics affected: AG-07 (AG7-D25, AG7-D27, §13 Q24), AG-17 (added 18 Oct), AG-09 ("Request a document" per filing), SA-01 / SA-01B (pin wording), AG-08 (questionnaire per filing). Evidence: `scratchpad` code check — pin has a placeholder `case_id` "no Case table yet" (`client-spine.prisma:466-467`); keys kept for "FUTURE composite FKs" (`:506-508`); COMPLETE forms carry no `case_id` by design (`apps/api/infra/04-spine-constraints.sql:263-264`, `tools/lib/spine-constraint-declarations.ts:603-616`, test `pack-party-owner.e2e.test.ts:383`); SA-01 `:136`, `:443` and SA-01B `:154` already assume a pin per case/filing. Rule: each **filing** (one application with its own program inside a case, AG-7-45) gets its own rules pin (new "case delivery" purpose keyed by the filing); forms and "Request a document" reach the case through that pin; `FormInstance` gets no direct `caseId` (option B rejected: reverses the built constraint and does not fix per-case additions). Sub-rules: (1) everyone on a filing uses that filing's one rules version; how dependants' forms attach is the backend lead's call (with AG07-D5); (2) adopting newer rules carries the firm's "Request a document" additions to the new pin (today they stay on the old pin, `pack-tenant-extension.service.ts:312-315`; AG16-60); (3) a filing added later in the same case starts on the case's current rules version, not the newest (AG-7-56); staff may adopt newer. Backend lead decides: exact key, NULL handling (NULLS NOT DISTINCT or a chain key), the adoption FK/trigger. Status: approved, not yet applied.
- **AG07-D3 — Retainer from a lead, a client or a case; representative pre-filled, never forced (option A; the product owner, 18 Oct).** Home: AG-05 (retainer). Epics affected: AG-05 (§3.6 initiation `AG-05…:352` only from the lead today), AG-02 (conversion carries the retainer and its representative), AG-07 (case lead representative pre-filled at creation; "Prepare retainer" on the case), AG-01 (AG01-5 wording). The product owner: "a case need not start from the prospect module … could start from client directly … cover every way to send a retainer … in conjunction with e-sign"; "ideally the same person, but we will not block the firm". Rules: (1) **"Prepare retainer" on a lead, a client and a case**, one engine and one e-sign flow; from a lead it moves to the client and case at conversion; from a client or case it attaches directly. (2) Every outcome recorded for all three starting points: e-signed, signed on paper (upload, "signed offline", AG-5-39), re-issued as a new version (AG-5-35), expired, voided with a reason. (3) **Representative** (the licensed person who signs) pre-filled: the case's lead representative if started from a case; else the firm's default lead representative (AG01-5); else the preparer if licensed; else staff pick. (4) **Same person is the goal, never forced:** a different person shows a warning ("Client A is this case's lead representative. Client G will sign this retainer."); a case created from a lead gets its lead representative pre-filled from the retainer, editable. (5) Pre-filled from what the system holds (client personal details, billing details, scope) via AG-05 merge fields (§3.2); a missing value shows as "missing" before sending. (6) Signatures per AG01-20 / AG01-23. **Kept:** AG01-6 block when the firm has no active licensed person at all (the one exception to warn-never-block). Status: approved, not yet applied.
- **AG07-D4 — Case permissions: use the built keys, fold everyday work into `case:edit` (option A; the product owner, 18 Oct, on Claude's high-confidence recommendation).** Home: SA-06 (catalogue); AG-07 §10 lists them. Epics affected: AG-07, SA-06, AG-01 (seeding). Evidence: built `agent/pack:adopt`, `agent/form:create`, `agent/pack:extend` (`packages/contracts/src/schemas/permission-catalog-rows.pack-bridge.ts:71, :84, :109`); AG-07 §10 (`AG-07…:1282-1309`) invents parallel keys and needs `case:milestone-record` for a hand-recorded government step but only `case:edit` from a letter. Rule: use the three built keys; **`case:edit`** covers documents, deficiencies, sending for internal review and government steps (by hand or from a letter) — `case:document-manage`, `case:form-prepare` (→ `form:create`), `case:deficiency-manage`, `case:review-submit`, `case:milestone-record` withdrawn; kept separate: `case:submit`, `case:record-decision`, `case:waive-deficiency`, `case:form-approve`, `case:review-approve`, `case:reopen`, `case:assign`, `case:export`. No visible change for firms (role editor capabilities unchanged). Status: approved, not yet applied.

Staff pick a document type from the pack or "Other" with a typed name; pick the person it is for (default the principal); "Ask the client now" is a class-2 box ticked by default (firm may set it unticked, SA5-X25); the item can be deleted until it is sent or a file arrives, then only "Not needed". (k) **Client-file record** created only when a person becomes a lead or client. AG-02. (l) **Import exact email match** defaults to "Use existing", shown in the dry run; never merged unseen. AG-18. (m) **"Keep private from the principal":** one tick per adult on the case's applicant list. AG-07, AG-08, AG-15. (n) **Privacy requests for children:** the principal acts for minors through the firm; adults act on their own record. SA-11. (o) **"Needed again"** after "Not needed", with a reason, audited. AG-09. (p) **Document return reasons:** the firm keeps its own short list, defaults supplied. SA-01B, AG-09. (q) **A hand-recorded step** duplicating an open step follows AG07-D10. AG-07. (r) **"Welcome not sent"** shows on the case header only when sending failed; a deliberate untick shows only in the case activity. AG-07. (s) **A filing added later** when filings are on different rules versions uses the principal's filing's version. AG-07. (t) **UCI at submission:** a snapshot of each applicant's UCI. AG-07. (u) `agent/case:view-dashboard` folds into `agent/case:view.all`. AG-07, SA-06. (v) **"From <name>" tag** is read from the handover record; no new case field. AG-07, AG-01. (w) **Usual timelines** in the pack count calendar days; the immigration expert confirms. SA-01B, AG-07.
- **AG07-D21 — AG-07 rewritten in the new format as one version (option A; the product owner, 18 Oct).** Home: X-1 process. v2.13 is completed with every decision, then frozen in `_archive/` marked "Replaced by v3.0 — do not build from this"; v3.0 is the only build source; requirement IDs unchanged; v3.0 carries a "Where did it go?" table (every v2.13 ID → its new place); no-loss checks: script (IDs, fields, values, keys, events, numbers), a second reviewer for meaning, an AI test; anything deliberately dropped is listed in an appendix. Also approved: DEV-TASKS gets a new section for the 18 Oct decisions that change code (written as section H, tasks H1–H9, because section G already existed).
- **AG07-D22 — AG-07 v3.0 open questions answered (the product owner, 18 Oct: "ok go ahead" to the recommendations, all items).** Home: AG-07 v3.0 (cards named per item; D9 of v3.0 lists the question text). Epics affected: AG-07; plus AG-15 (Q42, Q43, Q49, Q79), AG-08 (Q43, Q79), AG-02 (Q44, Q65), AG-13 (Q84 if it uses buckets), DEV-TASKS (Q45/Q46, Q65). (Q41) **Answer sheet approval:** the consultant approves the answer-sheet values (`agent/case:form-approve`) before staff copy them into the government form; the final completed form is then attached to the filing as a document. (Q42) **One application number per filing** (each filing is its own application, AG07-D8(a)). (Q43) **The case's principal is the case's client** (Client B): the person who sees family answers, the subject of "Keep private from the principal", the Welcome recipient, and "the principal's filing" for AG07-D20(s). (Q44) **Returning client with a new program:** a new case by default; adding a filing to an open case is also allowed. (Q45, Q46) **`Filing.status` stored values: Open, Submitted, Decided, Withdrawn;** withdrawing one filing sets its status to Withdrawn; adding, editing or removing a filing needs `agent/case:edit`. (Q47) **Filings:** the pack's program proposes them and the case officer confirms in the wizard; for a sequential route the later filing is created when the earlier level is done. (Q48) **Corporate client without a primary contact:** saved, with a prompt to add one (warn, never block). (Q49) **Employer portal seats: Release 1** (roster and seats). (Q50) `CaseEvent` is a leftover name; `CaseMilestone` is the only record (AG16-73). (Q52) **Several filings submitted together:** one action creates one `Submission` per filing, with one confirmation and one "Application submitted" box for the case. (Q53) **A person who can submit but cannot pass a CRITICAL warning:** the submission waits; the warning shows in Needs you of the holders of `agent/case:waive-deficiency` (AG-7-28). (Q57) **One review per case**; `filingId` set only when one filing is reviewed alone. (Q59, Q77) **Several filings:** readiness state and score are per filing; case-level screens show the lowest readiness, the lowest score and the earliest stage. (Q60) **Linked case the user cannot open:** the badge shows the case number only. (Q61) **"Create work permit case" when the worker has a case:** offer the existing case and link it. (Q62) **Evidence bundle with a missing document:** export anyway and list what is missing. (Q63) **Correcting a decision** re-evaluates the open after-decision items only. (Q64) **Reopen** clears `closedAt`, `closedReason`, `closeOutcome` and `withdrawnAt`; the audit entry keeps the old values. (Q65) **Client number (`clientCode`) is assigned at conversion to client;** a lead has none. (Q73) **Prospect item with no pack line** stays on the case as an extra line. (Q74) **Handover across authorities:** one task naming every authority. (Q78) **Exactly one `PRINCIPAL` per filing;** no other hard limits; removing an applicant after documents or forms exist warns, never blocks. (Q79) **A child's answers** show on both the staff case screen and the client portal, unless "Keep private from the principal" is ticked. (Q83) Only standard government steps with no deadline may be hidden (W-15). (Q84) **Buckets:** In process, Review, Submitted, After decision, Closed. (Q85) **On hold** is set by a holder of `agent/case:edit`. (Q86) **A government step placed before submission** is recorded by a person; nothing starts it automatically. (Q88) English first, screens translation-ready (AG16-88). Questions Q51, Q54–Q56, Q58, Q66–Q72, Q75, Q76, Q80–Q82, Q87, Q89–Q94 stay open for the backend lead or the immigration expert.
- **AG07-D23 — A child's answers and employer seats (the product owner, 18 Oct: "1. it should show to both parents 2. A").** Home: AG-07 (AG-7-79, AG-7-107) for (1); AG-15 and SA-03 for (2). Epics affected: AG-07, AG-08, AG-15, SA-03, DEV-TASKS (H9), 99-Glossary. (1) **A minor's answers are shown to both parents on the case** — the case's client and the other parent when that parent is on the case (e.g. Client B and Client A see Client D's answers) — on the staff case screen and in each parent's portal. There is no "Keep private from the principal" tick for a minor; the tick stays one per adult (AG07-D20(m)). A parent is a person linked to the child as a parent in the family links (built `PartyRelationship.relationshipType`, free text today: `client-spine.prisma:125-133`). This replaces the AG07-D22 Q79 words "unless 'Keep private from the principal' is ticked" for minors. (2) **Employer portal seats are Release 1 and stay a separate plan extra** the owner switches on (SA3-D20); the words "when built" are dropped. Corrects the glossary Release 2 list.
- **AG07-D24 — A person who can submit but cannot pass a CRITICAL warning; Case Officer holds the pass by default (the product owner, 18 Oct: "3A + default on"; refines AG07-D22 Q53; amends SA6-D2 for one key).** Home: AG-07 (AG-7-28, AG-7-31, BR-10) for (a); SA-06 for (b). Epics affected: AG-07, SA-06, AG-17, DEV-TASKS, 99-Glossary. (a) **Nothing is held as pending.** When the person recording the submission does not hold `agent/case:waive-deficiency` and a CRITICAL warning is open, **Record submission** is unavailable to them and the screen names the people who can pass it; the warning shows in those people's Needs you as waiting for a submission. Once it is passed, the person clicks **Record submission** again; they enter the real date the application was sent, which may be earlier than the recording. No pending-submission record; nothing is recorded or sent to the client automatically. The product owner's principle: the firm's role settings decide who may do what; ExpatReady does not restrict beyond them (AG17-D9). (b) **The starter Case Officer role holds `agent/case:waive-deficiency` by default**, as do Owner and Case Manager. `agent/case:form-approve`, `agent/case:review-approve` and `agent/retainer:countersign` stay Owner and Case Manager by default (SA6-D2 otherwise unchanged). The firm may remove or grant any of them; every pass is audited with actor, case and reason.
- **AG07-D25 — Admin also holds the pass of a CRITICAL warning by default (option A; the product owner, 18 Oct; follows AG07-D24(b)).** Home: SA-06. Epics affected: SA-06, AG-01, AG-07, AG-17, DEV-TASKS. Reason: SA6-D3 lets an Admin give only roles whose every key the Admin holds (AC6.1a); once the Case Officer role holds `agent/case:waive-deficiency`, an Admin without it could no longer give the Case Officer role to a new hire. Rule: the starter **Admin** role holds `agent/case:waive-deficiency` by default, with Owner, Case Manager and Case Officer. `agent/case:form-approve`, `agent/case:review-approve` and `agent/retainer:countersign` stay Owner and Case Manager by default. SA6-D3 and AC6.1a are unchanged.
- **AG07-D26 — AG-07 v3.0 open questions, second set (the product owner, 18 Oct: "A- ok, B- … give option to restrict through settings", "C- … review and its cycle could be at filing level", "D- Ok", "E1 … we should have a target date … warn and reminder … but shouldn't be mandatory", "go with your suggestions for 7, 11, 14, 15, 16", 17/18 answers, "go ahead").** Home: AG-07 v3.0 unless stated. Epics affected: AG-07, AG-10, AG-11, AG-13, AG-14, SA-01B, SA-06 (check only), DEV-TASKS, 99-Glossary. (1) **Application numbers sit on each filing** (Q102, Q103). A letter carrying an application number matches its filing; with no number, staff pick the filing; the AG-10 matcher returns the filing. Supersedes the application-number part of AG16-73(2); provincial file numbers unchanged. AG-07, AG-10. (2) **An unapproved or unsigned form at submission is an ordinary CRITICAL warning** (Q115), passed by any holder of `agent/case:waive-deficiency` (Case Officer by default, AG07-D24/D25). One key for all CRITICAL warnings; the firm restricts by removing it from a role in Settings → Roles (no separate key, Rule 9). For an answer-sheet form, staff tick **"Signed by client"** when attaching the final form; e-signed forms read the envelope. AG-07, SA-06 (no change). (3) **Review is per filing** (Q116, Q95; replaces AG07-D22 Q57). Each filing has its own review status and rounds; one "Send for review" may cover several filings (all ready filings ticked by default); a later filing may have its own review. A review moves to `UNDER_REVIEW` when any holder of `agent/case:review-approve` first opens it, and shows who. A change after approval (a form regenerated or overridden, a document on that filing's checklist replaced or its status changed, an answer of a person on that filing changed; not notes, tasks or messages) shows **"Changed since review: <what>, <who>, <date>"** on the case; it does not undo the approval. At submission it asks one confirmation, like a missing review approval (BR-19), with no permission; no notification (AG16-42). AG-07. (4) **One-click "Record step" for a letter with no deadline** (e.g. AOR): the letter review offers "Record step: <step>, <date>"; a person clicks (AG16-31). AG-07, AG-10. (5) **Target filing date** (renames the target date, `targetClosureDate`; AG7-D15 and AG07-D20(i) replaced): the firm's own aim for submitting a filing; optional, empty by default, set by a holder of `agent/case:edit`; **no pre-fill from processing times** — SA-1B-101 and the pack field "usual processing time (days)" are withdrawn. Amber in the last 20 % of the window from case creation to the date (AG07-D16 Q23 kept); the case officer is reminded 2 days before and on the day (Minder); after the date the case is red and the officer's Needs you shows "Past target filing date"; no escalation (AG16-22 unchanged); everything stops once the filing is submitted. Reverses AG07-D6 for this date only. After submission the case shows "Submitted <date> · N days waiting". AG-07, AG-11, AG-13, AG-14, SA-01B. (6) **Recommendations approved:** (Q99) a SUGGESTED deadline is not a recorded government step until Confirm; "Not a deadline" dismisses it (AG16-59(6)); (Q101) a government step gets **"Step done"** with a date, for holders of `agent/case:edit`, stopping its reminders; AG-07 owns the control (AG-11-125); (Q108) reminders follow a changed date, and "Not a deadline" cancels them; (Q112) **"Make principal"** on another applicant swaps the role; a filing added by mistake is removed with **"Remove filing"** (`agent/case:edit`), not Withdraw; (Q111) when an earlier level is approved ExpatReady suggests **"Start next stage"** in Needs you, plus a manual button; the next filing reads the same person's answers, nothing copied (X-2; SA-01B redesign #10); (Q98) `POST_SUBMISSION` → `SUBMITTED`, `DECISION` → `AFTER_DECISION`; built `StageKind` lacks the after-decision and closed kinds (`country-pack-rules.prisma:14-25`) — DEV-TASKS; (Q100) the program shown for a case is derived from the principal's filing, not stored; filtering by program searches every filing; (Q114) `agent/case:submit` covers saving the numbers offered at submission; an existing UCI is never overwritten; (Q97) reopen also clears `withdrawReason`, and reopening a withdrawn case offers to reopen its withdrawn filings; (Q85) filing chips "<person> · <filing type> · <status>", principal first, decided or withdrawn greyed and last, "+N" on the board only, click opens the filing; (Q56) a missing person detail is a questionnaire gap (`sqr`), an unmet program rule an `eligibility` gap, the detail names what is wrong; (Q58) a shared-document offer is accepted by any holder of `agent/case:edit` on the receiving case; it shows in Needs you only while that filing is not submitted. (7) **Who fills which form** (Q94): the pack states per form who completes it, how many copies (per application / per person / per couple), which people (principal, spouse, dependants, minimum age), the condition, and the country variant. A family member who is not accompanying completes forms only when listed in the application (e.g. Express Entry non-accompanying spouse). A child's forms are signed by a parent on the application or the parent with custody; staff pick that parent per child. People outside the case (friend, family member, agent) fill using the client's own link — no third-party flow. SA-01B, AG-07. (8) **Document sub-requirements** (Q87): the document's language is set at upload (staff, or AI suggests when on); a language other than English or French adds "Translation + translator's affidavit" to the checklist automatically. A document older than the program allows (e.g. police certificate issued within 6 months) shows a warning only, never a block (Rule 7). SA-01B gets a new section **"Country-specific requirements"** shared by every program (e.g. how a police certificate is obtained per country). SA-01B, AG-07, AG-09. Also to apply: the AG-7-31 test in which Client C submits is corrected (the default Case Officer lacks `agent/case:submit`); Q51 example dates fixed by the editor.
- **X-3 — Expiry watch (the product owner, 18 Oct: "status is something very critical … need to be put somewhere as placeholder"; "1. Yes - but there has to be way not to spam and stop"; "A is fine").** Home: Agent `00-Overview` → "Product rules every epic follows" (the rule); AG-11 Minder (how it runs). Epics affected: AG-11, AG-13, AG-08, SA-01B, AG-07, AG-10, 99-Glossary. (1) **Staff reminders** before a person's status expires (work permit, study permit, visitor record, PR card; full list in the AG-11 review): **30, 15, 7 and 2 days before**; when the case is closed, one earlier reminder **90 days before** is added, to the lead representative of the last case (unassigned task if that person has left, AG-11-47). Government deadlines keep 2 days before and on the day (AG-11 §3.4). (2) **They keep running after the case closes** (AG17-D63 confirmed). (3) **They stop by themselves** when a new expiry is recorded for the same status, or a renewal or extension for that person is submitted; staff may click **"Stop watching"** with a reason (client left Canada, now with another firm, became PR or citizen, other), audited and reversible. Each reminder fires once; Snooze applies (AG-13). Staff reminders cannot be switched off by the firm (AG-11-48). (4) **Client reminders** ("PR card / status expiry watch", Automatic messages, AG-11 :520): 30 and 7 days before; **on by default for an open case; off by default for a closed case**, which, when switched on, passes the consent check as a marketing message (AG-11-37); every expiry email carries an unsubscribe link and an unsubscribe stops them. (5) **Placeholders, to be specified in the AG-11 review:** AG-08 (which status dates each person has and their keys; with AG-07 D9 Q109), SA-01B (document expiry yes/no and validity), AG-07 (what the case shows, including restoration — a deadline 90 days after status is lost, suggested and confirmed by staff — and maintained status after an extension is submitted). Status: approved, placeholders to apply.
- **AG07-D27 — AG-07 v3.0 questions Q118–Q124 and form signing (the product owner, 18 Oct: "c", "c", "A", "lets go with you", "B is fine … where are we maintaining which form need signing?", "ok A", "A", calendar days "because expiry of status happens on calendar days", "go ahead").** Home: AG-07 v3.0 unless stated. Epics affected: AG-07, AG-11, SA-01B, AG-16, AG-10, AG-15, 99-Glossary, DEV-TASKS. (1) **Target filing date reminders with no case officer** (Q118): the case's lead representative gets them; the case also shows in the Unassigned queue to holders of `agent/case:assign` (AG07-D19). AG-07, AG-11 (AG-11-126). (2) **Filings submitted on different days** (Q119): each filing chip shows its own submission date; when the dates differ the case header shows "N filings submitted · <first> – <last>" and no single waiting figure; when they are the same, "Submitted <date> · N days waiting". (3) **"Step done" can be undone** (Q120) by a holder of `agent/case:edit`, audited; the step opens again and only its reminders still to come restart; passed reminders do not fire late. AG-07, AG-11 (AG-11-125). (4) **A reopened withdrawn filing becomes `OPEN`** (Q121); its old application number stays in the history and a new one is entered at the next submission; checklist, forms and documents are kept. (5) **Readiness: a required form is satisfied when approved and, if it needs a signature, signed** (Q122; AG-7-24, AG-7-27, BR-30 aligned). **Which forms need signing:** two pack fields on each form line in SA-1B-102 — **who signs** (no signature · the person the form is about · a parent for a minor · the principal for the family · the person and the representative) and **how** (electronic · ink) — set by the immigration expert; a form line with the field not yet filled is treated as **needing a signature** (AG16-60). Staff may switch "Signature needed" on or off for one form on one case, with a short reason, audited (`agent/case:edit`; e.g. "Signed in portal"). No firm-wide setting. "Signed" = the e-signature envelope completed, or the "Signed by client" tick (AG07-D26(2)). A child's form is signed by the parent staff picked (AG07-D26(7)). SA-01B, AG-07. (6) **A filing's principal cannot be removed directly** (Q123): the Remove control is not shown for the principal; the screen says "Make someone else principal first"; "Make principal" then Remove (AG07-D22 Q78 kept). (7) **The 20 % amber window counts calendar days** (Q124). Rule for the whole product: **calendar dates** — government dates and the target filing date — count calendar days, and a reminder that falls on a closed day moves to the open day before (AG16-7); **working-time clocks** — Needs you overdue, lead response time, the government-deadline approval lead time, bookable slots — count the firm's open hours (AG16-6, amended: case target dates removed). Client E: window Thu 5 Nov → Fri 20 Nov 2026 = 15 days, last 20 % = 3 days, amber from Tue 17 Nov. BR-12, AG-7-91, AG-11 (:1081), AG-16, AG-10, AG-15 aligned.
- **AG01-29 — AG-01 gap check answers (the product owner, 18 Oct: "ok", "B, and no before submission", "a", "a", "ok", "ok B", "ok", "k", "go").** Source: silent-gap audit of AG-01 v1.23 with an independent verification against code and decisions. Home: AG-01 unless stated. Epics affected: AG-01, AG-02, AG-04, AG-07, AG-11, AG-13, AG-12, SA-06, DEV-TASKS. (1) **Sign-out after 28 days unused stays** (built, platform setting 1–365 days; staff and clients). AG1-D20 and the 8 Oct session decision read: "no short idle timeout and no firm session setting; after 28 days unused, sign in again." (2) **"Update representative with the authority" task** is created whenever the lead representative of a **submitted** case changes — in a handover or later on the case screen — assigned to the new lead representative; never before submission (the new representative signs the IMM 5476 filed with the application). Home AG-07 (AG-7-44). (3) **Suspended staff get no new work:** treated like a deactivated person for new work only — tasks an automation would give them are created unassigned, no bookable times, no notifications; their open work and seat stay; a "Suspended" tag shows beside their name where assigned; nothing moves automatically. AG-01, AG-04, AG-11, AG-13. (4) **A licence record removed from someone who still leads cases:** nothing moves. Their cases keep them and show the existing "Lead representative is not licensed" warning (one-click **Submit anyway**, AG07-D20(b)); the default lead representative setting shows "<name> is no longer licensed — choose another" and new cases get no default until one is picked; the Owner gets one Needs you item "<name> is no longer licensed — N cases led by them" linking to the list. AG-01, AG-07. (5) **Appointments in a handover move only if AG-04's availability check says the new person is free** (ExpatReady bookings plus the calendars they chose to check, AG-4-27, AG16-18); else "Needs a new host". AG1-D14's "not doing" line reads: "no separate clash screen in the handover; 'free' is AG-04's availability check". AG-01, AG-04. (6) **The handover moves all of the leaver's clients (file owner) to the main choice**, in the same click; preview "N clients (M with open cases) → <name>"; "Choose per type" and "Skip for now" unchanged. Safety line: an item for a client whose file owner is deactivated is created with no owner and shows in "Needs an owner", never sent to the departed person (AG-1-15; AG16-25(6), AG16-29(1), AG16-67(2)). AG-01, AG-02, AG-10. (7) **Firm details — who edits:** legal name and trading name (built `legalName`, `operatingName`) — ExpatReady support (Super Admin), like the portal address; business address with tax number, and billing email — Owner only (built `agent/firm-profile:manage`, `firm-record.ts:667-670`); public phone, public email, website (new fields, needed by AG-12-109), logo, colours, sender name, footer, email signature, language, time zone, default currency, offices — Owner and Admin (`agent/settings:edit`, not yet in code). AG-01 :791 corrected: the built `Firm` has `name` and `legalName?` (`tenancy-core.prisma:22, :80`), and the profile contract carries `operatingName` (`firm-record.ts:461`); there is no `tradingName`, firm `email`, `phone` or `website` field. *(Corrected same day: `legalName?` exists.)* AG-01, AG-16, SA-06, DEV-TASKS. (8) **One licence record per person** (`RegulatorCredential`, unique `heldByUserId`); can be lifted later without moving data. Also: the built role-assignment bug (an Admin can remove the Owner role, including from the last active Owner; a role-assign holder can change their own roles) is recorded in DEV-TASKS as a priority fix; AG-01's requirements already forbid it.
- **AG16-105 — AG-16 gap check answers (the product owner, 18 Oct: "ok", "ok", "go").** Home: AG-16. Epics affected: AG-16, AG-06, AG-04, SA-06. (1) **Recently deleted shows each person what they could restore:** items whose delete key they hold (AG16-104) from records they are allowed to see (SA6-D1 visibility); Owner and Admin see everything. No new key or setting. (2) **Notification groups (AG16-14 "about 8"), fixed list:** 1 Work handed to me (assigned case, task or lead incl. new lead and handover; review requested from me; my review sent back; a client booked a consultation with me); 2 Needs you; 3 My deadlines and reminders (government deadlines on my cases, target filing date AG-11-126, expiry watch X-3, my overdue tasks, reminders I set); 4 Client messages; 5 Mentions; 6 Team watch (a deadline nobody is watching, escalation alerts, work left unassigned after someone left) — shown only to holders of `agent/case:assign`; 7 Payments (payment failed and invoice overdue email on; payment received, invoice sent, refund, instalment due in-app only) — shown only to billing holders; 8 Firm and connections (connection problem, token expiring, payment account paused) — Owner and Admin. Email on by default for groups 1–6 and 8. Security emails always on, no switch. A person sees only groups that can reach them. AG-06 (AG-6-36 defaults), AG-04 (host notified of a booking). *(Same day: "token expiring" follows AG-16-16 — shown on the connection card, email only when the connection has failed.)*
- **AG01-30 — Deadlines on a suspended person's cases; dunning escalation (the product owner, 18 Oct: "A, B, apply fixes").** Source: independent check of the AG01-29 / AG16-105 edits. (1) **A standard deadline whose reminder would go to a suspended person** is treated as "a deadline nobody is watching": the people who can reassign cases (`agent/case:assign`) are alerted, as for a deactivated file owner (AG-11-47); the suspended person gets nothing; no work moves (follows AG01-29(3)). Home AG-11; epics affected AG-11, AG-13, AG-01. (2) **Dunning escalation** is in the Payments notification group, **in-app only** (the "invoice overdue" email for the same invoice has already gone; AG16-42). Home AG-16 (AG16-105(2)); epics affected AG-16, AG-06 (closes AG-06 open question 23).
- **AG16-106 — `agent/billing-config:currency` withdrawn (the product owner, 18 Oct: "ok").** Not built; no requirement names an "invoice currency rule". An invoice's currency comes from the billing party, else the firm default (AG-6-46); the firm default for new cases is `agent/settings:edit`; one currency per case (AG-07, R18). "Change billing settings" keeps `agent/billing-config:tax`, `:numbering`, `:terms`. Home: SA-06. Epics affected: SA-06, AG-06, AG-16.

## AG-02 review — decisions (the product owner, from 19 Oct)

Numbered per epic (AG02-Dn); each states Home and Epics affected. Spec: `FINAL-Agent-FR/AG-02-prospect-lead-sales-crm_v2.0_2026-10-20.md` (final; the draft is in `FINAL-Agent-FR/_archive/`). All AG-02 decisions of 19–20 Oct are recorded below (the product owner, 20 Oct: "write the decisions"). Pending items for other epics carry IDs AG02-P1 onward and sit in each epic's open-questions section. The 77 small defaults settled by the 20 Oct check are written on the D8 rows of the AG-02 draft, each marked "Decided 20 Oct — AG02-D12".

- **AG02-D0 — Six founding rulings for AG-02 v2.0 (the product owner, 19 Oct; logged 20 Oct when v2.0 was approved).** Home: AG-02. Epics affected: AG-03, AG-07, AG-17. (S1) A **lead** is neutral: a person who showed interest; it has no Individual/Corporate type. (S2) **Engage** is one click by staff; it turns the lead into a **prospect**; Individual or Corporate is chosen then and is editable until the prospect is won. (S3) Employers use the same lead → prospect → client path; AG-17 is revised last. (S4) The assessment (AG-03) is optional, never a gate. (S5) A best-in-class sales CRM that is not complex on the firm side; complexity is acceptable on the platform side; a Meta app and WhatsApp integration for lead campaigns are in scope. (S6) Epic review order: AG-02 → AG-03 → AG-04 → AG-05 → AG-06 → AG-08 → AG-09 → AG-10 → AG-11 → AG-12 → AG-13 → AG-15 (+CP-01) → AG-14 → AG-18 → AG-17.
- **AG02-D1 — How new leads are shared; round robin for leads and bookings (the product owner, 20 Oct: "let firm decide how they want and build the same engine rule here", "nothing to do with program etc", "yes").** Closes AG-02 D8.10 Q-ROUTE and D8.74 Q-TAKE-IT. **Replaces AG16-89(8) "no round robin" and AG07-D20(j) "booking round robin withdrawn"** for leads and multi-host bookings only; AG16-89's other exclusions (routing rules, tags, teams) stay. Home: AG-02 (leads); AG-04 (bookings). Epics affected: AG-02, AG-04, AG-16 (Settings → Advanced), AG-13 (New leads list), SA-06 (no new key), 00-Overview (rule 8 wording). (1) **One setting, "How new leads are shared"**, Settings → Advanced, Owner or Admin (`agent/firm-capability:manage`), shown only once the firm has 2 or more users (X-4): **Anyone takes it** (default; the lead waits in New leads) · **Round robin** between the people the admin ticks · **Always one person**. **No conditions** (no programme, source, language or country), no weighting, no workload limits. (2) **Multi-host booking type** (AG-04): **First free person** (today, AG-4-51) or **Round robin** — turns in order among the hosts free at that slot. (3) **One shared "next person" helper** for both places; each place keeps its own list and its own turn (least recently given, skipping inactive people). (4) **Facts come first; the setting never overrides them:** a one-user firm's user, a one-to-one booking's host, or the person who typed or created the lead owns it. (5) **Take it** (anyone with `agent/prospect:edit` who can see the lead; same as AG-7-106 for cases) and **Assign** (`agent/prospect:assign`) always work, in every mode. (6) **"Skip me for now"** per person in the rotation, on the New leads screen, switched by that person or the admin in 1 click (there is no away status in the product). (7) **A person who leaves or is deactivated** drops out of every rotation; if the "one person" leaves or the rotation is empty, the setting returns to Anyone takes it and the Owner is told. (8) **Late-response notice:** to the owner; if the lead was not picked by a person (shared automatically, or still unowned), also to Owner and Admin (AG16-60). (9) **A lead flagged as a possible match skips the rotation**: it waits in New leads with the banner and the matched person's owner is told (AG-02 Q-DEDUP / Q-RETURN, AG16-44). (10) **"This week: Client C 5 · Client A 3 · Farah 4"** beside New leads, shown to Owner and Admin. (11) **Reuse** the built frontend screen `ERv2-frontend/src/features/pipeline/prospect-settings/components/prospect-assignment-rule-pane.tsx` (mock data, no backend) with its source and programme conditions removed. Code: V1.5 `ROUND_ROBIN` is an enum value only (`Expat V1.5/DevAPI-main/src/booking/booking.enums.ts:7`); no round-robin logic exists in ERv2. Effort about 1–1.5 developer-weeks. Pattern: Calendly (round robin hosts), Zoho CRM (lead round robin). Not built.
- **AG02-D2 — Becoming a client: three ways in, one step; Undo while empty (the product owner, 19 Oct: "a", "go", "a"; 20 Oct: "keep undo convert too").** Closes AG-02 D8.1 Q-CONVERT. Home: AG-02. Epics affected: AG-05 (§3.11, `:163`), AG-07 (AG-7-70, `:542` "convert a signed lead"), AG-06, AG-14, SA-06, contracts. (1) A prospect becomes a client **once**, by whichever comes first: **ExpatReady Signer EXECUTED** (client signed **and** firm countersigned; while waiting the deal shows "Client signed, waiting for countersign"); **Signed offline** (AG-5-39, `agent/retainer:offline-sign`, reason); or **manual "Convert to client"** with the confirm "Retainer not signed. Work can start, invoices can't, the case will show a warning." A later signature only clears the warning and enables invoicing — no second client, no second WON. (2) **WON = became a client.** Store `wonVia` (`E_SIGNED` / `SIGNED_OFFLINE` / `MANUAL`) and the signed date separately (AG-14: "converted before signing", "days to sign"). (3) Nothing client-facing fires; onboarding starts at case creation (AG-07). (4) **Undo convert** only while the client is empty (no case, no invoice, no signed retainer): the deal returns to its previous stage, WON is cleared, the person returns to prospect, the client number is never reused; reason required; timeline and audit. After a case exists: Withdraw (AG-7-50); the deal stays WON. (5) Convert and Undo use `agent/prospect:convert` (SA-06:1033); Undo is added to the SA-6-18 reason-required list. (6) Contract: `prospect.converted` requires `caseId` (`packages/contracts/src/events/crm.events.ts:37-62`); use `prospect.won` + `client.created` instead. Pending: AG02-P1 (billing without a signed retainer), AG02-P2 (correcting a wrong "Signed offline") in AG-05 §13.
- **AG02-D3 — Meta Lead Ads through Zapier now, native later; lead keys on every plan (the product owner, 19 Oct: "yes B+", "A").** Closes D8.2 Q-META (option B+) and D8.17/17a Q-PLAN. Home: AG-02 (lead arrival); AG-16 (Connections). Epics affected: AG-16, SA-03, SA-08, 99-Phasing. (1) An **ExpatReady app on Zapier** with a "Create lead" action (invite-only first) and a ready-made Zap "Facebook Lead Ads → ExpatReady lead", linked from Connections. (2) Data foundations now: source "Meta lead ad", Meta lead / form / ad ids, arrival key `meta:<lead id>`. (3) The AG16-32 form-email fallback stays. (4) **Native Meta Lead Ads is a named Release 2 item** (AG02-P5 in SA-08 §13). AG-2-6's non-goal "No listing of ExpatReady in Zapier's app directory" is withdrawn; AG16-72 and AG16-32 are not reversed. (5) A lead with no email and no phone is refused by the API ("A lead needs an email or a phone"); Zapier shows the failed run. (6) **The lead API key and the Zapier app are on every plan**, created by Owner or Admin under Connections; SA-03 #21 Developer API (read API, webhooks) stays sellable and unchanged.
- **AG02-D4 — WhatsApp in the sales flow (the product owner, 19–20 Oct).** Closes D8.3/3a Q-WA, D8.9 Q-AUTOLEAD, D8.16a. Home: AG-10 (WhatsApp); AG-02 (lead creation). Epics affected: AG-10, AG-16, SA-08, SA-03. (1) Own number vs platform number is already decided by AG16-19 and AG16-48 (firm's own account through a contracted provider, SA-8-72). (2) Message fees moved to SA-08/SA-03 (AG02-P3). AG-2-89 becomes a one-line pointer to AG-10/AG-16. Web forms, the Lead API / Zapier and form-notification rules (AG16-32) still create leads automatically. (4) No automatic in-window WhatsApp acknowledgement (SA05-Q11 kept).
- **AG02-D5 — "Who is this" panel on every conversation (the product owner, 19 Oct: "yes, go with this design").** Home: AG-10 (conversation screen). Epics affected: AG-02, AG-07, AG-09. On every email and WhatsApp conversation a panel shows who the sender is and their stage, with **one main button** and the rest under "More": Unknown → **Create lead** (More: Add client · Add client and start case · Link to existing); Lead → **Engage** (More: Convert to client · Start case); Prospect → **Convert to client** if not signed (More: Start case); Client → **Start case** if none open, else **Open case**. Every button opens an existing flow, runs the duplicate check, creates nothing by itself and sends nothing. Follows X-4 (no owner line or Assign in a one-user firm; email only until WhatsApp is connected). **Parked to the AG-09 review (AG02-P10):** when a case is created, offer files received on chat or email before the case for its checklist with one Yes.
- **AG02-D6 — "Is this the same person?" on every way in (the product owner, 20 Oct: "yes"; Case Officer: "2 yes").** Closes D8.4 Q-DEDUP, D8.4a–h, D8.5 Q-RETURN, D8.5a, D8.69 (in part). Home: AG-02 (AG-2-14, AG-2-68, AG-2-78). Epics affected: AG-04 (BR-7, AG02-P8), AG-07, AG-10, AG-18, SA-06. Applies AG16-44(1) to every door (form, WhatsApp, booking, API, typed by hand). (1) The lead is created on a new person with a banner "Same email as Client B (client)": **Link** (pre-selected for an exact email) · **Keep separate** · **Merge…**; nothing links, merges or hides by itself. (2) No stored match strength; the banner says what matched (email, phone, business number). (3) A repeat inquiry while a lead is open makes a second lead (AG16-60). (4) Two arrivals in the same second are both created and flag each other once saved. (5) After Link the existing owner wins; if none, the new lead's owner carries over. (6) Merge copies every consent row, including withdrawals. (7) "New company" despite a suggestion writes no flag. (8) A pair kept separate is flagged again with "Kept separate before". (9) A returning client's enquiry: new lead + banner + one notice to the matched person's owner; it skips round robin (AG02-D1(9)). (10) `wasClientAtArrival` is true only after Link; `previousLeadId` is set on Link. (11) **Link and Keep separate come with `agent/prospect:edit`** (Case Officers included); Merge, delete, Message many and import stay with Case Manager and above. (12) The built `merge-party.service.ts` must not be wired to a normal request route as shipped; a Merge route needs a SECURITY-DEFINER redesign (AG02-P12).
- **AG02-D7 — Lead owner, reply reminder and going cold (the product owner, 20 Oct: "1", "yes").** Closes D8.53, D8.65/65a Q-CLOCK, D8.23 Q-FRT, D8.67 Q-BREACH-NOTIFY, D8.68, D8.43 Q-COLD-N, D8.66 Q-COLD-DAYS. Home: AG-02. Epics affected: AG-16 (Timings screen), AG-13, AG-14, AG-11. (1) **A lead has its own owner** (`Lead.ownerUserId`), like a prospect. Assigning a returning client's new enquiry to Client A does not change Client C as file owner. A brand-new person's file owner is the owner of their first lead. (2) **Lead reply reminder:** one optional setting, "Remind us if a new lead has no reply after: Off / 1 hour / 4 hours / 1 working day / custom", **Off by default**; the New leads list always shows how long each lead has waited. When on, it applies to leads from unattended doors only; a logged call or sent message counts as the reply. (3) **Going cold** is a quiet mark only, with no notice; default 7 days the firm is open, firm-changeable on the Timings screen (X-5); any call, message or meeting resets it.
- **AG02-D8 — "Message many" (the product owner, 20 Oct: "sure", "3 paid extra").** Closes D8.7 Q-CAMPAIGN and D8.7a–f, D8.8 Q-CONSENT (other arrivals), D8.17b. Home: AG-02 (audience, consent check); AG-10 (delivery). Epics affected: AG-10, AG-12, SA-03 (#28 renamed, #26), SA-06 (SA-06:1193 "Send anyway" conflicts: AG02-P13), SA-11, AG-14. (1) One action **"Message many"** on any filtered list of leads or clients: choose email or WhatsApp and a template; preview "112 will get it, 8 skipped (no consent)"; Send; cancel while sending stops queued recipients. (2) **Express consent on that channel only**; an inbound WhatsApp message is not marketing consent; imported consent per MAP:368 stands. (3) "Stop" opts out at once. (4) Frequency limit default 1 marketing message per person per week, counting every marketing message; firm-changeable (X-5). (5) Results: sent, opened, replied. (6) One permission (`agent/communication:bulk-send`). (7) **Not built:** campaign objects, schedules, A/B tests, drips, list uploads, marketing dashboards. (8) **Paid extra:** SA-03 #28 renamed "Message many"; WhatsApp sending also needs #26. WhatsApp sending starts once the firm's provider is connected.
- **AG02-D9 — What a lead carries and what staff do with it (the product owner, 20 Oct).** Closes D8.6 Q-ENGAGE, D8.16 Q-ACK, D8.19 Q-SEQ, D8.25 Q-TAGS, D8.86 Q-INDICATIVE-RESULT, D8.18 Q-KEYS, D8.85 Q-LEADKEY-PERM. Home: AG-02. Epics affected: AG-07 (labels on the person; AG02-P9 firm-service case), AG-11, AG-12, SA-01B, SA-01, SA-06. (1) **"Interested in"** on the lead and the prospect: pack programmes plus the **firm's own services** (e.g. Canadian passport, OCI card, translation), added by staff like a label; the Owner or Admin renames or removes one from the same list; the web form question uses the list; board and lists filter by it; one pipeline. (2) **Labels on the person** (follow the person as lead, prospect and client): "+ Label" on the header only; at most 2 chips then "+n"; type-ahead, case-insensitive; unused labels drop out of suggestions; Owner or Admin rename or delete from the filter menu; a filter on every list, bulk "Add label to selected", usable in Message many; staff only; no colours, groups, automation or labels on emails. Changes Overview rule 8 "no shared tag systems" for people only. (3) **Indicative result is staff only** (DEC:28); SA-01B lines 70, 123, 434, 534 to be corrected. (4) **Automatic "thanks" email** only for unattended doors (web form, Lead API / Zapier, form-notification email), not for typed-in leads, bookings, imports or returning clients; the firm switches it off or edits it on the Automatic messages page. (5) **Engage is always a person's click** (S2); the Engage box opens inline from retainer, booking or proposal; no new `agent/prospect:engage` key. (6) **One-click "Add to follow-up"** in Release 1 using library follow-ups; the firm edits wording and timing and can switch steps off; new steps and firm-written follow-ups are Release 2. (7) The lead API key is made by Owner or Admin under Connections, on every plan (AG02-D3).
- **AG02-D10 — Pipeline, model and dashboard defaults (the product owner, 20 Oct).** Closes D8.11–D8.15, D8.21, D8.24, D8.45, D8.52, D8.76, D8.87, D8.26. Home: AG-02 unless stated. - **AG02-D12 — Small AG-02 defaults settled by the 20 Oct check (the product owner, 20 Oct: "run a check and decide the one with 90% or more confidence").** 77 D8 sub-questions decided at 90% or more confidence, each following a decision above; written on the D8 rows of the AG-02 draft (full table: `FINAL-Agent-FR/_drafts/AG02-D8-TRIAGE_2026-10-20.md`). - **AG02-D13 — Nine gaps found while writing the AG-02 draft (the product owner, 20 Oct: "apply").** Home: AG-02. Epics affected: AG-02, AG-18 (item 4). (1) When the lead reply reminder is switched on, 1 hour is pre-selected. (2) "New leads" in AG02-D1/D7 means the Unassigned leads list; the name stays "Unassigned". (3) A logged call counts as the reply whether outbound or inbound. (4) An imported lead with no owner waits in Unassigned leads and never goes through the sharing setting. (5) Unassigned leads are listed oldest first. (6) Renaming or removing a firm service or a label needs `agent/prospect:configure-pipeline` (Owner, Admin). (7) A firm service works on Individual and Corporate prospects; "Interested in" is copied from the lead to the prospect at Engage and is editable. (8) After Undo convert, if the same person becomes a client again, they get their earlier client number back; a number is never given to anyone else. (9) An expired or voided retainer does not supply the representative; the next retainer pre-fills it from the normal default (AG01-5, AG07-D3), as in v1.14 AG-2-74.
- **AG02-D14 — Answers after the end-to-end checks of the AG-02 draft (the product owner, 20 Oct: "go").** Source: three read-only checks of 20 Oct (draft against itself, against the other epics, against the code on `ERv2-sanitized` branch `feat/ag02-step0-client-contracts` @c20b60aa3 and `ERv2-frontend` `dev` @5ccd9faa). Home: AG-02. Epics affected: AG-07, AG-04, AG-11, AG-14, AG-17, SA-06. (1) **Undo convert** removes client status only if this sale created the client (`originProspectId`); a person who was already a client stays a client. (2) A **booking made by staff** counts as the reply to a lead. (3) **"Use existing person"** counts as Link. (4) **Link has its own simple route**; only Merge waits for the SECURITY-DEFINER redesign (AG02-P12). (5) **Who can see a person's items** follows AG-7-90; AG-02 adds no rule of its own. (6) **One client-number counter per firm**, used by Convert and by Add client (AG-07). (7) The **lead representative** follows AG-7-104. (8) **Firm-made lead sources** are Release 1. (9) **Dropped:** stage entry conditions; per-stage "going stale" days; frontend forecast panel, leaderboard, first-response target screen, assignment-rule conditions, priorities, "away" windows and load-balanced strategy (AG02-D1 keeps only the three sharing choices and "Skip me for now"). (12) **Prospect number** `P-00001`, no year (the branch's `PRS-2026-000001` changes). (13) **Referral partner links** are Release 1: each partner gets a tracked web-form link and its leads arrive with source Referral and that partner. (14) **Company contact roles:** HR, Signatory, Billing, Primary, Other (Applicant and Spouse are family links, not company roles). (15) The AG-02 code on the branch follows v1.14; the change list is `FINAL-SuperAdmin-FR/DEV-TASKS_2026-10-12.md`, section I.
- **AG02-D15 — Last four AG-02 points (the product owner, 20 Oct: "ok").** Home: AG-02. Epics affected: AG-06, AG-18, SA-06, SA-03, AG-14, SA-11. (1) The lead reply reminder covers unattended doors only; a lead made by a person's "Create lead" click (from Unmatched, WhatsApp or an email) has no reminder. Reverses the AG02-D12 default for D8.65. Leads are AG-02's own records (AG-2-64), so AG-7-90 is unchanged.
- **AG02-D17 — Pre-share check of AG-02 v2.0 (the product owner, 20 Oct: "a").** Home: AG-02. (1) A known person with an open lead (no open prospect) who books a consult themselves is handled like every other door (AG02-D6): the booking makes a new lead with the possible-match banner; it counts on the existing lead (e.g. for going cold) only after staff click Link. (2) Business number matches companies only, at Engage, not people. (3) A Zapier Meta lead without `platformLeadId` is still refused; Zapier shows the failed run to the firm (same pattern as "a lead needs an email or a phone", AG02-D3(5)).
- **AG02-D18 — "Send anyway?" for a one-to-one marketing email (the product owner, 21 Oct: "B").** Home: SA-05 §3.6.5 (send routes). Epics affected: SA-08, SA-11, DEV-TASKS, AG-10. A staff member sending **one** marketing email from the firm's own mailbox to a person without marketing consent sees the "Send anyway?" warning and may send; the send is recorded. **"Message many" stays strict:** express consent on that channel only, no "Send anyway?", no WhatsApp-to-email fallback (AG02-D8(2), D15(4)). Service emails and replies are not affected. Narrows how SA-08 and SA-11 applied AG02-D8(2) on 21 Oct.
- **AG02-D19 — WhatsApp marketing always needs the WhatsApp marketing consent (the product owner, 21 Oct: "yes" to option A).** Home: SA-05 §3.6.5 (send routes); consent purpose in SA-11. Epics affected: SA-11, AG-10, SA-08, AG-02 (no change: already strict for Message many). Any WhatsApp marketing message, one-to-one or Message many, goes only to people with the express `WHATSAPP_MARKETING` consent; there is no "Send anyway?" on WhatsApp (Meta can downgrade or block the firm's number). The AG02-D18 exception applies to email from the own mailbox only. WhatsApp service messages and replies are unchanged (they need the WhatsApp opt-in). Also settled (90%+, no question): a "Send anyway?" email is recorded as an audit entry on that message in SA-11's audit log (who, when, "marketing consent missing").
- **X-4 — Grows with the firm (the product owner, 19 Oct: "make sure we do not complicate for solo or small firms… across the epics and platform"; 20 Oct: X-4 kept).** Home: Agent `00-Overview` → "Product rules every epic follows". Epics affected: all. There is no firm-size setting or tier. Team features (owner lines, Assign, offices, sharing settings, routing hints) appear only when a fact makes them useful: a second user, a second office, a connected channel. Day-to-day features are never reduced. Already applied in AG16-42, AG01-10, AG16-25, AG16-89(2), AG16-22, AG16-40.
- **X-5 — The firm sets the timings that shape its work (the product owner, 20 Oct: "let firm decide… any such decision anywhere must be revisited"; narrow version "yes").** Home: Agent `00-Overview` → "Product rules every epic follows"; the screen: AG-16 Settings → Advanced → **Timings**. Epics affected: all (each epic's review applies it to its own timings). A firm may change a timing **only if it affects its clients or how the firm chooses to work** (reminders and chasers to clients, the lead reply reminder, going cold, task due dates). Each has a stated default and sits on the one Timings screen, pre-filled. Everything else stays a fixed default and is not shown. Government deadlines and security or legal limits stay fixed. Sweep of 100 timings (29 firm, 17 borderline, 49 fixed, 5 stale) in `FINAL-Agent-FR/_drafts/TIMINGS-SWEEP_2026-10-20.md`, to be applied epic by epic.

## AG-04 review — decisions (the product owner, 21 Oct)

Asked one at a time on 21 Oct 2026. The product owner's standing instruction for every decision and for the AG-04 rewrite (21 Oct): use what already exists in the product; do not over-engineer. Fix list and impact map: `scratchpad/AG04-IMPACT.md` (session 4dc0f451). Home of every decision is AG-04 unless stated.

- **AG04-D1 — An unpaid paid booking still makes a lead (the product owner, 21 Oct: "a").** Epics affected: AG-02, DEV-TASKS, DEV-HANDOVER-AG02. When a paid booking's slot hold ends unpaid (`EXPIRED`), the booker becomes a lead: source Booking, note "Payment not completed". A booker who pays becomes a lead as today (`booking.scheduled`). No lead while the hold runs. Client messages stay the Q23 ones. AG-04 emits `booking.expired` (same payload as the built `booking.scheduled`); AG-02's arrival path consumes it with the arrival key `booking:` + bookingId, so one booking never makes two leads.
- **AG04-D2 — Case calls start from the case (the product owner, 21 Oct: "a"; closes the open part of AG02-P8).** Epics affected: AG-02, AG-07, SA-05, 00-Overview, 99-Phasing, DEV-TASKS, DEV-HANDOVER-AG02. Appointment types with purpose Follow-up or Case review never show on the public page. From a case, staff book the time, or click "Send booking link" (an email with a no-login link for that case). The booking carries `caseId`, lands on the case, and makes no lead, no banner and no "Call now" task. Staff made the link, so AG02-D6 holds. A client who books a public consult on their own still becomes a new lead with the banner (AG02-D17(1)).
- **AG04-D3 — Attendance is asked, never guessed (the product owner, 21 Oct: "a"; closes AG-04 Q8).** Epics affected: AG-02, AG-11, AG-14, SA-05, 99-Phasing. No automatic No-show. 30 minutes after a meeting ends (fixed, not shown), the booking and the host's home screen show "Did Client B attend? Attended · No-show"; one click records it and the host can change it later. No answer → nothing sent. `booking.completed` / `booking.no_show` are emitted only by that click; the No-show recovery and Post-consultation recipes stay off by default (11 Oct decision 5).
- **AG04-D4 — Booking timings: firm defaults on Timings, changeable per type (the product owner, 21 Oct: "a"; reconciles R15 with X-5).** Epics affected: AG-16, AG-12, SA-05. The Timings screen holds the defaults; each appointment type may change any of them under its "Advanced". Defaults: reminders 24 h and 1 h before; shortest notice 4 h; booking window 60 days; gap before 0 min / after 10 min; client self-service cancel or reschedule up to 4 h before (then "reply to this email"); paid hold 30 min, "Complete your payment" at 10 min (Q23).
- **AG04-D5 — Video gaps closed (the product owner, 21 Oct: "a"; closes AG-04 Q15–Q17).** Epics affected: AG-16, AG-01, AG-11, SA-03, SA-05, SA-08, 00-Overview, 99-Phasing. (1) My account → Video: "Use for my video bookings: Meet/Teams · Zoom · My meeting link", pre-set to the first source the person has. (2) No source: Video is still offered; the confirmation says "Your join link will follow"; one home-screen item "Add a video link for Client B's 10:00 call"; a booking moved to a new host gets the new host's link. (3) A person's own Zoom is on every plan (as AG16-24).
- **AG04-D6 — Staff may book any time, with a warning (the product owner, 21 Oct: "a"; closes AG-04 Q18).** Epics affected: AG-16, SA-06, 00-Overview. Closed day, outside hours, inside the notice limit or a clash with the host's other booking: a warning, then the staff member may continue. The public page stays strict. Rule 7.
- **AG04-D7 — Consult record: every field, nothing entered twice (the product owner, 21 Oct: "b but more efficient and remove redundancy", then "yes").** Epics affected: AG-02, AG-05, AG-07, 00-Overview, 99-Phasing. Attended (shown from D3), time spent (pre-filled with the booked length), notes, recommended programmes and services (picked from "Interested in", AG02-D9(1)), optional quoted fee. The next step is the button clicked: "Engage and create proposal" on a lead (Engage, then AG-05's proposal pre-filled), "Create proposal" on a prospect, "Request documents", "Close lead" (reason). No separate next-step list. A case call's record sits on the case and offers no proposal.
- **AG04-D8 — Six booking permission keys (the product owner, 21 Oct: "a"; keeps AG16-83 #25).** Epics affected: SA-06, AG-01, AG-02, AG-05, AG-06, AG-14, SA 99-Phasing, DEV-TASKS, DEV-HANDOVER-AG02. Kept: `agent/booking-event-type:list`, `:edit` (create, delete, rules, reminders, booking page), `:configure-availability`; `agent/booking:list`, `:create`, `:edit` (reschedule, cancel, attendance, consult record). Withdrawn: `booking-event-type:create`, `:delete`, `:configure-reminders`, `booking-page:configure`, `booking:reschedule`, `:mark-attendance`, `booking-consult:write`, `:create-proposal` (AG-05's key), `booking-analytics:view` (AG-14's keys; paid-consult revenue under `agent/report:view-financial`). Billing holds no booking key.
- **AG04-D9 — Group session attendees become leads without "Call now" (the product owner, 21 Oct: "a").** Epics affected: AG-02. Each new attendee of a group booking becomes a lead (source "Booking: <session name>") with no "Call now" task; one-to-one bookings keep "Call now".
- **AG04-D10 — A full group session takes details, no waitlist (the product owner, 21 Oct: "a").** Epics affected: AG-02. A full session's page says "This session is full. Leave your details and we'll contact you."; the person becomes a lead (source "Booking: session full", no "Call now"). No waitlist, no automatic seat offers. Replaces the AG-04 "waitlists" deferral.
- **AG04-D11 — Booking consent sentence is the firm's (the product owner, 21 Oct: "a"; the AG16-88(5) flag for the booking page).** Epics affected: AG-16, SA-05. The booking page carries an English starter consent sentence the firm can edit (so a Quebec firm can write French). No French build in Release 1; revisit before selling to Quebec firms.
- **AG04-D12 — The calendar event holds only what runs the meeting (the product owner, 21 Oct: "yes, that's fine").** Epics affected: SA-08, SA-11. The event ExpatReady writes to a person's own calendar: title "<type> — <booker name>", time, video link, "Open in ExpatReady" (signed-in staff only). Never intake answers, phone or notes. The booker is not added as an attendee (ExpatReady sends its own invite).
- **Settled without a question (stated to the product owner 21 Oct).** (S1) A paid consult invoice before Engage sits with the person (`partyId`), per AG02-D15(1); AG-06 corrected. (S2) No AG-04 dashboard: consult volume, show-rate, no-show rate and paid-consult revenue per currency (R18) are defined in AG-04 and shown in AG-14's Sales section (R19, AG02-D10(7)). (S3) The booking page sits under the firm's one web address (AG01-1). (S4) The slot start interval stays a per-type field (built in the frontend, `appointment-type-types.ts:66-67`), defaulting to the meeting length (Rule 9); closes AG-04 Q9. (S5) Refunding a cancelled paid consult is Release 1 (R16 kept refund tiers): full or none by the notice window; AG-06 gains this one refund case. (S6) Group and collective hosting stay Release 1 (R16) with their missing rules written; the per-type video provider is dropped (AG16-53 postdates R16); hosts in two offices read their hours in their own office's time zone (AG16-45).
- **AG04-D13 — A new paid booker gets a person record when the booking starts (the product owner, 22 Oct: "a"; closes AG-04 C11 Q1).** Epics affected: AG-02, AG-06, DEV-TASKS. When a paid booking starts (`PENDING_PAYMENT`) and the booker's email is not on file, AG-02 makes the person record only: no lead, no "Call now", nothing in New leads. The consult invoice sits on that person (S1). The lead follows on `booking.scheduled` or `booking.expired` (D1 unchanged).
- **AG04-D14 — Refunds outside the rule are made outside and recorded (the product owner, 22 Oct: "b but there has to be a place to update that payment has been refunded to track").** Epics affected: AG-06, DEV-TASKS. The S5 rule stands: ExpatReady offers the refund only when the client cancels before the self-service limit. When the firm cancels late, or a payment arrives after the hold ended, the firm refunds in its own payment account or bank; on the consult invoice staff click **"Record refund"** (amount, date, reason, optional reference) — the existing offline-refund record of AG-6-58 — and the invoice shows Refunded. Uses `agent/refund:create`. Nothing new is built.
- **AG04-D15 — Orchestrator rulings O1–O4 confirmed (the product owner, 22 Oct: "a").** Epics affected: AG-04, AG-02, AG-06. (O1) A full session shows AG-02's lead form (web-form door). (O2) A refund for an in-time client cancel is offered on the refund holder's home screen ("Refund Client B $150?") and confirmed with one click; nothing is refunded by itself. (O3) "Send booking link" from a case uses `agent/booking:create`. (O4) D6's knowing overlap is for staff bookings only; the public page never double-books.
- **AG04-D16 — AG-04 author defaults confirmed (the product owner, 22 Oct: "a").** Epics affected: AG-04. AG-04 Part D7 items 1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 7 confirmed as written: a case booking link is used once and ends when the case closes or a new link is sent; the attendance item stays in the host's My Tasks until answered; the first host of a collective booking is the main host; an Owner or Admin may edit a colleague's hours (`configure-availability`); the consent sentence is a `ConsentWording` edited in Legal settings; the booking address is `/book/<type-slug>`. Item 5 was decided by D14; item 8 by D17.
- **AG04-D17 — Several named sets of hours per person, as built (the product owner, 22 Oct: "a", after "are you sure it's best for expat?").** Epics affected: AG-04, AG-16, DEV-TASKS.
