Built stays: a person keeps one or more named sets of weekly hours, one marked default (`ERv2-frontend/src/features/scheduler/components/availability/availability-types.ts:72-74`); each appointment type's host row says which of that host's sets it uses (`appointment-types/appointment-type-types.ts:1-11`). A new person gets one set "Normal hours" copied from the office hours (Rule 9); a solo user never needs a second. Use: evening or early hours for overseas clients on some types only. Time off stays the out-of-office dates. Replaces AG-04 Part D7 item 8.
- **AG04-D18 — Team list shows no video status (the product owner, 22 Oct: "a"; closes AG-04 C11 Q2).** Epics affected: AG-04. The Team list column stays "Email / Calendar connected" (AG-01). A host with no video source is caught at booking time by D5(2).
- **AG04-D19 — Owner and Admin may enter a colleague's out-of-office dates (the product owner, 22 Oct: "a").** Epics affected: AG-04, AG-16, SA-06. Same key and screen as a colleague's hours (`agent/booking-event-type:configure-availability`, Owner, Admin); each person still manages their own with no key. Entering time off never cancels or moves existing bookings; staff move them (AG16-76).
- **AG04-D20 — AG-14 follows R18: no conversion (the product owner, 22 Oct: "a").** Epics affected: AG-14, AG-06, AG-05, SA 00-Overview (applied 22 Oct; AG-06 still carried `FxRateSnapshot` and a firm reporting currency). R18 already decided "no FX feed, no conversion — totals shown per currency, never summed; reporting-currency and FX-source settings removed". AG-14's `reportingCurrency`, `FxRateSnapshot` and FX-normalising rules are removed; every money total is one total per currency (e.g. "Collected this month: CAD 42,000 · USD 3,500"). No new decision; applies R18.
- **AG04-D21 — Each set of hours keeps its built time-zone picker, pre-filled with the office zone (the product owner, 22 Oct: "a").** Epics affected: AG-04. Built stays (`ERv2-frontend/src/features/scheduler/components/availability/availability-types.ts:73`, `schedule-editor-panel.tsx:65,161`). A new set starts in the person's office time zone (firm zone with one office, AG16-45); the person may change it, e.g. a case officer working from Delhi sets "Asia/Kolkata". The set's hours are read in the set's zone; the screen shows it ("Times in Asia/Kolkata"). Office business hours and closed days stay in the office zone (AG16-45). Narrows AG-04 rule "the person does not choose a zone".
- **AG04-D22 — Each person decides their own booking hours; business hours no longer cap slots (the product owner, 22 Oct: "a is fine.. but every user must have right to decide their calendar, 24 hours.. 7 days a week.. why restrict anything"; then "a" on closed days).** Amends AG16-6 (business hours no longer bound bookable slots; they stay for the firm's internal clocks). Epics affected: AG-04, AG-16, DEV-HANDOVER-AG07-AG16. (1) A person may set hours on any day, at any time, in any of their sets. Bookable slots come from the person's set (∩ the type's windows, if any), never capped by the firm's business hours. (2) Business hours are only the starting copy for a new person's "Normal hours" (AG04-D17). (3) The firm's closed days still block public slots (AG16-46); staff may still book a closed day after a warning (D6). (4) Staff warnings keep "outside the host's hours"; "outside business hours" is dropped. (5) Notice, booking window and caps stay per-type timing rules.
- **X-6 — Two currencies, one per prospect or case, no mixing (the product owner, 22 Oct: "let's keep it simple… CAD and USD… one prospect or one case must have only one currency, no mix… anything which is no prospect or case can be put in as default unless the firm user changes it before sending… restrict unnecessary complexity"; rules 1–7 then "a").** Home: Agent `00-Overview` → "Product rules every epic follows". Narrows R18 and AG01-9; replaces AG-6-46's "billing party's currency" rule; settles the AG-6-46 vs AG-07 BR-16 conflict. Epics affected: AG-01, AG-02, AG-04, AG-05, AG-06, AG-07, AG-14, AG-16, Agent 00-Overview and 99-Phasing; SA documents that list firm currencies (not SA-04, which is ExpatReady's own subscription billing). Rules: (1) Release 1 supports **CAD and USD only**; the firm default is CAD, and the firm may switch its default to USD in the existing default-currency field (AG01-9). More currencies later. (2) **Each prospect and each case has one currency**, set at creation from the firm default. (3) **A case takes its prospect's currency** when the prospect becomes a client; nobody chooses it twice. (4) **The currency locks when the first invoice is sent** on that prospect or case; before that staff may change it. Every proposal, retainer, invoice, payment, refund and credit note on that prospect or case uses it. (5) **Anything with no prospect or case** (e.g. a paid consult before Engage) uses the firm default; staff may change it before sending. A paid appointment type's price uses the firm default unless staff change it on the type. (6) **No currency field on a person or company** (the "billing party's currency" is removed). (7) **Reports**: at most two totals, e.g. "CAD 42,000 · USD 3,500", never added or converted (R18). Also closes the AG-02 wording that calls exchange rates "Release 2": there is no conversion (R18).
- **X-6(8) — The built exchange-rate code is removed (the product owner, 22 Oct: "a").** Home: DEV-TASKS J20. Epics affected: AG-02, AG-16, SA-09, DEV-TASKS. R18 and X-6 rule out conversion, so the built `fx` module (`apps/api/src/fx/`), the `FxRateSnapshot` table (`apps/api/prisma/models/events-rails.prisma:65-90`) and the `fxSnapshotId` field in the event contracts (`packages/contracts/src/events/billing.events.ts:39, 94, 153`; `agreements.events.ts:45`, where it is required) are deleted. Replaces AG-16's "leave it built and dormant" default and SA-09's "parked, stays unused". "Built stays" covers kept features, not code for a rule the product owner has withdrawn.

## AG-05 review — decisions (the product owner, 22 Oct)

Asked one at a time on 22 Oct 2026 under `REVIEW-METHOD_2026-10-22.md`. Gap check: `scratchpad/REVIEW-AG05-INTERNAL.md`, `REVIEW-AG05-CROSS-EPIC.md`, `REVIEW-AG05-CODE.md` (session 6bdc8979; code read at backend `origin/dev` 2617f7f4b and frontend `origin/dev` 31f36e99). Home of every decision is AG-05 unless stated.

- **AG05-D1 — One signing tool for every document; no new settings, no new keys (the product owner, 22 Oct: "a but can you make it better and confidence 95%", then "ok" to a+).** Epics affected: AG-05, AG-07, AG-09, AG-12, AG-15, AG-17, SA-05, SA-06, 00-Overview, 99-Phasing. (1) Any document can be sent for signature: a generated letter or job offer, or any PDF in the client's Documents, from a "Send for signature" button where the document is. (2) The sender picks who signs at send time: the client, the organisation's signatory, and optionally a firm person. A retainer comes with the firm's countersign pre-added (the retainer's representative); other documents have no firm signer unless added. No per-template countersign setting. (3) Same for every document: no-login signing link by email (AG16-92); the same signing page from the portal to-do (CP1-D4, AG-15-24); consent; certificate; automatic signing reminders; expiry; void; "signed offline" by uploading the paper (AG17-D66); the signed copy and certificate are emailed and filed in the client's Documents (AG-09). (4) Keys: retainers keep the `agent/retainer:*` keys; for any other document, whoever may send it to the client (`agent/portal:request-work`, AG-15) may send it for signature and mark it signed offline. (5) Retainer-only: countersign, the "Retainer not signed" warning, becoming a client, the invoice gate. (6) Government forms keep AG-07's own rule (a "Signed by client" tick or an upload; the representative's stored signature, AG-7 rule 7 at AG-07:2558-2564).
- **AG05-D2 — Several client signers on one retainer in Release 1 (the product owner, 25 Oct: "a").** Home: AG-05. Epics affected: AG-05, AG-02, AG-07, AG-15, CP-01, SA-05, DEV-TASKS. Removes AG-05's Release 2 deferral "multi-party beyond client + firm" (AG-05 v1.14:911). (1) The sender picks signers from the people on the case or prospect (principal, spouse, sponsor) or an organisation signatory (AG-5-50), as AG05-D1(2). (2) Client signers sign in any order, each with their own no-login link, consent and certificate line. (3) The firm countersigns after all client signers. (4) The retainer counts as signed (deposit request, becoming a client, invoice gate) only when everyone has signed. (5) Signing reminders go only to those who have not signed; status shows per person ("1 of 2 signed"). (6) A decline by one signer declines the whole retainer; staff edit and resend (MS-D2). (7) A witness signer stays out of Release 1.
- **AG05-D3 — The representative's stored signature is applied at send (the product owner, 25 Oct: "a"; confirms AG01-20 and settles it against AG-5-27).** Home: AG-05 (AG-01 keeps the stored signature, AG-1-72). Epics affected: AG-05, AG-01, AG-02, AG-07, SA-08, DEV-TASKS. (1) When the retainer's representative has a stored signature with consent (AG01-20), it is applied when staff send; the retainer is signed the moment all client signers have signed (AG05-D2); no countersign click and no countersign request. (2) With no stored signature, the representative countersigns after the clients (AG01-23(b)); when the representative is away another holder of `agent/retainer:countersign` may sign instead, recorded (AG07-D20(c), AG-5-27). (3) An edit before signing re-applies it (MS-D2). (4) The certificate states "Client A's signature applied, sent by Client C, 14 Mar" (AG01-20). AG-5-27's countersign step applies only when no signature is stored.
- **AG05-D4 — Remaining AG-05 open items closed (the product owner, 25 Oct: "a" = accept all 11).** Home: AG-05. Epics affected: AG-05, AG-02, AG-06, AG-07, AG-11, SA-06, DEV-TASKS. (1) The retainer's representative may countersign their own retainer without `agent/retainer:countersign`; others need the key to sign instead (closes Q13). (2) A countersign request notifies only the representative (AG07-D20(f)); other key holders see it on the retainer and may sign instead, not notified (closes Q14). (3) Every retainer includes the firm's signature (stored or countersigned); no "no countersignature" switch (closes Q1). (4) A corporate retainer with no organisation signatory on file warns "Pine Bakery has no signatory. Add one or choose who signs"; never a block. (5) "Marked signed by mistake" on a signed-offline retainer: reason required; holders of `agent/retainer:offline-sign`; allowed while nothing is billed from it and no deposit is received; returns it to its earlier state; recorded (closes AG02-P2). (6) A retainer never expires once any client has signed; a wait for the firm shows on the representative's Needs you. (7) A never-sent draft can be deleted; a client's decline reason is optional. (8) A decline shows "Declined" on the prospect or case and gives the case lead (or file owner) one home-screen item. (9) A second open retainer for the same client is allowed with "A retainer for Client B is already out. Open it?" (AG16-60; closes Q12). (10) Events: a "client signed, waiting for the firm" event (closes AG02-P85) and a separate "signed offline" event that carries no file (closes AG02-P87); names by the backend lead. (11) AG-05 becomes v2.0 in the X-1 format; v1.14 to `_archive/`; Page 1 lists the frontend screens to keep (builder, detail, offline-sign, void, certificate, signed list) and to change (Quote tab, refuse-to-send check, country clause defaults, bilingual preview, separate portal signing room). Applied as already decided (no new decision): starter clauses ordinary editable text, Owner locks the firm's own clauses (`agent/legal-settings:manage`, MAP:99, Q13 at MAP:405, O21); missing French → "Send anyway?" (MAP:25); English first (AG16-88); automatic signing reminders 3 days then every 4 (MAP:97); expiry 14 days on Timings (SA-Q2, X-5); consent text firm-replaceable, ExpatReady default (SA8-D28/D29); certificate fixed and factual (SA8-D28), evidence fixed in code (R32); retention = keep-period warning (Q19); storage as built (ExpatReady Canadian storage, firm-prefixed, per-firm keys; SA8-D8); no stuck-retainer alert (AG16-42); every plan (SA-03 #16); no portal opt-in flag (R17); no regulator names (Rule 7).

## AG-19 review — decisions (the product owner, 22 Oct)

Decided on 22 Oct 2026 for the new module AG-19 PDF Studio. Spec: `FINAL-Agent-FR/AG-19-pdf-studio_v1.0_2026-10-22.md`. Home of every decision is AG-19 unless stated. Open questions raised while writing are in AG-19 D4 (Q1–Q13) and in AG-09 §13 (open questions 29–31); none is decided here.

- **AG19-D1 — PDF Studio is its own module (the product owner, 22 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19. Epics affected: AG-19, AG-09, AG-07, 00-Overview, 99-Phasing. PDF Studio is not a Doer and not part of AG-09. It has its own menu item and workspace (page thumbnails, drag and drop, preview). It also opens in context: "Open in PDF Studio" on any AG-09 document and on a Binder checklist line, which pre-loads that line's files.
- **AG19-D2 — Release 1 tools; three tools to Release 2 (the product owner, 22 Oct: approved; the product owner chose the three Release 2 items).** Home: AG-19. Epics affected: AG-19, 00-Overview, 99-Phasing. Release 1: combine (merge), split / extract pages, remove pages, organize (reorder, add pages, duplicate page, insert blank page), rotate, crop, compress, images to PDF (JPG, PNG, HEIC, TIFF — the AG-09 allowed types), Word to PDF, phone scan, unlock (remove a password the client supplies), page numbers, redact. Workspace basics: undo / redo, select all, small / medium / large thumbnails, files grouped by source file. Release 2: repair PDF, webpage to PDF, OCR (make searchable).
- **AG19-D3 — Redaction in Release 1, true removal (the product owner, 22 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19. Epics affected: AG-19, AG-09. Redaction moves from Phase 2 (AG-09 v1.14:1334) into Release 1, inside PDF Studio. It must truly remove the underlying text and images, not draw a box over them.
- **AG19-D4 — Not built (the product owner, 22 Oct: "not needed, I don't see value").** Home: AG-19. Epics affected: AG-19, 00-Overview, 99-Phasing. Watermark, password-protect, compare versions and PDF/A are not in Release 1 and are not built. Not proposed for Release 1 and not ruled on one by one: PDF to Word / Excel / PowerPoint / JPG, PowerPoint / Excel to PDF, translate PDF, AI summarizer, PDF to Markdown; listed in AG-19 open question Q11 as "confirm: Release 2 or never".
- **AG19-D5 — No free editing of evidence; every Studio action audited (the product owner, 22 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19. Epics affected: AG-19, AG-09, SA-11. No free text or image editing on documents (misrepresentation risk). Allowed: page operations, crop, compress, convert, redact, page numbers. Every Studio action is written to the document's audit trail: who, when, what, source files.
- **AG19-D6 — Several files on one line (the product owner, 22 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 (data model); AG-19 states what Studio needs. Epics affected: AG-19, AG-09, AG-07, AG-15, 99-Phasing. A checklist line / document can hold several files ("parts") while it is being worked; e.g. a client uploads 4 pay stubs to "Pay stubs" and none is superseded. Versions remain for true replacements. Changes AG-09's one-current-file model (AG-09 v1.14:276, :283, :329). Applied in AG-09 v1.15 as `DocumentPart` (AG-9-65); the cases the decision does not cover are AG-09 open questions 29 and 30.
- **AG19-D7 — Originals kept (the product owner, 22 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19. Epics affected: AG-19, AG-09. When staff save a combined result, the merged PDF becomes the line's current file. The source files stay linked to it, read-only and restorable. The status stays Received until a person accepts (AG16-31).
- **AG19-D8 — Standalone use; no Studio storage (the product owner, 22 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19. Epics affected: AG-19, AG-09. From the menu, with no case, the result is saved to a record (case, prospect or client) or downloaded. Studio has no storage of its own (single home, AG16-11). Working files are cleared when the session ends.
- **AG19-D9 — Access: the document-edit permission, no new key (the product owner, 22 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19. Epics affected: AG-19, AG-09, AG-07, SA-06. Anyone with `agent/document:edit` (AG-09 §10) can use Studio, redaction included. No new permission (Rule 9). AG-09's existing read rule still applies: opening a part needs `agent/document:download` (AG-9-65 field 8).
- **AG19-D10 — Phone scan for clients and staff (the product owner, 22 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19. Epics affected: AG-19, AG-09, AG-15, 99-Phasing. In the client's no-login upload link (AG16-92) and the portal, and in Studio: snap several pages → one clean PDF, with automatic crop and straighten.
- **AG19-D11 — Engine: permissive open source; spike first (the product owner, 22 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19. Epics affected: AG-19. Only permissively licensed libraries (examples given: pdf-lib, qpdf, libvips/sharp, LibreOffice for Word to PDF). No AGPL library, no commercial SDK. True redaction and strong compression are the main risk: the first build card (AG-19-1) is a spike that proves both on real immigration document types (scanned bank statement, phone-photo pay stub) against a pass / fail bar; if it fails, it comes back to the product owner. Note found while writing: libvips is LGPL and the repo licence gate denies LGPL rows (AG-19 open question Q4).
- **AG19-D12 — Runs in the browser first; never a third-party service (the product owner, 22 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19. Epics affected: AG-19, AG-09, 00-Overview. Combine, organize, split, compress and redact run on the staff device; nothing leaves the device until staff save to a record. Word to PDF runs on ExpatReady's own server. Never a third-party processing service (founder directive, 00-Overview "In-House by Design").
- **AG19-D13 — "Prepare for upload" is the top tool (the product owner, 22 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19. Epics affected: AG-19, SA-01B (pending knock-on), 99-Phasing. One click on the selected files or pages: combine, convert images, compress to the size limit for that document type, name the file → preview → staff click Save. Size limits and file naming come from the country pack per document type; SA-01B's content pack configurator must carry these fields (pending; not yet in SA-01B v1.7). The label names no regulator (Rule 7). Over the limit after compressing → warn, never block (Rule 7). Staff still decide (AG16-76).
- **AG19-D14 — Interactive government forms are refused (the product owner, 22 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19. Epics affected: AG-19, AG-09, AG-07. Studio refuses XFA forms and never merges or flattens them. Message: "This is an interactive government form." with an [Open in form viewer] action pointing at the form viewer (`XFA-Gov-Form-Viewer-Spec_v1.0_2026-10-18.md`; AG-7-109); no Adobe detour. What the action opens for a firm-uploaded form is AG-19 open question Q3.

**Decided 23 Oct 2026 (the product owner approved AG19-D15 – AG19-D26 and said "yes" to applying them).** They answer AG-19 open questions Q1, Q3, Q4, Q6, Q8, Q11 – Q13 and parts of Q2 and Q10, and AG-09 open questions 29 – 31. Not decided, still open: AG-19 Q5 (unlock), Q7 (fillable and signed PDFs in Combine), Q9 (desktop-to-phone handoff). Applied on 23 Oct in AG-19 v1.1, AG-09 v1.16, AG-07 v3.5, AG-15 v1.12, CP-01 v0.4 and 99-Phasing-and-Glossary v1.13.

- **AG19-D15 — Licences (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19. Epics affected: AG-19. LGPL libraries (for example libvips inside sharp, and libheif) are allowed only on ExpatReady's own server, unmodified. Each must be added to the licence gate's allow-list (licence inventory): a build task; no ERv2 code or document is edited by this decision. The browser uses only permissive libraries (for example pdf-lib, pdf.js) plus the browser's own image encoder. HEIC converts on the server. A short legal check of the licence list happens before build. Corrects AG19-D11's example (libvips is LGPL, not permissive). Resolves AG-19 Q4.
- **AG19-D16 — XFA forms (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19. Epics affected: AG-19, AG-09, 99-Phasing. Studio refuses XFA government forms with a plain explanation: "This is an interactive government form. It can't be combined or changed. It stays on the checklist as its own file." The file stays on its line as its own part or document. [Open in form viewer] appears only when the country pack publishes that form edition; the viewer never opens firm-uploaded PDFs (`XFA-Gov-Form-Viewer-Spec_v1.0_2026-10-18.md:98`). Replaces AG19-D14's unconditional button. No regulator names in product text. Resolves AG-19 Q3.
- **AG19-D17 — Unredacted original (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19. Epics affected: AG-19, AG-09. After redaction the redacted file is current. The unredacted original is kept as a source but hidden from the client, the portal and the normal staff view. Only users holding AG-09's document-delete permission `agent/document:delete` can open it; every opening is written to the audit trail. Resolves AG-09 open question 31 and AG-19 Q13 (part).
- **AG19-D18 — Replace and Remove (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 (data and lifecycle). Epics affected: AG-09, AG-19, AG-07, AG-15, CP-01, 99-Phasing. Staff can Replace a single part (pick a new file; only that part changes) and Remove one or many parts in bulk (multi-select). Replaced and removed files go to "Recently deleted" (the existing AG-09 delete rule: Undo, then 30 days) and are then permanently deleted. Exception: a file that was already Accepted or submitted stays in history as evidence. Client uploads always add, never replace. Resolves AG-09 open questions 29(a) and 30.
- **AG19-D19 — Upload to an Accepted line (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09. Epics affected: AG-09, AG-15, CP-01. The file simply attaches as a part. The status does not change, nothing is blocked, nothing is re-checked automatically. The line shows the extra file; staff decide with Add, Replace or Remove. Principle: ExpatReady facilitates, the firm decides (AG16-76). Resolves AG-09 open question 29(b).
- **AG19-D20 — Virus-flagged file (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09. Epics affected: AG-09, AG-19. A Quarantined part cannot be opened, combined in Studio or accepted: a safety rule, not a judgement. Other parts on the line work normally. The line can be accepted only when no part is Quarantined; staff Remove the Quarantined part first and can ask for a resend. *(Wording corrected later on 23 Oct by AG19-D31: staff do not need to ask; the client is asked automatically, AG-9-55.)*
- **AG19-D21 — A ZIP is files attached one by one (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09. Epics affected: AG-09, AG-07, AG-19, 99-Phasing. No special ZIP rule: a ZIP is unpacked and scanned, and each file follows the normal path. An upload answering one request (a reply to that request email, that line's upload link) becomes parts of that line. An upload answering no specific request goes to the case's new "To file" list; staff multi-select and click "File to line". Changes AG-9-54. The To file list and bulk "File to line" are new; AG-9-47 (unmatched import files) is unchanged. Resolves AG-09 open question 29(c).
- **AG19-D22 — Plans (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19. Epics affected: AG-19, SA-03 (note), 99-Phasing. PDF Studio is on every plan, including the cheapest. Resolves AG-19 Q8.
- **AG19-D23 — Entry points (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19. Epics affected: AG-19, AG-09 (pointer, AG-9-66), 99-Phasing. One PDF Studio: a top-level main-menu item (no "Tools" group), plus "Open in PDF Studio" inside every record's documents (case, prospect, corporate case) and on Binder lines, saving back to that record. The exact menu position is set when the main menu is specified. Gap flagged for the product owner: no epic owns the staff main menu. Resolves AG-19 Q12.
- **AG19-D24 — Release 2 list (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19. Epics affected: AG-19, 99-Phasing. PDF to Word, Excel, PowerPoint and JPG; PowerPoint and Excel to PDF; Translate PDF; AI summarizer; PDF to Markdown go on the Release 2 wish-list. Translate keeps the note: machine translation is not a certified translation. Resolves AG-19 Q11.
- **AG19-D25 — Redaction scope (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19. Epics affected: AG-19, 99-Phasing. Release 1 redaction is hand-drawn boxes only (true removal). Find-and-redact (search a value, mark all occurrences) moves to Release 2 with OCR. Resolves AG-19 Q13.
- **AG19-D26 — Technical defaults (the product owner, 23 Oct: accepted the stated defaults).** Home: AG-19. Epics affected: AG-19, AG-09 (permission note). (a) Studio tools need `agent/document:edit`; saving a result as a new document (standalone) also needs `agent/document:create` (existing AG-09 rule). (b) Engine spike pass bar: a 10-page scan under 4 MB, one photo under 1 MB, under 15 s on an ordinary office laptop, every planted test string removed by redaction; a failure returns to the product owner. Resolves AG-19 Q1. (c) Browsers: current Chrome, Edge, Safari and Firefox on laptop and desktop; phone scan on phone browsers. Resolves AG-19 Q10 except the largest session size, which stays open. (d) Session: unsaved Studio work survives a page reload; it is cleared at logout or after 8 hours. Resolves AG-19 Q6. (e) When the pack has no size limit for that document type, Prepare for upload still combines and names, and skips compression. Resolves the first half of AG-19 Q2; the SA-01B name-token half stays open (pending SA-01B knock-on).
- **SCOPE-TRIAGE line 124 (23 Oct).** The "multi-page camera assembly" proposal to move to Release 2 is marked superseded by AG19-D10 (Release 1) in `FINAL-Agent-FR/SCOPE-TRIAGE_2026-10-05.md`, with a dated note; the line's text is kept.

**Decided 23 Oct 2026, later (the product owner approved AG19-D27 – AG19-D34: "go").** They answer AG-19 Q14 and AG-19 D2 row 13, AG-09 open questions 33, 34 and 36, and parts of AG-09 open questions 32, 35 and 37. Applied on 23 Oct in AG-19 v1.2, AG-09 v1.17, AG-07 v3.6, AG-02 v2.2, AG-10 v1.19, AG-16 v1.22, 00-Overview v1.20 and 99-Phasing-and-Glossary v1.14. Still open: AG-19 Q5 (unlock), Q7 (fillable and signed PDFs in Combine), Q9 (desktop-to-phone handoff) and Q15 (XFA edition detection).

- **AG19-D27 — Nothing from Studio is kept in browser storage (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19. Epics affected: AG-19. Nothing from PDF Studio is kept in the browser's storage, as for the form viewer (AG07-D28(7)). If staff close or reload the page with unsaved Studio work, the browser warns "You have unsaved changes – leave anyway?". A reload loses the edits; the originals are always safe on the record. **Supersedes AG19-D26(d).** Resolves AG-19 Q14. Found while writing: browsers show their own fixed wording on close and reload (AG-19 Q16).
- **AG19-D28 — Restoring a redacted version's sources (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 (the restore rule, AG-9-65 rule 8). Epics affected: AG-09, AG-19. The sources of a redacted version can be restored as current, after the warning "This brings back the unredacted file." that staff confirm. Only holders of `agent/document:delete` (the people who can open the unredacted original, AG19-D17) can restore it. The restore is audited. Resolves AG-09 open question 33. Found while writing: who sees the restored file (AG-09 open question 38).
- **AG19-D29 — Evidence until submissions are recorded (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-67 rule 4). Epics affected: AG-09, AG-07. Until AG-07 records which files went into a submission (AG-07 D9 Q55), only Accepted files are kept as evidence after Replace or Remove. "Submitted" protection starts when that record exists. AG-07 D9 Q55 and AG-09 open question 36 point to each other. Resolves AG-09 open question 36 for now.
- **AG19-D30 — Removing the last part (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-67 rule 5). Epics affected: AG-09, AG-19. Removing the last part on a line sets it back to Needed. No automatic message goes to the client. Resolves AG-09 open question 32(b); 32(a) and 32(c) stay open. *(Home: the brief's default was AG-19; the status rule lives in AG-09's card, so it is placed there under AG16-11.)*
- **AG19-D31 — Infected file (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-55, SA8-D2). Epics affected: AG-09, AG-19. SA8-D2 / AG-9-55 stands: the client is asked automatically to upload again; this is a safety notice, not a chase. **Corrects AG19-D20's wording** "staff can Remove it and ask for a resend": staff do not need to ask. Resolves AG-09 open question 35(c); 35(a) and 35(b) stay open. *(Home placed in AG-09, as for D30.)*
- **AG19-D32 — Email replies to a request (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-68, AG-9-62). Epics affected: AG-09, AG-10, AG-07. A reply to a request email that asked for several documents puts its attachments on the To file list; each shows the requested lines as one-click targets ("File to: Passport / Pay stubs / Bank statement"). A reply to a request for a single document lands straight on that line as parts. No filing by guesswork (AG16-44). Settles AG-9-62 against AG19-D21: AG-9-62's one-click "Mark request as received?" applies only to emails and WhatsApp media that are not a reply to a request email. Resolves AG-09 open question 37(a) and (d).
- **AG19-D33 — A To file list on every record (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-68). Epics affected: AG-09, AG-02, AG-07. The To file list exists on every record that holds documents: prospect, client and case. A corporate case is a case with client type Corporate (AG-09 §3.0; AG17-D4, D5), so it is covered. On conversion prospect → client or case, To file items carry over with the history (AG-02). Resolves AG-09 open question 37(b). *Applied as AG-02's rule (AG-2-47 rule 7): history is never re-parented; the items stay on the person with the prospect's tag and show on the client and the case. Found while writing: whether a lead has a To file list (AG-09 open question 39).*
- **AG19-D34 — Keys and the "Remove pages" label (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 for the keys (AG-9-67, AG-9-68 field 8); AG-19 for the label (AG-19-3 rule 2, AG-19-8 rule 5). Epics affected: AG-09, AG-19, AG-07, 99-Phasing, SA-06 (note; not applied in this pass). Replace, Remove and File to line need only `agent/document:edit`: the 30-day undo makes Remove safe, and Accepted files stay protected. The PDF Studio toolbar page button is labelled **"Remove pages"**; "Remove" alone always means taking a file off a line. Resolves AG-09 open question 34 and AG-19 D2 row 13. Found while writing: AG16-104 restores from Recently deleted with the delete key (AG-09 open question 40, AG-16 open question 29).

**Decided 23 Oct 2026, later still (the product owner approved AG19-D35 – AG19-D49: "yes").** They answer AG-19 open questions Q2 (the rest), Q5, Q7, Q9, Q15 and Q16; AG-09 open questions 32 (a) and (c), 35 (a) and (b), 37 (c), 38, 39, 40 and 41; AG-16 open question 29; AG-10 open question 41; and, for the SA writer, SA-06 Q33, SA1B-Q15 and SA1B-Q16. Applied on 23 Oct in AG-19 v1.3, AG-09 v1.18, AG-02 v2.3, AG-10 v1.20, AG-12 v2.8, AG-16 v1.23, CP-01 v0.5, 00-Overview v1.21 and 99-Phasing-and-Glossary v1.15; SA-03, SA-06 and SA-01B are applied by the SA writer. Raised while writing (not decided here): AG-19 Q17 – Q19; AG-09 open questions 42 – 45. As for D30 and D31, a status or lifecycle rule is placed in AG-09 (single home, AG16-11), and each entry names its home.

- **AG19-D35 — Restore from Recently deleted with the edit key (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-67 rule 10). Epics affected: AG-09, AG-16, AG-19, SA-06. Holders of `agent/document:edit` can see and restore, within 30 days, files they themselves removed or replaced. Holders of `agent/document:delete` still see and restore all. Resolves AG-09 open question 40, AG-16 open question 29 and SA-06 Q33 (SA writer).
- **AG19-D36 — A restored unredacted file is an ordinary file (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-65 rule 8). Epics affected: AG-09, AG-19. A restored unredacted file (AG19-D28) has normal visibility, the client portal included if the line is shown there. The restore warning reads: "This brings back the unredacted file. The client will be able to see it." Resolves AG-09 open question 38.
- **AG19-D37 — Leave warning on close and reload (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19 (AG-19-3 rule 12). Epics affected: AG-19. On a browser close or reload, the browser's own fixed warning applies. ExpatReady's own message appears only when staff leave Studio within the app. Accepted limitation. Resolves AG-19 Q16.
- **AG19-D38 — Prepare for upload naming (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19 (AG-19-17 rules 5 – 5b). Epics affected: AG-19, 99-Phasing (key term), SA-01B (SA writer). The pattern pieces are document type, person name, case number and date (e.g. `Pay-stubs_Ravi-Sharma_2026-11-12.pdf`). With no pattern in the pack, the name is `<document type>_<person>.pdf`. 1 MB = 1,000,000 bytes. Resolves the rest of AG-19 Q2 and SA1B-Q15 (SA writer). Found while writing: which date the date piece is, and whether the example's separators are the rule (AG-19 Q17).
- **AG19-D39 — XFA refusal with no button (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19 (AG-19-5). Epics affected: AG-19, AG-09 (AG-9-66), 00-Overview, 99-Phasing, SA-01B (SA writer). **Replaces the conditional [Open in form viewer] button of AG19-D16** (the rest of D16 stands). Message: "This is an interactive government form. It can't be combined or changed. It stays on the checklist as its own file. To fill forms, use the case's Forms tab." "Forms tab" is AG-07's own name for the Filler panel (`FINAL-Agent-FR/AG-07-case-core-and-delivery_v3.6_2026-10-23.md`, Chapter 5 "Screens": "Filler panel on the case (the Forms tab)"); the decision's wording "Forms section" was replaced by it, as the decision asked. No regulator names. No pack matching data is needed. Resolves AG-19 Q15 and SA1B-Q16 (SA writer).
- **AG19-D40 — A lead's To file list (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-68 rules 2b – 2c). Epics affected: AG-09, AG-02 (AG-2-75 rule 13). A lead also gets a To file list. It carries over on conversion, as AG19-D33: at Engage the items stay on the person with the lead's tag and show on the prospect, then on the client and the case. Resolves AG-09 open question 39.
- **AG19-D41 — No AI suggestion on the To file list (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-68 rule 11). Epics affected: AG-09, AG-10. No Sorter suggestion on the To file list. Resolves AG-09 open question 41 and AG-10 open question 41.
- **AG19-D42 — No plan-catalogue row for PDF Studio (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: SA-03 (applied by the SA writer). Epics affected: SA-03; AG-19 records it (D1, D3). PDF Studio has no row in the plan feature catalogue: it is always included, like the Filler. Recorded only in this pass; no Agent text changed (AG-19 BR-14 already says every plan).
- **AG19-D43 — A staff change on an Accepted line (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-67 rule 5; AG-9-26 rule 7). Epics affected: AG-09, AG-19 (pointer). Staff Replace or Remove of a file on an Accepted line returns it to Received. Resolves AG-09 open question 32(a).
- **AG19-D44 — Accept only when every part is clean (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-26 rule 5; AG-9-65 rule 5). Epics affected: AG-09, AG-19 (AG-19-18 rule 5). Accept is available only when every part is clean: not Checking, not Scan unavailable, not Quarantined. Resolves AG-09 open question 32(c).
- **AG19-D45 — Restoring a replaced file (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-67 rule 10). Epics affected: AG-09, AG-19 (pointer). Restoring a replaced file swaps it back into its old place; the newer file goes to Recently deleted with its own 30-day undo. Resolves AG-09 open question 35(a). Found while writing: a newer file that was Accepted since (AG-09 open question 42).
- **AG19-D46 — An infected file is never restored (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-67 rule 7; §3.6). Epics affected: AG-09, AG-19 (pointer). A removed Quarantined (infected) file can never be restored; it follows its own disposal schedule (AG-09 §3.6). Applied as: it does not enter Recently deleted. Resolves AG-09 open question 35(b).
- **AG19-D47 — Junk on the To file list (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-68 rule 10). Epics affected: AG-09, AG-19, 99-Phasing (key term). Junk in the To file list is removed with the normal Remove (bulk, Recently deleted, 30-day undo). Resolves AG-09 open question 37(c).
- **AG19-D48 — Unlock, Combine of fillable and signed files, desk-to-phone scan (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19 (AG-19-13, AG-19-7, new AG-19-20). Epics affected: AG-19, 00-Overview, 99-Phasing. (a) Unlock runs on the device if a permissive library works, else on ExpatReady's own server. A file that opens without a password but forbids changes is unlocked automatically. A password-to-open file always needs the real password; no cracking. Resolves AG-19 Q5. (b) Combine flattens fillable (non-government) form boxes so the values stay visible. A digitally signed file shows "The digital signature won't survive combining – the original stays on file." Warn, never block. Resolves AG-19 Q7. (c) The desktop-to-phone QR hand-off is in Release 1: Studio shows a QR code; staff scan it with their own phone; pages appear in the open Studio session, through a secure short-lived link tied to that staff user and session. Resolves AG-19 Q9. Undecided technical points (link lifetime, phone sign-in, server relay, logging) are written as proposed defaults marked for confirmation (AG-19 Q18). Found while writing: (a)'s server fallback does not exist for the client-side unlock of AG19-D49 (AG-19 Q19).
- **AG19-D49 — Password-protected uploads (the product owner, 23 Oct: approved; new client-facing automation approved under principle 1, AG16-76).** Home: AG-09 for the upload behaviour (new AG-9-69, with AG-9-7); AG-19 for the unlock engine (AG-19-13). Epics affected: AG-09, AG-19, AG-10 (the automatic email and its link), AG-12 (the row on the Automatic messages page), CP-01 (the portal prompt, CP-1-97), 99-Phasing (key term), SA-05 (template wording, pending, SA writer). (1) On the no-login upload link and in the client portal, before upload, the page detects a password-protected PDF and asks "This file is password protected. Enter the password so your advisor can open it." with [Unlock] and [Upload as is]. Unlock happens on the client's device; only the unlocked copy is uploaded; the password is never sent, stored or logged. A wrong password → try again. "Upload as is" → the file arrives locked with a "Password protected" label for staff. A file that only forbids changes is unlocked automatically without asking. (2) Email reply with a locked attachment: ONE automatic email to the client, "One file is password protected – tap here to unlock it", with a no-login link showing the same prompt. The product owner approved this as client-facing automation (principle 1 requires his approval). It is a fix-it notice like SA8-D2, not a chase. When the client unlocks through the link, the unlocked copy replaces the locked file, and the locked file goes to Recently deleted (a later exception to AG19-D18's "client uploads always add"). (3) Staff always see the "Password protected" label and can unlock in Studio (AG-19-13). Found while writing: AG-09 open questions 43 – 45 (which emails and how many; staff acting first or an Accepted locked file; who sees the client-replaced file in Recently deleted). *(25 Oct: narrowed by AG19-D50 — the prompt only where the client uploads inside ExpatReady; part (2), the automatic unlock email and the client replace, withdrawn; the server fallback of AG19-D51 applies to part (1).)*

**Decided 25 Oct 2026 (the product owner approved AG19-D50 – AG19-D56: "yes").** They answer AG-19 open questions Q17, Q18 and Q19; AG-09 open questions 42 – 45; and, for SA-01B, SA1B-Q17. Applied on 25 Oct in AG-19 v1.4, AG-09 v1.20, AG-08 v1.14, AG-10 v1.23, AG-12 v2.10, AG-15 v1.14, AG-16 v1.24, CP-01 v0.6, SA-01B v1.11, 99-Phasing-and-Glossary v1.17 and 00-Overview v1.22. Raised while writing (not decided here): AG-09 open question 46.

- **AG19-D50 — D49 narrowed; the automatic unlock email withdrawn (the product owner, 25 Oct: approved).** **Narrows AG19-D49 and withdraws AG19-D49(2).** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-69). Epics affected: AG-09, AG-19, AG-08, AG-10, AG-12, AG-15, CP-01, 99-Phasing (key term). The client password prompt ([Unlock] / [Upload as is], unlock before upload, the password never stored or logged; a file that only forbids changes is unlocked automatically) appears ONLY where the client uploads inside ExpatReady: the client portal Documents tab, the Smart Questionnaire's upload (AG-08; the questionnaire has no upload question type, SA-1B-3, so this is its document-request card, AG-8-18) and the no-login upload page (AG-9-7). A locked file that arrives as an EMAIL attachment just gets the "Password protected" label; staff unlock it in PDF Studio (AG-19-13). The automatic unlock email of AG19-D49(2), its no-login unlock link, the client's unlocked copy replacing the locked file, and the "Password-protected file — unlock" row on the Automatic messages page (AG-12) are WITHDRAWN (the product owner: too much engineering). The SA-05 wording for that email is no longer needed. A client upload always adds, with no exception (AG19-D18). Resolves AG-09 open questions 43, 44 and 45: no locked copy reaches ExpatReady in the prompt flow unless the client chose Upload as is, and the email path has no client unlock. *(25 Oct, later: widened by AG19-D58 — the prompt shows in every place a client uploads inside ExpatReady, portal message attachments included.)*
- **AG19-D51 — Client unlock fallback (the product owner, 25 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19 (AG-19-1 rule 6, AG-19-13 rule 8) with AG-09 (AG-9-69 rule 3). Epics affected: AG-19, AG-09, CP-01, 00-Overview. If the build trial (AG-19-1) shows the client's browser cannot unlock the file, the file is unlocked on ExpatReady's own server: the password is sent once over the secure connection, used in memory to unlock, then discarded — never stored, never logged. The client experience is identical. AG-19-1's pass bar must test the client-side unlock; a fail selects this path and does not go back to the product owner. Resolves AG-19 Q19.
- **AG19-D52 — Restore into an Accepted line (the product owner, 25 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-67 rule 10). Epics affected: AG-09, AG-19 (pointer). Restoring a replaced file (AG19-D45 swap) when the line or the newer file is Accepted counts as a staff Replace: the swap happens, the Accepted newer file stays in the history as evidence (AG19-D29, AG-9-67 rule 4) instead of Recently deleted, and the line returns to Received (AG19-D43). Resolves AG-09 open question 42. Found while writing: AG-09 open question 46 (an Accepted line whose newer file was itself added after the Accept, where AG19-D54 would keep it Accepted). *(25 Oct, later: clarified by AG19-D57 — applies only when the displaced newer file was itself accepted.)*
- **AG19-D53 — Infected files and Recently deleted (the product owner, 25 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-67 rule 7, AG-9-55). Epics affected: AG-09, AG-16, AG-19 (pointer). A removed Quarantined (infected) file never appears in Recently deleted; staff see "Removed — infected file" on the line (AG-9-55). Confirms the writer's reading of AG19-D46.
- **AG19-D54 — Extra file after acceptance (the product owner, 25 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-67 rule 5). Epics affected: AG-09, AG-19 (pointer). Removing a file that was added AFTER the line was Accepted (AG19-D19) keeps the line Accepted, because the accepted files are unchanged. AG19-D43 (back to Received) applies only when a file that was part of the accepted set is replaced or removed. Corrects AG-9-67 rule 5 (and the AG-9-26 rule 7 example). *(25 Oct, later: clarified by AG19-D57 — restores follow the same rule.)*
- **AG19-D55 — File naming details (the product owner, 25 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19 (AG-19-17 rule 5a). Epics affected: AG-19, SA-01B (SA-1B-106). `{date}` = the day staff run Prepare for upload, as YYYY-MM-DD. Separators always: `_` between pieces, `-` for spaces inside a piece. A piece with no value is left out (e.g. `{caseNumber}` outside a case) along with its separator (default, approved in package: Claude's default, approved by the product owner as part of the package). Staff can edit the proposed name before saving. Resolves AG-19 Q17 and SA1B-Q17.
- **AG19-D56 — QR hand-off defaults approved (the product owner, 25 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19 (AG-19-20). Epics affected: AG-19. (a) The code works 10 minutes from when shown, and stops earlier when staff click Stop or the Studio session ends; (b) one phone per code; (c) no phone sign-in — the code is the authority; (d) pages pass through ExpatReady's own server only and are not kept there; (e) "code shown" and "code used" are logged with user and time, never content. Resolves AG-19 Q18.

**Decided 25 Oct 2026, later (the product owner approved AG19-D57 – AG19-D60: "yes").** They answer AG-09 open question 46. Applied on 25 Oct in AG-09 v1.21, AG-19 v1.5, SA-01B v1.12, CP-01 v0.7 and AG-10 v1.24; 00-Overview v1.23 updates its pointer only. Raised while writing (not decided here): AG-09 open question 47.

- **AG19-D57 — Restores follow the accepted set (the product owner, 25 Oct: approved).** **Clarifies AG19-D52 and AG19-D54.** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-67). Epics affected: AG-09, AG-19 (pointer). Only changes to files that were part of the accepted set reset a line to Received, and this includes restores. AG19-D52 applies only when the newer file being displaced was itself accepted. If that newer file was added after acceptance (AG19-D19), the line stays Accepted (AG19-D54), and the newer file goes to Recently deleted as in AG19-D45. Closes AG-09 open question 46.
- **AG19-D58 — The password prompt in every client upload place (the product owner, 25 Oct: approved).** **Widens AG19-D50.** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-69). Epics affected: AG-09, AG-19 (pointer), CP-01 (portal messages, CP-1-97, CP-1-67), AG-10 (portal message attachments, one-line pointer). AG-08 and AG-15 need no change: their pointers do not list the places. The client password prompt (AG-9-69) appears in EVERY place a client uploads inside ExpatReady: the questionnaire's document-request card, the portal Documents tab (including documents requested separately from the questionnaire), the no-login upload page, and portal message attachments. It is stated as one rule on the shared client upload component, so future client upload places inherit it. Only email attachments get the "Password protected" label instead (AG19-D50). Found while writing: AG-09 open question 47 (where a portal message attachment uploaded as is, and its label, land).
- **AG19-D59 — `{documentType}` is the token name (the product owner, 25 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19 (AG-19-17). Epics affected: AG-19, SA-01B (SA-1B-106). The file-name piece for the document type is named `{documentType}` everywhere. SA-01B's `{document}` is renamed in SA-1B-106 (rules, examples, the refusal message, tests) and in its AG19-D38 and SA1B-Q15 rows; AG-19 already used `{documentType}` except in the Q2 proposal row.
- **AG19-D60 — The message on a used QR code (the product owner, 25 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19 (AG-19-20). Epics affected: AG-19. When a second phone scans an already-used QR code (AG19-D56(b)), the phone shows: "This code has already been used. Ask for a new code on the computer." English in Release 1; French comes with the translation of all screen text (AG16-88), so no French text is written in the epic. AG-19-20 rule 2 and a new test.

**Decided 25 Oct 2026, latest (the product owner approved AG19-D61: "yes").** It answers AG-09 open question 47. Applied on 25 Oct in AG-09 v1.22, AG-10 v1.25 and CP-01 v0.8. The same day, with no decision change, the build paths of AG-19 (v1.6) and of AG-09's PDF Studio cards and sections (v1.22) were cleaned of version tags and history notes (X-1); the removed text is kept word for word in AG-19 D6.1 and AG-09 §14. 00-Overview v1.24 updates its AG-19 pointer only.

- **AG19-D61 — A portal message attachment goes to the To file list (the product owner, 25 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-68 To file list, AG-9-69 label). Epics affected: AG-09, AG-10 (portal messages, one-line pointer), CP-01 (CP-1-67, §3.9, one-line pointer). A file a client attaches to a portal message shows in the conversation AND is placed on the record's To file list. If it is locked and the client chose "Upload as is", it carries the "Password protected" label there (`ToFileItem.passwordProtected`); staff unlock it in PDF Studio and file it. Same as a loose email attachment. Closes AG-09 open question 47. Applied as AG-9-68 rule 2d (the record is the case the message is tagged to, or the client for a "General" message, CP1-D6; route value "portal message") and AG-9-69 rule 6. *(25 Oct, final: the writer's choices are confirmed by the product owner — a "General" portal message attachment goes to the client's To file list; a case-tagged message goes to that case's list; the conversation and the To file item share one stored file (`mediaId`); the route value is "portal message".)*

**Decided 25 Oct 2026, final (the product owner approved AG19-D62 – AG19-D65 and the PDF Studio review fixes: "yes"; "no cuts" — every earlier decision stays, including the swap-back restore of AG19-D45, AG19-D52 and AG19-D57, the accepted-set rule of AG19-D54 and AG19-D57, and the full QR hand-off of AG-19-20; "don't overkill" — gaps are closed with the simplest rule, no new feature or setting).** Applied on 25 Oct in AG-19 v1.7, AG-09 v1.23, AG-07 v3.9 (pointer), CP-01 v0.9, SA-01B v1.13 (pointer), 99-Phasing-and-Glossary v1.18 and 00-Overview v1.25. Values the writer chose are marked "(writer default — confirm)" or "(default — confirm in spike report)" in AG-19 and AG-09. Raised while writing (not decided here): AG-09 open questions 49 and 50.

- **AG19-D62 — A Studio save keeps every unused part (the product owner, 25 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-65 rule 6). Epics affected: AG-09, AG-19 (AG-19-6 rule 2, AG-19-5 rule 6, AG-19-13 rule 1, AG-19-18 rule 1). On a PDF Studio save to a Document, every part NOT used in the result stays on the line as its own part, after the result: a refused government form, a part not loaded (still Checking), a part not selected, a locked part skipped, a part that arrived while Studio was open. Nothing disappears silently. Test: 3 stubs + 1 government form → version 2 holds the combined stubs and the form. Fixes review finding B1.
- **AG19-D63 — Open in PDF Studio on every To file item; "Save and file to line" (the product owner, 25 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19 (AG-19-4 ways in, AG-19-6 save targets) and AG-09 (AG-9-68 To file list). Epics affected: AG-19, AG-09 (AG-9-66, AG-9-68, AG-9-69 rule 7), 99-Phasing (key terms). Every To file item offers "Open in PDF Studio". After unlocking or any edit, staff choose the line and save in one step: "Save and file to line". The item leaves the To file list; the "Password protected" label clears. AG19-D61 is also added to AG-19's decisions table, the AG-19-2 pointer and the A2 term "To file list". Fixes review finding B2.
- **AG19-D64 — No automatic unlock of government forms or signed PDFs on a client upload (the product owner, 25 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-69 rule 5). Epics affected: AG-09, AG-19 (A2 Unlock, AG-19-2 pointer), CP-01 (CP-1-97). On a client upload, the automatic removal of "forbids changes" restrictions is SKIPPED for interactive government forms and digitally signed PDFs: they upload as the original, untouched. If they need unlocking, staff do it in PDF Studio, where the original is kept as a source. The password prompt still works for ordinary locked files. Not decided here: a password-to-open file that, once opened, turns out to be signed or a government form (AG-09 open question 49, with the simplest option). Fixes review finding B3.
- **AG19-D65 — PDF Studio on every record that holds documents (the product owner, 25 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19 (AG-19-4, AG-19-6). Epics affected: AG-19, AG-09 (AG-9-66 pointer), 00-Overview (AG-19 scope row). "Open in PDF Studio" and the Studio save targets cover every record that holds documents: lead, prospect, client, case, corporate case. Fixes the AG19-D8 and AG19-D23 wording in AG-19's Part A. Fixes review finding S19.


**Decided 25 Oct 2026, after the final block (the product owner approved AG19-D66 – AG19-D69).** They answer AG-09 open questions 49 and 50 and AG-19 D2 rows 18 and 19. Applied on 25 Oct in AG-19 v1.8, AG-09 v1.24, CP-01 v0.10, SA-01B v1.14 (pointer), 99-Phasing-and-Glossary v1.19 and 00-Overview v1.26. Raised while writing (not decided here): AG-19 Q20 (how a government form that needs a password to open is recognised, since the server cannot set `isXfaForm` on a locked file).

- **AG19-D66 — Government forms are never altered anywhere (the product owner, 25 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19 (AG-19-5 rule 8). Epics affected: AG-19, AG-09 (AG-9-69 rules 5a, 7), 00-Overview (AG-19 row), 99-Phasing (key term Password protected). Interactive government forms are never altered: not by the client-side unlock (AG19-D64), not in PDF Studio (AG-19-5). They are filled on the case's Forms tab. Only digitally signed PDFs are unlocked by staff in Studio, with the original kept as a source. Resolves AG-19 D2 row 18.
- **AG19-D67 — A Studio save on extra files keeps a line Accepted (the product owner, 25 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-26 rule 7). Epics affected: AG-09 (AG-9-26, AG-9-65 rule 6, §3.4, BR-5, §6, §9), AG-19 (AG-19-6 rule 5, AG-19-2). A PDF Studio save on an Accepted line that uses only files added after the Accept keeps the line Accepted. Same principle as AG19-D57: only changes to accepted files reset a line. The "Studio save → Received" wording based on AG19-D7 is now conditional. Resolves AG-09 open question 50 and AG-19 D2 row 19.
- **AG19-D68 — A locked PDF that turns out to be signed or a government form (the product owner, 25 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-69 rule 5a). Epics affected: AG-09, CP-01 (CP-1-97 pointer), AG-19 (AG-19-13 rule 10 pointer), 99-Phasing. The client types the password of a password-to-open PDF. If, once opened, it is digitally signed, it is unlocked anyway: the signature is lost, and the client's upload is the unlocked copy. If it is an interactive government form, it is NOT unlocked: the original uploads as is with the "Password protected" label (consistent with AG19-D66). Resolves AG-09 open question 49. Found while writing: AG-19 Q20 (above).
- **AG19-D69 — Writer defaults approved (the product owner, 25 Oct: approved).** Home: each card below. Epics affected: AG-19, AG-09, SA-01B (pointer), CP-01 (none marked). Every value or rule marked as a writer default for confirmation in AG-19 v1.7, AG-09 v1.23, CP-01 v0.9 and SA-01B v1.13 is approved and the marks are removed from the build path. **AG-19:** AG-19-4 rule 5 (no button when every part is a government form); AG-19-5 field 7 (`ToFileItem.isXfaForm`); AG-19-6 rule 6 (audit summary verbs), rule 11 (default result name `<base>_<tool>.pdf`), rule 12 (`studioTool` values and verbs); AG-19-13 rule 1 (Skip this file, "Not included: locked file"); AG-19-16 rule 8 (a source from another Document is not hidden); AG-19-17 rule 5a (empty `{person}` / `{documentType}` left out; the character rule); AG-19-18 rule 1 (count of parts that are not government forms); AG-19-20 rule 4 (the relay; phone binding), rule 6 (how the server knows the session ended), rule 6a (expiry during a scan, "This code has expired. Ask for a new code on the computer."), field 7 (hand-off link fields). **AG-09:** AG-9-26 rule 4 (Restore source files sends no "Document received"); AG-9-65 rule 7 (restore order: source parts, then the other parts), rule 8 (a source from another document); AG-9-68 field 7 (`ToFileItem.isXfaForm`). **Spike-dependent values** keep the plain note "confirmed in the AG-19-1 report": compression levels Standard 150 dpi at quality 75 and Strong 100 dpi at quality 60 (AG-19-1 rule 6); phone bar C5 (AG-19-1 rule 5); largest session 200 pages or 200 MB (AG-19-3 rule 14, A5, C5 rule 3; AG-19 Q10 resolved); "Already small" under 50 % of the limit (AG-19-15 rule 7). CP-01 v0.9 and SA-01B v1.13 had no mark in their build paths.

**Decided 25 Oct 2026, later (the product owner approved AG19-D70).** It answers AG-19 Q20 and AG-19 D2 row 20. Applied on 25 Oct in AG-19 v1.9, AG-09 v1.25, CP-01 v0.11 and 00-Overview v1.27 (pointer only).

- **AG19-D70 — A government form that needs a password to open (the product owner, 25 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-19 (AG-19-5 rule 7). Epics affected: AG-19 (AG-19-5 rule 7, fields 7, 9, 11, 12; AG-19-13 rules 5, 10, tests; AG-19-2; A2; C3), AG-09 (AG-9-65 field 7, AG-9-66, AG-9-68 field 7, AG-9-69 rules 5a, 7, 8, field 7, tests, edge cases; §7), CP-01 (CP-1-97 pointer). Builds on AG19-D68 (a locked government form the client opens uploads as is, still locked). (1) **Client upload component** (portal Documents tab, questionnaire card, no-login upload page, portal message): the page has already opened the file with the client's password, so it sends `isXfaForm` = yes with the upload; the server stores it; "Open in PDF Studio" is hidden on that part or To file item. (2) **Email attachment** (the server cannot read it): `isXfaForm` stays unknown, and "Open in PDF Studio" shows. When staff open it, Studio asks for the password, recognises the form after opening it, refuses it with the AG-19-5 message, and the stored file stays untouched. Studio then sets `isXfaForm` = yes, so the button is hidden from then on. (3) `isXfaForm` therefore has three values: yes, no, unknown. AG-19-5 rule 7 and field 12 and AG-19-13 rule 5 now say the form is refused after the password is entered, before any change (was: "before unlock is offered"). Writer reading, confirmed by the product owner on 26 Oct (AG19-D73(3)): a locked file the client uploaded as is ("Upload as is") follows (2), because the server cannot read it either (AG-19-5 rule 7(c); AG-9-69 rule 8). Resolves AG-19 D2 row 20 and Q20.

**Decided 26 Oct 2026 (the product owner approved AG19-D71 – AG19-D73 and the final re-check fixes).** Applied on 26 Oct in AG-19 v1.10, AG-09 v1.26, AG-07 v3.10, CP-01 v0.12, 99-Phasing-and-Glossary v1.20 and 00-Overview v1.28 (pointer only); the AG-19 pointer in SA-01B v2.0 updated in place.

- **AG19-D71 — Accept new files (the product owner, 26 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-26 rule 14; AG-9-67 rules 4, 5). Epics affected: AG-09 (AG-9-26, AG-9-65 rule 6 and field 7, AG-9-67, §3.4, §6, §7, §10), AG-19 (AG-19-2, AG-19-6 rule 5, BR-5), AG-07 (AG-7-15 rule 3 and field 8: the Binder shows the status actions). An Accepted line that holds parts with no `acceptedAt` (added after the Accept, or a Studio result kept under AG19-D67/D72) shows **Accept new files (N)**. One click sets `acceptedAt` and accepted-by on those parts; the line stays Accepted throughout. A Studio save that keeps the line Accepted carries each accepted part's `acceptedAt` into the new version's part rows. The submission package rule is unchanged: it uses every part. "Accepted" now means every part either was checked at Accept or was accepted with Accept new files; unchecked new parts are shown with the button. Key: AG-09's existing accept key `agent/document:change-status` (AG-9-26 field 8). Audit: "new files accepted (document, parts, by)".
- **AG19-D72 — One rule for a Studio save on an Accepted line (the product owner, 26 Oct: approved).** Home: AG-09 (AG-9-26 rule 7). Epics affected: AG-09 (AG-9-15, AG-9-26, AG-9-65, §3.4, BR-5, §6, §9), AG-19 (AG-19-6 rule 5 and tests, AG-19-2, A2, A3, BR-5, C2.1). Clarifies AG19-D67. If the result used any file of the accepted set, the line returns to Received. Otherwise (new photos, files from the staff computer, another Document's parts, files added after the Accept) it stays Accepted, and the new result shows under Accept new files. One test replaces the two that read differently.
- **AG19-D73 — Confirmations and the final re-check fixes (the product owner, 26 Oct: approved).** Home: each card named. Epics affected: AG-19, AG-09, AG-07, CP-01, 99-Phasing-and-Glossary. Confirmations: (1) worked examples use the Client E family (Client B client, Client C officer) and the line name "Pay stubs – Client B" everywhere; closes AG-19 D2 row 10. (2) On lead or prospect conversion, To file items stay on the person and show on the new client and case; closes AG-19 D2 row 15. (3) The four AG19-D70 writer readings are accepted: Upload as is follows the email path; an ordinary locked file leaves `isXfaForm` unknown, narrowed by fix N3; no audit for the flag write, superseded by fix M2; a client page's yes is trusted, narrowed by fix N3. Fixes (from the final re-check): N2 the server-fallback unlock runs the same detection: a government form → the unlocked copy is discarded, the original stored locked, `isXfaForm` = yes; a signed PDF → the unlocked copy is kept (AG-9-69 rule 3; AG-19-5 rule 7(b)). N3 a client-sent `isXfaForm` = yes is kept only together with `passwordProtected` = yes; Studio can still open such a file from Add files → a record's documents; when Studio opens a file that is not a form, it sets `isXfaForm` = no (AG-19-5 rule 7(b) – (d); AG-9-65 field 7; AG-9-69 rule 5a). M2 Studio's flag write is the call `setXfaFlag`, key `agent/document:edit`, with an audit entry (AG-19-5 fields 8 – 9; C4). N8 after a Studio save the result part has no label; an unused locked part keeps its label (AG-9-69 rule 7; AG-19 A2; Glossary). M9 "digitally signed" = a PDF with a filled signature field; usage-rights signatures (UR3, DocMDP) alone do not count (AG-19 A2; Glossary; AG-9-69 rules 5, 5a; AG-19-7 rule 7). M11 the QR relay connection may reconnect within 30 seconds before the code is marked Session ended; hiding the QR panel does not close it; it lives while the code is valid (AG-19-20 rules 4, 6). S21 CP-1-39: only an infected file asks the client again; a file the scanner could not check stays Checking (AG-9-55). Wording and pointer fixes with no rule change: N4, N6, N7, M1, M4 – M8, M10.

## Money slice review — decisions (the product owner, from 22 Oct)

AG-05 review on hold (the product owner, 22 Oct: "i want to work on it right now and keep AG5 on hold till this is handled properly … lot of decision may be reversed, changed, enhanced"). Scope: consult → proposal → retainer money terms → deposit and trust → receipts → milestones → invoices → payments → refunds and credits → close-out → reports → import of balances. Gap check and sources: `scratchpad/MONEY-AG06-INTERNAL.md`, `MONEY-CROSS-EPIC.md`, `MONEY-CODE.md`, `MONEY-DOMAIN-RESEARCH.md`, `MONEY-SLICE-MAP.md` (session 6bdc8979). Rules researched: CICC Code of Professional Conduct SOR/2022-128 (ss. 23, 24, 31–36), CICC Client Account Regulation 2021-001; product text never names a regulator or claims compliance (Rule 7). Prefix MS-Dn; each decision names its Home and Epics affected.

- **MS-D1 — A simple trust ledger is Release 1 (the product owner, 22 Oct: "a").** Home: AG-06. Epics affected: AG-06, AG-05, AG-07, AG-14, AG-15, AG-16, AG-18, CP-01, SA-05, SA-06, Agent 00-Overview, 99-Phasing, DEV-TASKS. Replaces AG-06 §3.5 "trust-adjacent records, not a trust system" (AG-6-24, BR-13 trust part) and the "full trust accounting Phase-2/3" deferral. Release 1 has: a per-client trust balance; a trust request (not an invoice); a trust receipt; "Pay from trust" on an invoice, never more than the client's balance; refund from trust; a home-screen item when invoiced trust money has not been moved after 30 days; a monthly check (staff type the bank's trust balance, ExpatReady compares it with the total of client balances and saves the result with date and person); a per-client trust ledger and export. A firm with no trust account turns trust off once and invoices after the work only. Not in Release 1: bank statement import, bank feeds, three-way reconciliation journals (option b, declined). ExpatReady never holds money (BR-13 stays).
- **MS-D2 — No separate proposal; the retainer is sent and stays editable until the client signs (the product owner, 22 Oct: "why not send retainer and if client ask to change the firm can edit and resend … as long as a retainer is not signed shouldn't it be editable?", then "a").** Home: AG-05. Epics affected: AG-05, AG-04, AG-02, AG-12, SA-05, SA-06, 00-Overview, 99-Phasing, DEV-TASKS (FE). (1) The Proposal document, "Create proposal", the "Here is your proposal" email (SA-05 A.1 #6, #7), `ConsultRecord.proposalId` and the frontend Quote tab are withdrawn; replaces AG-5-59 and AG04-D7's "Engage and create proposal" / "Create proposal" (now "Engage and prepare retainer" on a lead, "Prepare retainer" on a prospect, pre-filled from the consult record). (2) A sent retainer is editable while it is SENT or VIEWED (not after the client signs): an edit creates a new version, the old signing link stops working at once ("This agreement was updated. Please use the latest email."), and one "Updated agreement — please sign this version" email goes to the client; no "Agreement withdrawn" email for an edit. Every version is kept and readable (who, what, when; which version was signed). Replaces void-then-re-issue for corrections (AG-5-34/35 stay for a true withdrawal). (3) After the client signs, changes need a new signature (amendment, MS question 5). (4) The firm may treat the signing request as the draft the rules expect; firm's call (Rule 7). A quick fee note uses an ordinary library email.
- **MS-D3 — Consultation agreement on by default for first consults, free and paid (the product owner, 22 Oct: "a").** Home: AG-04 (AG-4-15). Epics affected: AG-04, AG-02, AG-09, AG-12, SA-05, DEV-TASKS. Changes AG-4-15 rule 1 (tick off by default). (1) The per-type tick "Client must accept our consultation agreement" starts on for types with category Free consult and Paid consult; off for Follow-up, Case review and Other; the firm may switch it off per type. (2) Public booking page: the tick, as built in the spec (accepted version and time on the booking). (3) Staff-made booking: the confirmation email carries a no-login "Read and accept the consultation agreement" link; the booking shows "Agreement accepted <time>" or "Not accepted yet" (a warning, never a block, Rule 7). (4) The accepted copy (that version, with time) is emailed to the client and filed on the person (AG-09). The wording is the firm's own document template (MAP:72). The product text names no regulator.
- **MS-D4 — The retainer holds structured money terms (the product owner, 23 Oct: "a").** Home: AG-05. Epics affected: AG-05, AG-04, AG-06, AG-07, AG-12, SA-05, DEV-TASKS. Replaces the free-text `feeSchedule` (AG-05 §7) and AG-06's unbacked `paymentMilestone[]` / `isPaymentRequired` / `paymentAmount` (AG-06 §7). A **Fees** panel on the retainer holds: services with fees; fee type Fixed (default) or Hourly (rate × estimated hours); schedule rows (label + amount), the first row "Deposit on signing"; a disbursement estimate (lines); tax from the firm's settings. Refund policy and payment terms stay the firm's clause text. Pre-filled from the consult record (services, quoted fee); a warning, never a block, when the schedule does not add up to the fee. The rows drive the deposit trust request (deposit row) and the milestone invoices (other rows); billing never retypes them.
- **MS-D5 — "Prepare amendment" on a signed retainer (the product owner, 25 Oct: "a").** Home: AG-05. Epics affected: AG-05, AG-06, AG-07, AG-12, AG-17, SA-05, DEV-TASKS. (1) A signed retainer (e-signed or signed offline) offers "Prepare amendment": a short document linked to it, from the firm's new **Amendment** starter template (Must; SA-05 and AG-12), showing only what changes (services, fees, schedule rows, disbursement estimate). (2) It goes through the same signing flow (AG05-D1), countersign included; it is editable until the client signs (MS-D2). (3) Once signed, the agreed terms are the original plus its signed amendments; new schedule rows join the client's schedule (MS-D4). (4) An invoice that takes the billed total above the agreed total shows "This invoice is above the agreed fees. Prepare an amendment?"; staff may continue (warning, never a block, Rule 7). Closes AG-17's open "amendment if the fee changes" (AG-17:207). The original retainer never changes (BR-7 stays).
- **MS-D6 — Deposit request: an Automatic messages row, "Ask me first" by default (the product owner, 25 Oct: "can we have hybrid?", then "sure").** Home: AG-06. Epics affected: AG-06, AG-05, AG-11, AG-12, AG-15, SA-05, SA-06, CP-01, DEV-TASKS. Replaces the automatic deposit invoice at signing (AG-06:216, 228, 411, 477; AG-6-32). (1) When a retainer is signed (e-signed or signed offline; both start it), a **Deposit request** pre-filled from the signed retainer's deposit row (MS-D4) is prepared. It is an Automatic messages row (AG12-D21) with the choices Send automatically · Ask me first · Off; default **Ask me first**: it waits in Needs you for the person who sent the retainer, one click sends it. A firm may switch the row to Send automatically. Approved by the product owner as a client-facing automatic message under AG16-76. (2) Nothing is prepared when the retainer has no deposit row or the firm has trust turned off (MS-D1); then the deposit row is billed as the first invoice once earned. (3) A deposit request is not an invoice; it has its own reference (DR-…), a no-login pay link and the firm's offline payment details (AG16-92). (4) Payment: online is recorded from the processor fact; offline is recorded by staff or accepted from the bank-notice suggestion (AG16-30). The money goes to the client's trust balance. (5) Receipts: a numbered **trust receipt** for every advance payment and a numbered **payment receipt** naming the invoice for every invoice payment, one receipt number series per firm; both are emailed automatically. Settles AG-06's numbered vs "no series" receipt conflict (AG-06:341, 654 vs AG-6-22).
- **MS-D7 — Where invoice payments go: one choice, set once (the product owner, 25 Oct: "a").** Home: AG-06 (rule); AG-16 places it (Getting paid → Advanced). Epics affected: AG-06, AG-16, AG-14, DEV-TASKS. Getting paid → Advanced: "Invoice payments go to: Operating account (default) · Trust first". Operating: a payment against an invoice is recorded to operating and marks the invoice paid. Trust first: the payment is recorded into the client's trust balance and applied to the invoice in the same step, and the 30-day "move to operating" item applies (MS-D1). Online and offline payments follow the same choice. The Owner sets it; it applies to payments recorded after a change. Shown only when trust is on (MS-D1). The product text names no regulator.
- **MS-D8 — A milestone is billed by a click; "Pay from trust" is pre-ticked (the product owner, 25 Oct: "a").** Home: AG-06. Epics affected: AG-06, AG-05, AG-07, AG-11, AG-14, SA-05, DEV-TASKS. Replaces AG-06's payment-plan "draw mode" that generates invoices on due dates (AG-06 §3.2, AG-6-9/10) and closes AG-06 item 11 (milestone suggestion on a case event): no invoice is created or sent without a person. (1) Each schedule row (MS-D4) on the client's or case's Fees panel has **Bill this**: a draft invoice pre-filled with the row's service line, amount and tax; staff may add lines (e.g. a government fee the firm paid) and send it. (2) A row may carry a date; from that date it shows in **Ready to bill** (a list of unbilled rows for all clients; show, don't push, AG16-42). (3) When the client has a trust balance, the draft pre-ticks "Pay from trust", up to the balance (never more, MS-D1); the amount is applied when the invoice is sent; the rest is due from the client. Staff may untick or lower it. Matches R18 (one invoice per instalment).
- **MS-D9 — Government fees: the firm pays, then bills and reimburses from trust; paying straight from trust waits for the immigration expert (the product owner, 25 Oct: "a").** Home: AG-06. Epics affected: AG-06, AG-07, AG-17, DEV-TASKS. Uses what exists: the case's Government fees section creates the non-taxable government-fee line, billed after the fee is paid (AG17-D13, AG17-D43); "Pay from trust" (MS-D8) reimburses it. No "pay government fee from trust" action and no trust-permission line in Release 1. Reason: the CICC Code (s.32(f) vs s.33(1)) and the Client Account Regulation (s.6.1(a)(i)) read unclearly together; not resolved by assumption. **Open for the immigration expert:** may a disbursement be paid straight from trust with the client's written permission before it is invoiced? If yes, adding it is small (a permission line in the Fees panel and one action).
- **MS-D10 — Card payment for trust deposits through an optional second payment account (the product owner, 25 Oct: "b").** Home: AG-06 (rule); SA-08 (the payment rail); AG-16 places the connection (Connections / Getting paid). Epics affected: AG-06, AG-16, SA-08, SA-03, CP-01, AG-15, DEV-TASKS. (1) A firm may connect a second payment account for its trust bank account (optional; Owner only, as the first, AG16-90). (2) When it is connected, a deposit request (MS-D6) offers "Pay by card"; the client's trust balance is credited the full amount; the processor fee is recorded as the firm's own cost ("Firm's cost: cover from your own funds") and never reduces the client's balance. (3) When it is not connected, a deposit request shows only offline methods (e-Transfer, bank transfer, wire, cheque). (4) Card payment for invoices stays on the firm's main (operating) account. How the firm covers the fee at its bank is the firm's responsibility (Rule 7); the product text names no regulator. Every plan (SA-Q1).
- **MS-D11 — Refunds, corrections, credit notes and write-offs are Release 1 (the product owner, 25 Oct: "accept all").** Home: AG-06. Epics affected: AG-06, AG-04, AG-12, AG-14, AG-15, SA-05, SA-06, CP-01, DEV-TASKS. Replaces AG-06's "refunds Phase-2" (§3.1 lifecycle, §7 `REFUND`) and BR-7's overpayment refusal; closes AG-06 item 12 (write-off). (1) Refund any payment: a card refund through the processor (full or part); an offline refund recorded (amount, date, method, reference; extends AG-6-58); a refund from trust reduces the client's trust balance. Key `agent/refund:create`; a person always confirms. (2) **Reverse entry**: a payment recorded by mistake is reversed with a reason by a holder of the payment-record key; audited, never deleted. (3) **Part credit note** on an issued invoice (amount + reason), besides the full cancel; an issued invoice is never edited. (4) **Write off** a small balance (amount + reason): the invoice is settled and the write-off shows in reports. (5) **Overpayment accepted**: the extra goes to the client's trust balance when trust is on (MS-D1), otherwise to credit for the next invoice; a receipt is sent either way (MS-D6). (6) "Refund issued" and "Credit note" become starter templates (SA-05, AG-12; Must), sent by a person.
- **MS-D12 — Close-out when a case ends (the product owner, 25 Oct: "this is taking care of refund or non invoiced etc all scenario?", then "a+").** Home: AG-06 (money rules); AG-07 shows the item on the case. Epics affected: AG-06, AG-07, AG-11, AG-13, AG-14, AG-15, SA-05, DEV-TASKS. (1) Trigger, when a case closes (any close outcome): the closed case has unbilled work (unbilled schedule rows, unbilled government fees the firm paid), or the client has a trust balance, a credit or an open balance and no other open case. (2) One home-screen item for the person who sent the retainer, dated 30 days after closing ("Settle Client B's money: CAD 600 in trust, by 24 Nov"). (3) **Close out** screen: a final statement PDF (everything received, invoiced, paid from trust, refunded, credited, written off); every unbilled item; a final invoice; "Pay from trust"; refunds of trust money and credit (MS-D11); "Mark settled" with a reason (client unreachable, settled outside ExpatReady). The item clears when nothing is left or it is marked settled. (4) **"Pay from trust" also works on any issued unpaid invoice**, not only at send (extends MS-D8). (5) **"Client disputes this invoice"** on an invoice turns off "Pay from trust" for it until cleared. (6) Never blocks closing the case (Rule 7). A reopened case keeps its history. Product text names no regulator.
- **MS-D13 — Invoicing a client whose retainer is not signed: a warning, not a block (the product owner, 25 Oct: "a"; closes AG02-P1).** Home: AG-06. Epics affected: AG-06, AG-05, AG-07, AG-17, AG-02, DEV-TASKS. Reverses AG17-D79 and the invoice block of AG17-D62 (AG-6-42, BR-17; AG-05 BR-14, AG-5-40 "gate billing on execution"). A new invoice for a client or case whose retainer is neither signed nor signed offline shows "<Client>'s retainer isn't signed. Invoice anyway?"; the person may continue; the choice is recorded with the person and time. Applies to government-fee lines too. The lines are typed by staff (no schedule rows without a signed retainer). Deposit requests still come only from a signed retainer (MS-D6). The case's "Retainer not signed" CRITICAL warning stays (AG-5-51).
- **MS-D14 — Opening balances are Release 1; old invoice history stays Release 2 (the product owner, 25 Oct: "a").** Home: AG-18 (import); AG-06 (what the balances are). Epics affected: AG-18, AG-06, AG-14, DEV-TASKS. Changes AG-18's "Invoices, payment history and opening balances — Release 2" (AG-18 v1.9:194, 216). (1) Per client: an opening **trust balance** (a trust ledger entry dated the go-live day, "Opening balance"), an amount **owed** (one opening-balance item, payable and reminded like an invoice) and a **credit**. (2) Entered through the existing AG-18 import (three columns) or typed on the client ("Add opening balance"). (3) Past invoices and payments stay Release 2; the old system's statement may be uploaded as a document for reference. Imported old invoices, when they come, keep their numbers (AG16-13 rule 15).
- **MS-D15 — Paid consults: money at booking is trust money; the "Attended" click bills it (the product owner, 25 Oct: asked for market and competitor research, then "what if the consultation fees are not refundable irrespective? or other services … where it doesn't come under CICC", then "a" to the simplified version).** Home: AG-04 (booking), AG-06 (money). Epics affected: AG-04, AG-06, AG-02, AG-12, AG-14, SA-05, CP-01, DEV-TASKS. Research: `scratchpad/MONEY-PAID-CONSULT-RESEARCH.md`. Replaces AG-4-44's consult invoice at booking (`Booking.invoiceId` is set at Attended) and the void of an issued invoice on hold expiry.
