(1) Each paid appointment type has one choice, "Client pays: When booking · After the consult". (2) When booking: the payment is a trust payment request (MS-D6 receipts); a trust receipt is emailed; payment confirms the booking. "When booking" is offered only when trust is on (MS-D1) and a trust card account is connected (MS-D10); otherwise the type uses "After the consult", with a nudge "Connect your trust payment account to take card payment when booking". (3) The attendance prompt (AG04-D3) reads "Did Client B attend? Attended (bill CAD 150 from trust) · No-show"; Attended issues the consult invoice and pays it from trust in that click. (4) No-show opens "Keep the fee (bill it from trust) · Refund"; a person chooses; nothing automatic. (5) The cancel rule gains "Never refund" beside "Refund if cancelled up to [4 h] before" (default); an in-time cancel offers "Refund from trust?" (AG04-D15 O2) unless the type says Never. (6) After the consult: the invoice with a pay link is issued at Attended. (7) An expired hold numbers nothing. (8) No per-type or per-service "advance payments go to" choice: trust on/off decides (proposal dropped). Whether a firm may keep a fee on a no-show is the firm's call under its own agreement (Rule 7); listed for the immigration expert. AG-14 paid-consult revenue counts at Attended.
- **MS-D16 — A simple case for a firm service (the product owner, 25 Oct: "how would the flow and setting work?", then "a"; closes AG02-P9).** Home: AG-07 (case kind); AG-06 (Request payment). Epics affected: AG-07, AG-02, AG-05, AG-06, AG-09, AG-13, AG-14, AG-15, AG-18, CP-01, SA-06, DEV-TASKS. (1) "Open case → Firm service: <service>" on a client or prospect (on a prospect it makes the person a client, as Convert does, AG02-D2). Case kind Firm service: no pack programme, no filing, no pack rules, government forms, government-fee table or government deadlines; states Open and Done ("Mark done"). (2) Same case screen: tasks, documents ("Request a document", AG-9-63, no-login upload link), notes, messages, Fees panel (pre-filled from the service price, editable), invoices, trust, close-out (MS-D12). (3) "No retainer needed" by default: no "Retainer not signed" warning and no MS-D13 warning; "Prepare retainer" stays available, and once prepared the normal warnings apply. (4) **Request payment** (new) on any client or case: a trust request when trust is on (MS-D1, MS-D6 receipts); when trust is off, bill after the work. (5) The service may link one existing task template (AG-13-4), applied when the case opens. (6) Case board and AG-14 group these cases by service; the portal shows In progress / Done; AG-18 may create one from an imported matter with no pack programme. A government fee may still be added by hand as an invoice line.
- **MS-D17 (direction) — Optimise the money slice around one Services list (the product owner, 25 Oct: on the proposal "one Services list that drives fees, work and settings, plus one Getting paid page": "lets optimize everything around it.. have a full audit and rerun and then come back").** Home: to be set after the re-audit (proposed AG-16 places the screens; the rules stay in their epics). Direction only: each thing the firm sells (pack programme or firm service) carries its own defaults, used everywhere; one Getting paid page. Detail and any changes to MS-D1 – MS-D16 come back to the product owner after the re-audit (`scratchpad/MONEY-SERVICES-*.md`).
- **MS-D18 — The Services model (the product owner, 25 Oct: "did you see and evaluate end to end?", then "a" to the revised model after the full walk-through).** Home: AG-16 places Settings → Services; AG-02 owns the list (`FirmService` and "Interested in"); AG-05 and AG-07 consume the defaults. Epics affected: AG-02, AG-04, AG-05, AG-06, AG-07, AG-12, AG-13, AG-14, AG-16, AG-17, SA-01B (pack government fees read), SA-06, DEV-TASKS (FE Fee tab becomes Services; Expense tab removed). Design: `scratchpad/MONEY-SERVICES-MODEL.md`, `MONEY-SERVICES-ADVERSARIAL.md` §D, `MONEY-SERVICES-MARKET.md`. (1) One **Settings → Services** list: the pack programmes the firm uses plus its own services; the same list as "Interested in" and the web form. (2) Optional defaults per service, all blank by default: fee type Fixed or Hourly with price (or rate × estimated hours); schedule rows (deposit first); disbursement estimate = the pack's government fees (kept current from the pack until copied) plus the firm's own lines; task template (AG-13-4); retainer template; documents to ask for; **"Needs a signed agreement"** (on by default; when off the retainer warnings do not apply to cases of that service; refines MS-D16(3)). (3) **Copied on use**: preparing a retainer, an amendment or a case copies the defaults; later edits to the service never change sent or signed retainers, open cases or invoices. (4) A quoted fee from the consult record beats the service price; the panel shows the source. (5) Several services on one retainer: one combined schedule, each line tagged with its service. (6) Programme defaults may sit at any level of the pack's programme tree; the closest level wins. (7) **"Save these fees as the default for <service>"** after a retainer, one click. (8) Keys: defaults `agent/billing-config:terms`; names keep AG-02's keys (`agent/prospect:edit` add, `agent/prospect:configure-pipeline` rename or remove). (9) Consult prices stay on the appointment type. (10) Prices pre-fill only in the same currency as the prospect or case (X-6); otherwise labels only. Simplifies MS-D4 (the Fees panel is one component for Services, retainer, amendment and case) and MS-D16(2)(5). Replaces MS-D17 (direction).
- **MS-D19 — Trust is held per case; money before a case is held on the person or company (the product owner, 25 Oct: "a").** Home: AG-06. Epics affected: AG-06, AG-04, AG-05, AG-07, AG-14, AG-17, AG-18, CP-01, DEV-TASKS. Amends MS-D1 ("per-client balance"), MS-D8(3), MS-D12(1) and MS-D14(1). (1) Each case has its own trust balance, in the case's one currency (X-6). (2) Money received before a case exists (a paid consult, a deposit on a prospect, a request on a client with no case) is held on the person or company in that request's currency, and moves to the case when the case opens (recorded). (3) "Pay from trust" uses the invoice's own case, and only money paid by the invoice's Bill-to party; using money held for another case is a deliberate pick ("Use CAD 500 held for Client B – PR?"), recorded with person and time. (4) Every trust receipt records who paid; refunds default to the payer. (5) Close-out (MS-D12) triggers per case: the closed case has unbilled items or a trust, credit or open balance. (6) The person's and company's screens show a total across their cases and pre-case money. (7) Opening trust balances (MS-D14) are entered per case, or on the person when no case is imported.
- **MS-D20 — Trust correctness fixes (the product owner, 25 Oct: "a" = accept all 19).** Home: AG-06 (rules); AG-16 places the settings; SA-08 the processor question. Epics affected: AG-06, AG-01, AG-02, AG-04, AG-05, AG-07, AG-14, AG-16, AG-18, SA-08, CP-01, DEV-TASKS. Source: `scratchpad/MONEY-SERVICES-ADVERSARIAL.md` §A–C. (1) Every payment records "received into: Trust · Operating", set from the route and editable when recording; under "Trust first" invoice card payments go through the trust card account when connected, otherwise they record as operating; mismatches go to the To move list (fixes MS-D7 vs MS-D10(4)). (2) Two offline payment-detail blocks, operating and trust; deposit, trust and paid-consult requests show the trust block; if it is empty the request waits with "Add your trust payment details". (3) **Currency locks at the first money document sent** (a retainer with fees, a deposit request or an invoice); amends X-6 rule 4. (4) Card fees, refunds the processor keeps a fee on, and chargebacks on trust deposits are the firm's own cost, never the client's; the monthly check shows "Not yet covered from your own funds: CAD X". **Open for SA-08:** can the trust processor account bill fees, refunds and disputes to the operating account? (5) Reversing a payment that trust money already spent first undoes those applications (with a preview); a shortfall shows in red on the case, the monthly check and the Owner's Needs you. (6) One **To move** list (applied, not yet moved; fee cover) with a total and one "I moved these" click; one home-screen item only when something is older than 25 days; the monthly check is bank = case balances + waiting to move − fees not covered, per currency. (7) Turning trust off while money is held keeps the trust screens (refund, pay) visible until every balance is zero; a warning, not a block. (8) The deposit and close-out items go to the case lead, or the file owner before a case exists (they follow the AG01-29 handover); amends MS-D6(1) and MS-D12(2). (9) One deposit request per retainer version; "Signed offline" ends the open signing link. (10) A signature on an old version is refused when an edit landed first; an edit after the client signed is refused with "Client B just signed. Prepare an amendment." (11) An amendment below what is already billed offers "Credit note or refund CAD 500?"; an unpaid deposit request offers "Update to the new amount?". (12) "Client disputes this invoice" also offers "Return CAD X to trust" while that money is still waiting to move. (13) Bank-notice matching (AG16-30) also matches deposit and trust requests (DR-… references). (14) A bank notice for more than the balance becomes "Record CAD 1,700 (CAD 200 extra goes to trust or credit)?" (replaces AG16-68's refusal); with trust off the extra is "Refund · Keep as credit", chosen by a person (amends MS-D11(5)). (15) Reports show "Trust received" apart from "Fees collected"; fees count when billed from trust or paid. (16) "Invoice payments go to" shows "Last changed <date> by <person>"; audited. (17) **Trust is asked once, the first time it matters** (a signed retainer with a deposit row, or a paid type set to "When booking"): "Do you hold client money in a trust account? Yes · No"; Owner answers; replaces MS-D1's default-on. (18) Imported amounts owed (MS-D14) start with no due date and send no reminders until staff set one; reference OB-…. (19) An "Attended" clicked by mistake is undone in one action (credit note + return to trust); the trust balance check and the application are one transaction; the consultation agreement and the payment sit on one page, accept first; a card refund refused by the processor offers "Record an offline refund instead".
- **MS-D21 — Consistency fixes (the product owner, 25 Oct: "a" = accept all 20).** Home: AG-06 unless stated. Epics affected: AG-06, AG-01, AG-02, AG-05, AG-07, AG-09, AG-12, AG-13, AG-14, AG-16, SA-05, SA-06, DEV-TASKS. (1) MS-D13 narrowed: on a case that needs a retainer, schedule rows come only from a signed retainer or amendment; a firm-service case whose service does not need an agreement takes rows from the service. (2) "Request payment" on a client or case whose retainer is not signed shows the MS-D13 warning, unless the service does not need an agreement. (3) The deposit row is still billed by "Bill this" and paid from trust; the deposit request only moves money in early, so billed equals agreed. (4) Deposit and payment requests include estimated tax ("CAD 1,130 incl. estimated HST"). (5) When a retainer is signed the prospect shows "Update deal value to CAD 4,500?" (AG-02). (6) The time-entry rate pre-fills from the case's hourly rate (AG-13); hourly cases get "Bill unbilled time" (one line, entries marked Invoiced); the warning reads "above the estimate". (7) **Add cost** on a client or case (amount, date, receipt, label suggested from the service); costs show in Ready to bill and on close-out. (8) A firm-service case has one case-level document list ("Request a document" without a filing, AG-09). (9) Money dashboards, overdue ageing and a new **tax-collected report** (GST/HST) live in AG-14; AG-06 keeps the collections list and statements. (10) A Case Officer may create a draft from "Bill this" or "Add cost"; issuing stays with the billing keys (SA-06). (11) Keys: trust request and receipt, monthly check, "I moved these": `agent/payment:record`; Pay from trust: `agent/invoice:issue`; refund from trust: `agent/refund:create`; trust yes/no, invoice-payment destination and payment accounts: Owner. (12) One receipt number series per firm; MS-D6(5) replaces AG16-13(14) "no receipt series". (13) Payment reminders apply to issued invoices with a balance, not disputed, not written off; rows Payment reminder, Payment overdue, Final notice; timing −3 / 0 / +3 / +7 days (SA-05) on Timings; they stop at zero balance, cancel or write-off. (14) An issued invoice's due date may be changed, audited; reminders follow. (15) Exports: one CSV each for invoices, payments, refunds, credit notes, write-offs and the trust ledger, per period. (16) "Send statement" on a client at any time (PDF incl. trust), sent by a person. (17) Line tax: government fees never taxed; other cost lines taxable by default, changeable per line. (18) **Getting paid page** (AG-16): main — how clients pay (card account; operating details incl. Interac e-Transfer email; trust details), tax and the tax number (its one home), trust; Advanced — where invoice payments go, numbering, due days. AG-01 checklist gains an optional item "Set up getting paid". (19) Payment plans withdrawn: AG-06 plans, the plan builder and `agent/payment-plan:manage`; schedule rows replace them. (20) When a consult was paid, the first retainer invoice suggests a "Consultation credit −CAD 150" line, removable by staff.
- **MS-D22 — Saved-card autopay stays out of Release 1; schedule rows can say when they are due (the product owner, 25 Oct: "I will go with your recommendation for both questions").** Home: AG-06. Epics affected: AG-06, AG-05, AG-07, AG-15, CP-01, SA-03, DEV-TASKS. (1) No saved-card automatic charging (AG-6-49(c) withdrawn); clients pay each invoice by link, card or e-Transfer. (2) A schedule row's "When" is On signing · On a date · When submitted · When decided (programme cases) or When done (firm services); at that moment the row appears in Ready to bill; nothing is billed by itself (extends MS-D8(2); uses the submission and decision events AG-07 already records).
- **MS-D23 — Eleven scenario gaps closed (the product owner, 25 Oct: "have u covered all scenarios?", then "I will go with your recommendation for both questions" = accept all). Source: `scratchpad/MONEY-SCENARIOS.md` (56 scenarios).** Home: AG-06 unless stated. Epics affected: AG-06, AG-05, AG-07, AG-14, AG-15, AG-17, CP-01, DEV-TASKS. (1) Money received with no request and no signed retainer is recorded as trust on the person, with the warning "No signed retainer". (2) The monthly check shows "In transit (card payouts not yet in the bank)". (3) Each receipt has "Cleared": card and e-Transfer cleared at once, a cheque cleared by a tick; applying uncleared money warns "CAD 1,500 is a cheque not yet cleared. Apply anyway?"; a bounced cheque uses Reverse entry with reason "Returned", undoing what it paid (preview). (4) A payment in another currency is recorded at the amount that arrived in the case currency, with a note ("Paid USD 1,100, received CAD 1,480"); no conversion (X-6). (5) "Paid by" may be a typed name; refunds default to that payer. (6) The case header shows what is owed: "Owes CAD 2,000 · 12 days overdue" (AG-07). (7) The submission check shows an information line "Client B owes CAD 2,000"; not a warning to pass (AG-07). (8) Disbursements above the agreed estimate warn: "Government fees are above the agreed estimate. Prepare an amendment?". (9) A government fee refunded to the firm: credit note for that line, then refund or keep as trust or credit, by a person. (10) A payment that matches no row goes to the oldest open invoice; anything left goes to trust (trust on) or credit. (11) A payment after close-out was settled is recorded to the case and re-opens the close-out item ("CAD 200 received after closing").
- **MS-D24 — Follow-ups found while writing (the product owner, 25 Oct: "a" = accept all 6), plus 34 points settled under existing decisions.** Home: per item. Epics affected: AG-02, AG-05, AG-06, AG-07, AG-12, AG-13, AG-14, AG-15, CP-01, SA-06, DEV-TASKS. Triage: `scratchpad/MONEY-WRITER-OPEN-TRIAGE.md` (items 1–34 "S" are writer fixes under existing decisions; 35–40 are this decision; 41 external). (1) When a case opens, it links that person's (or company's) most recent signed retainer not already linked to another case; staff may change the link (AG-07 home; AG-05). (2) The seeded stage `PROPOSAL` is relabelled **"Fees discussed"**; the key stays, so AG-2-37 rule 4 ("Follow up" after 5 days) still works; firms may rename it as today (AG-02). (3) Reminders to a client signer who has not signed stop after 3; the case lead (or file owner) then gets one item "Client A hasn't signed Client B's retainer" (AG-05). (4) Keys (existing only): part credit note and write-off `agent/invoice:void`; "Client disputes" `agent/invoice:edit`; Mark settled, opening balance and the cheque-cleared tick `agent/payment:record`; Send statement `agent/invoice:issue`; Case Officer also gets `agent/invoice:list` and `agent/invoice:show` (sees drafts they create); the legacy `agent/e-sign:*` keys are withdrawn (SA-06 home; AG-06, AG-05). (5) The client sees their trust balance per case in the portal and on statements ("Held in trust for your work permit: CAD 1,500") (AG-06, AG-15, CP-01). (6) AG-06 v2.0's author defaults (D7) and AG-05 v2.0's writer readings are accepted as written; each stays marked in its card.
- **Open after the money-slice questions (25 Oct).** For the immigration expert: (a) may a disbursement be paid straight from trust with the client's written permission before it is invoiced (MS-D9); (b) may a firm keep a consult fee on a no-show or late cancel (MS-D15); (c) are employer-paid LMIA costs refunded to the employer, and may any be recovered from the worker (MS-D19(4)). For SA-08 (payment rail): can the trust processor account bill fees, refunds and disputes to the operating account (MS-D20(4))? For the firm's accountant (not a product decision): tax timing for the tax-collected report (MS-D21(9)). Not yet written into any epic: MS-D1 – MS-D23 and AG05-D1 (one apply pass to follow).

## SA-01B redesign — decisions (the product owner, 18–24 Oct)

Decided in the SA-01B redesign session (18–24 Oct 2026), combining the product owner's and the co-product owner's SA-01B work, the founder's AI-assisted design ("Solution 1", draft 4.2) and the XFA form filler. Full wording, evidence and effects of each decision: `FINAL-SuperAdmin-FR/SA-01B-REDESIGN-DECISIONS_2026-10-18.md` (the "Detail" column below points to its rows). Home of every decision is SA-01B v2.0 unless stated. IDs continue above both SA-01B v1.9 (SA1B-D40) and the co-product owner's 14 Oct copy (SA1B-D53). Knock-ons for other epics: `FINAL-SuperAdmin-FR/SA-01B-v2.0-KNOCK-ONS_2026-10-24.md` (applied once the parallel sessions on those epics are finished — the product owner, 24 Oct: "a").

- **SA1B-D54 — AI drafts, people can always add or correct by hand (the product owner, 18 Oct: "A is fine").** Same review, expert confirmation and second-person publish for both. Withdraws the founder's "no manual authoring mode". Detail: Decision 1.
- **SA1B-D55 — AI drafts everything, including fees, steps and tables (the product owner, 18 Oct: "B - we will have them reviewed by the SME anyway").** Safeguards: source beside every number; numbers confirmed one by one; AI reads IRCC pages too; where government publishes nothing the AI proposes and people decide. Refined by SA1B-D98 (large tables). Detail: Decision 2.
- **SA1B-D56 — Government forms fill themselves in Release 1 (18 Oct: "A").** Reverses SA1B-D28 parking. Refined by SA1B-D82 (answer sheet for every mapped form; direct filling per edition after the trial). Detail: Decision 3.

Expat ready has no role there…").** Officer sees all data, fills forms, fixes on the form, may send feedback to ExpatReady. Refined by SA1B-D89 (three sources, no case-wide gate). Detail: Decision 5.

Refined by SA1B-D86 and SA1B-D98. Detail: Decision 17.

- **SA1B-D74 — Prospect flow (18 Oct: "we should have a small lead form …").** Firm's own short lead form → optional PQR → optional Assess (staff only); PQR fields are the SQR's definitions. Detail: "Prospect flow" row.
- **SA1B-D75 — Self-correcting forms, five layers, no blocking (23 Oct: "yes to all three").** Prevent · detect (cross-firm hand-fix counts) · warn (box notices) · fix fast · show the accuracy record. Replaces the founder's cross-firm hold. Detail: R1.

- **SA1B-D85 — "Start next stage" uses today's version; a side-by-side filing starts on the principal's version (23 Oct: "a").** Detail: G1.
- **SA1B-D86 — A government change during an open filing: the pin stays, the change is shown, one click applies it; fees follow the date the authority receives the application (23 Oct: "a").** Corrects AG-02D P3 ("fees by payment date"). Detail: G2.
- **SA1B-D87 — PQR answers do not pre-fill the SQR; the assessment is an optional sales tool kept in the prospect module (23 Oct: "b and yes. but it shouldnt be mandatory").** Programme pre-selected at conversion; a link on the client's history; no re-score on SQR submit. Detail: G3, G3b.
- **SA1B-D88 — Anyone who can work on leads and run assessments may share a reviewed assessment as a PDF report on the firm's letterhead (23 Oct: "a but anyone … must be able to send … on firm letter head and branding").** Closes AG02-P78. Detail: G4.
- **SA1B-D89 — Three sources for every value (client · document · officer), practical review (23 Oct: "a").** Client answers fill forms directly; upload first; one review per document with only problems highlighted; conflicts side by side; source badges; main source and fallback per field set by ExpatReady; no firm setting. Replaces AG-07's "verified values only". Detail: G5.
- **SA1B-D90 — Forms copy whatever answer is recorded; a blank is never defaulted; ExpatReady never ticks a declaration or signs (23 Oct: "a but keep it simpler").** Detail: G6.
- **SA1B-D91 — One date, one reminder, using IRCC's real rules (24 Oct: "a is fine").** Status documents feed the person's status date; passport-caps-permit check; validity rules per document type ("issued within 6 months", "valid 12 months from exam"); fixed timings. Detail: G7.
- **SA1B-D92 — The unified model ("one fact, used everywhere") with the structural upgrades (24 Oct: "a").** Field scope; per-subject resolver (person or organisation); relationship record; lists with stable entries; one time rule; stable IDs with crosswalks; persisted resolved items; corrections vs rule-change publishes; operational overlay; fact revisions; "unknown is visible"; corporate workspace (employer seats, signatory, standard steps with "done by", case links, shareable marks, privacy barrier, LMIA filing summary); real-world flows; the document master (fixed categories; standard and ordered alternatives with "when acceptable"; dates captured at upload — OCR pre-fills, the client confirms, the officer accepts; fields each document supplies; status-document mark; one management screen); practical batch actions. Detail: G8.
- **SA1B-D93 — A fix on a form applies to the whole application, with a "This form only" tick and one "Also update <client>'s record" button (24 Oct: "a").** Changes AG-7-23 rule 1. Detail: G9.
- **SA1B-D94 — Eight mechanisms simplified so a person does them; scale design for hundreds of firms with thousands of applications each; load test as a release gate (24 Oct: "ok … make sure … performance will not be issue").** Detail: G10.
- **SA1B-D95 — Staff-only "Calculate score" on Express Entry and PNP filings, from confirmed case facts (24 Oct: "a").** Detail: G11.
- **SA1B-D96 — The employer's quick LMIA Check pre-fills the new LMIA file; SA1B-D87 applies to the individual PQR only (24 Oct: "a").** Confirms AG02-D2. Detail: G12.
- **SA1B-D97 — LMIA validity uses the fixed timings; length from the table; staff only (24 Oct: "a").** Changes AG-17-40, AG-17-50. Detail: G13.
- **SA1B-D98 — Large tables: confirm the source once, then only the changes; hand-built tables stay number by number (24 Oct: "a").** Detail: G14.
- **SA1B-D99 — Small confirmations (24 Oct: "accept all").** (a) family-link email sign-in link; (b) box notices Advisory / CRITICAL; (c) failed answer-key test blocks publishing that item; (d) second person per SR-Q1; (e) official names on staff screens only; (f) second parent sees a minor's tab read-only; (g) to-do days edited in the template; (h) Release 2 wish-list: firm SQR questions, PQR pre-fill, port of entry, suggested family programmes, automatic "suspected" state, past-filing comparison, automatic Express Entry profile-vs-application comparison; (i) scoring reference copied for developers. Detail: G15.
- **SA1B-D100 — A corrected box map: drafts re-fill by themselves; approved or signed forms get the CRITICAL "Corrected: update this form" notice and one click, then re-approval and signature (25 Oct: "a").** Settles SA1B-D92(8) vs SA1B-D75. Detail: G16.
- **SA1B-D101 — No answer key, no publish: form maps, scoring tables and eligibility rules publish only after passing an expert-signed answer-key test; a missing test blocks like a failed one (25 Oct: "a").** ExpatReady content team only. Detail: G17.
- **SA1B-D102 — Exempt employer offers: AG17-D51 kept; Release 1 covers the offer inside the worker's work-permit filing (employer job details + government step with offer number and $230 fee or exemption proof, warning while missing); full employer qualifier and translator tooling are Release 2 (25 Oct: "a").** Detail: G18.
- **SA1B-D103 — Individual cases keep two kinds of steps (Decision 14); standard steps on employer files only; the Express Entry profile check is an ExpatReady review-checklist item per programme at review (AG-7-29), unticked = one confirmation (25 Oct: "a").** Detail: G19.
- **SA1B-D104 — Six small confirmations (25 Oct: "accept all").** (a) document checks Advisory until submission, then CRITICAL; (b) assessment result order and levers; (c) a published number with no source blocks publishing; (d) load-test gate profile 300 × 3,000; (e) impossible version moves refused with reason, filing keeps working; (f) "notarised copy expected" on the document type. Detail: G20.
- **SA1B-D105 — the co-product owner's SA1B-X19 kept as a content-plan note: programme names follow IRCC's wording ("Family sponsorship"); locations and table columns point to SA1B-D66 and SA1B-D98 (26 Oct: "a is fine").** Detail: G21.
- **SA1B-D106 — The representative applies their own stored signature on government forms with one click, only on boxes marked signed by the representative where electronic is allowed; never automatic; never on a client's box (26 Oct: "a").** Changes AG05-D1(6), AG-7-21 r7, AG01-20. Detail: G22.
- **SA1B-D107 — "Verified" for must-verify fields = confirmed on the document review ("Confirm & use", AI on or off), or entered/corrected by staff, or staff-confirmed with no document; unchecked client-typed values raise a passable CRITICAL warning, never a block; no new mechanism (26 Oct: "as long as it's … not a blocker I'm ok").** Detail: G23.
- **SA1B-D108 — Lead form holds the firm's own fields only; no automatic questionnaire link to leads; staff send it with one click after vetting (26 Oct: "a").** Supersedes AG02-D12 A5. Detail: G24.
- **SA1B-D109 — Government steps are never hidden; "not applicable" per case instead; AG07-D22 Q83 corrected (26 Oct: "a").** Detail: G25.
- **SA1B-D110 — Services "documents to ask for" defaults apply to the firm's own services only; ExpatReady programmes keep the pack checklist plus per-case "Request a document" (26 Oct: "a").** Narrows MS-D18. Detail: G26.
- **SA1B-D111 — Shareable document types are set by ExpatReady in the pack only; AG-16 Legal settings drops the firm setting (26 Oct: "a").** Detail: G27.
- **SA1B-D112 — Announced government changes are judged against the case's target filing date (today if empty), re-checked on submission day; no per-filing date (26 Oct: "a").** Refines SA1B-D86. Detail: G28.
- **SA1B-D113 — All forms at launch: every IRCC PDF form of the Release 1 programmes is mapped, answer-key tested and trialled for the viewer before launch; no first-forms list; portal web forms get the answer sheet and the extension (26 Oct: "we are going with all forms").** Supersedes the order in SA1B-D56/D82 and AG07-D28(17),(27). Detail: G29.
- **SA1B-D114 — "Approve filing" = one click that approves every ready form of the filing, each with its own approval record; failed-Validate or changed forms listed and left unapproved (26 Oct: "a").** Detail: G30.
- **SA1B-D115 — The AG01-6 retainer block stays (no active licensed person → no new retainer); Decision 7's "only Validate stops" is scoped to case and form work (26 Oct: "a").** Detail: G31.
- **SA1B-D116 — Case programme pre-selected from the signed retainer, else the consultation's recommendation, else the assessment, else staff pick; staff confirm (26 Oct: "a").** Detail: G32.
- **SA1B-D117 — Standing rule "Real value, not nudging": reminders, notifications and automatic to-dos only where a person would otherwise miss something with real cost (government deadline, expiry, money owed, legal step); everything else shown, never pushed (26 Oct, the product owner: "unnecessary reminding … firms will consider it spam … intuitive but not poking").** Detail: G33.
- **SA1B-D118 — No automatic ExpatReady to-do lists on cases; Decision 14(b) starter set withdrawn; firms may link their own task template per Service; overdue internal to-dos shown quietly, not pushed (26 Oct: "a").** Withdraws SA1B-D67(b), SA1B-D99(g). Detail: G34.
- **SA1B-D119 — Optional "which stage" on When submitted / When decided invoice rows (default first submission); pack fee lines carry a basis and the retainer estimate multiplies by a pre-filled, editable count (26 Oct: "accept").** Detail: G35.

## Build review — decisions (the product owner, 27–28 Oct)

Review of AG-02 v2.5 and AG-07 v3.11 against the code and the mini-epic packets (`FINAL-Agent-FR/_packets/`), so the two developers build from frozen, self-contained mini-epics. Sources: four independent audits (AG-02 spec, AG-07 spec, AG-02 → mini-epics, AG-07 → mini-epics) and two re-audits, 28 Oct. Each decision is also carried in every affected mini-epic (`_packets/tools/decisions.json`).

- **BUILD-1 — O4 is replaced: the client file is AG-02's `ClientFile` table (the product owner, 27 Oct: "O4 replaced").** Home: AG-02 (C3.2). Epics affected: AG-02, AG-07. Client fields do not go on `PartyProfile`. The AG-07 branch's client columns on `PartyProfile` and its own `ClientCodeCounter` are removed.
- **BUILD-2 — Add client duplicate check: "Create anyway", audited (the product owner, 27 Oct: "A").** Home: AG-07 (AG-7-73 rule 4). Epics affected: AG-07, AG-02. On a match, staff open the existing record, make that person a client, or click "Create anyway" (audited with the user and the matched record). Add client writes no `DuplicateFlag`. Closes AG02-P58.
- **BUILD-3 — A person can become a client directly (the product owner, 28 Oct: "A").** Home: AG-02 (C2.1). Epics affected: AG-02, AG-07, AG-18. New C2.1 row for a person: (none) → `CLIENT` through Add client, the client import, a work-permit case or "make this person a client". The client file record is created; the client number is set (an earlier number if the person had one); `becameClientAt` and the file owner are set; `client.created` is raised with its `path`. No lead or prospect is created.
- **BUILD-4 — Duplicate enquiries are flagged and combined at lead and prospect level; creating them is never blocked (the product owner, 28 Oct: "Yes, go with it"; window "60 days").** Home: AG-02. Epics affected: AG-02, AG-14 (counts), AG-16 (Timings screen). Chips: "Possible duplicate enquiry" (same person or company, both open, same program or service or one has none, within 60 days; the firm can change the window on the Timings screen, X-5); "Same person, different interest"; "Returning" (an earlier closed or lost enquiry, or one older than the window, shown as history). **Combine** two leads, or two open prospects: one is kept, the other closes as "Combined into …", both sources are kept, reports count one enquiry, the kept one shows both timelines, **Undo combine** reopens it. **Not a duplicate** clears the chip for that pair. Same rules for individual (email, phone; name and date of birth for family) and corporate (AG-2-79 company keys). New cards in AG-02 v2.6.
- **BUILD-5 — Person-level Merge ships in AG02-ME3 (the product owner, 28 Oct: "Round 3").** Home: AG-02 (AG-2-16). Epics affected: AG-02, AG-07. The backend lead designs the safe Merge route (AG02-P12) during rounds 1 and 2. Until Merge ships, the banner offers Link and Keep separate; a pair that needs Merge stays flagged and appears on a "People to merge" list on the Contacts screen. Replaces Page 1 "Do not build yet: the Merge route" from AG02-ME3.
- **BUILD-6 — The old AG-02 branch is retired (the product owner, 28 Oct: "A").** Home: AG-02 (Page 1). Epics affected: AG-02, DEV-TASKS (section I). `feat/ag02-step0-client-contracts` (v1.14) is not continued; AG02-ME1 starts from current `dev`, reusing only the contact normaliser.
- **BUILD-7 — Firm-service case: Mark done closes it as Completed (the product owner, 28 Oct: "Completed").** Home: AG-07 (AG-7-111, C2.10). Epics affected: AG-07, AG-14. A `FIRM_SERVICE` case is excluded from BR-31's filing-based auto-close, from the filing-based deadlines and from readiness; its pathway ids are empty. **Mark done** (`agent/case:edit`, MS-D24) sets `CLOSED` with `closeOutcome` = `COMPLETED`; reopen follows AG-7-13.
- **BUILD-8 — Last open filing withdrawn: close as Withdrawn only if no filing was decided (the product owner, 28 Oct: "After-decision").** Home: AG-07 (AG-7-50). Epics affected: AG-07. If any filing on the case is `DECIDED`, the case enters After decision and closes as `COMPLETED` when its items are done. Fixes the conflict between AG-7-50 rules 4 and 5.
- **BUILD-9 — An acknowledged CRITICAL warning still counts (the product owner, 28 Oct: "Yes, still open").** Home: AG-07 (C2.4). Epics affected: AG-07. `OPEN` and `ACKNOWLEDGED` both count in readiness and at submission until `RESOLVED` or `WAIVED`; `ACKNOWLEDGED` → `WAIVED` is allowed with the waive key and a reason (BR-26).
- **BUILD-10 — A reopened case stays open until a person closes it (the product owner, 28 Oct: "Staff close it").** Home: AG-07 (AG-7-13, AG-7-49). Epics affected: AG-07. After a reopen the case does not close itself again until new after-decision items are added and done, or staff close it with a reason.
- **BUILD-11 — A decision can be corrected as soon as it is recorded (the product owner, 28 Oct: "As soon as it's recorded").** Home: AG-07 (AG-7-58). Epics affected: AG-07, AG-17. Per filing, whenever that filing is `DECIDED`, with `agent/case:record-decision`, a reason and audit; a closed case still needs a reopen first.
- **BUILD-12 — On hold freezes the target filing date's colour and reminders; the date is unchanged (the product owner, 28 Oct: "Freeze, date unchanged").** Home: AG-07 (AG-7-91). Epics affected: AG-07, AG-11 (Minder), AG-14. While On hold the queue shows "On hold" and no reminders are sent; when the hold ends the colour and reminders return; a passed date shows red and the officer may change it. Nothing shifts the date by itself.
- **BUILD-13 — An imported matter with no programme becomes a firm-service case when it matches a firm service (the product owner, 28 Oct: "Firm-service case").** Home: AG-07 (AG-7-63, AG-7-111). Epics affected: AG-07, AG-18. If it matches nothing, the import preview shows "No program or service: not imported as a case" and the client is still imported.
- **BUILD-14 — Notify the new owner when someone else or the system gives them a lead or prospect (the product owner, 28 Oct: "When given by another").** Home: AG-02 (AG-2-48a). Epics affected: AG-02, AG-10, AG-16. Assigned by a person, Round robin, Always one person, a booking host, or Link to their client → notified; Take it or a lead they added → not notified; imported leads stay silent (BR-19). New notification kind "prospect assigned to you"; `lead.assigned` (FACT) is raised at arrival.
- **BUILD-15 — The automatic thank-you to a new enquiry is a service message until SA-11 rules otherwise (the product owner, 28 Oct: "Service message").** Home: AG-02 (AG-2-56, AG-2-33). Epics affected: AG-02, SA-11, AG-12. Sent without marketing consent; acknowledgement and next steps only, no offers.
- **BUILD-16 — Imports stay with Owner and Admin by default (the product owner, 28 Oct: "Owner and Admin").** Home: SA-06 (§10b). Epics affected: AG-02, AG-18. Closes N2 (D8.69). AG-02's "import stays with Case Manager and above" is corrected; a firm may grant `agent/import:run` to a role in Settings → Roles.
- **BUILD-17 — Clear fixes A1–A17 (the product owner, 28 Oct: "Approve all").** Home: AG-02 and AG-07. Epics affected: AG-02, AG-07, SA-06 (citations). AG-02: (A1) a person made at paid-booking hold start takes the lead from `booking.scheduled` / `booking.expired`, and gets its client file then (AG-2-10, C2.1); (A2) the AG-2-68 Link test line about `Call.partyId` moves to AG-2-16; (A3) choosing another owner on Reopen needs `agent/prospect:assign` (default the clicker), and the CSV owner fact keeps AG-2-7's `assign` condition (AG-2-29a, AG-2-85); (A4) `envelope.offline_sign_reverted` restores the previous retainer status, clears `signedAt` and shows "Retainer not signed" again (AG05-D4(5)); (A5) an unreadable main phone is `PartyProfile.phoneVerified = false`; (A6) C3, C4.3 and C4b synced with the cards (`IntakeReceipt.completedAt`, `UNMATCHED_*` keys, `showInterestedIn`, `agent/billing-config:terms`, one `engageRequestId` type, one lead index, Reopen raises `prospect.created`, `client.created` and `prospect.convert-undone` name the same consumers); (A7) SA-06 citations updated, AG-07 C3.2 adds `previousClientCodes`, `mergedAt`, `fileOwnerFromUserId`; (A17) Engage places the prospect in the first Active stage in board order, not a fixed `ENGAGED` key. AG-07: (A8) every AG-07 client-creation path uses AG-7-69's client-file step (BUILD-3); (A9) editing a client raises `client.updated` only; (A10) opening a firm-service case from a prospect needs `agent/prospect:convert` and `agent/case:create`; (A11) C4 seeds copied from SA-06 v3.15 §10b (Case Officer has no submit, record-decision, assign or export); (A12) "own" clients include every case-team role; (A13) C4.4 lists the other keys cards use; (A14) an invoice from before the person became a client does not block Undo convert; (A15) C3.2/C6.2 point at `ClientFile`, AG02-P58 closed, `Case.priority` dropped, two new D9 questions for the backend lead (who ends a case link by hand; where AG-7-55's carried values live); (A16) `deficiency.detected` keyed per deficiency per opening, `deficiency.cleared` given its producer, `case.updated` dropped unless a consumer is named (backend lead to confirm).

- **BUILD-18 — Client file owner: facts first, then an "Unassigned clients" view (the product owner, 28 Oct: "Yes, adopt"; closes AG-07 D9 Q141).** Home: AG-07 (AG-7-69 rule 8, AG-7-75). Epics affected: AG-07, AG-02 (C2.1 side effect), AG-18 (import preview). Same model as leads (AG-2-85) and cases (AG-7-36): (1) Add client → the person adding it, who may pick another in the File owner field; (2) "make this person a client" → the existing file owner, else the person clicking; (3) "Create work permit case" → the employer's file owner; (4) client import → the owner column, and for rows with none the import preview asks once "Clients with no owner go to: Me · a person · Leave unassigned" (default Me); (5) a client still without a file owner appears in an **Unassigned clients** view on the client list with **Take it** (`agent/client:edit`, for oneself) and **Assign** (`agent/client:edit`), and a count badge for those who can assign; no notification; (6) in a one-user firm the one user owns every client (X-4).
- **BUILD-19 — Back from hold: no stale reminders, one "Needs you" item only when the date needs attention (the product owner, 28 Oct: "Yes, adopt"; closes AG-07 D9 Q142).** Home: AG-07 (AG-7-91). Epics affected: AG-07, AG-11 (Needs you, Minder). Reminders that fell due during On hold are not sent. When the hold ends, if the target filing date has passed or is in its amber window, one item appears in the case officer's Needs you: "<case> is back from hold. Target filing date <date> has passed (or: is in N days). Keep or change it?" with one-click **Change date**. If the date is still green, nothing appears. Later reminders follow the normal schedule.
- **BUILD-20 — Link onto a person with an open lead closes the newer lead as "Combined into …" (the product owner, 28 Oct: "As Combined"; closes AG-02 D8.89).** Home: AG-02 (AG-2-68 rule 4). Epics affected: AG-02, AG-14. Replaces the 20 Oct "Duplicate of another lead" close (AG02-D6) for this case: counted once, sources kept, Undo combine works, whichever button staff press.
- **BUILD-21 — A new lead that repeats an open prospect is added to it and counted once (the product owner, 28 Oct: "Count once"; closes AG-02 D8.90).** Home: AG-02 (AG-2-80, AG-2-87, AG-2-96). Epics affected: AG-02, AG-14. Staff click **Add to prospect**: the lead closes into the open prospect, its source shows on the prospect as "Also came from: <source>", and it is left out of lead counts like a combined lead.
- **BUILD-22 — Someone else engages your lead: one notice (the product owner, 28 Oct: "One notice"; closes AG-02 D8.91).** Home: AG-02 (AG-2-75 rule 12). Epics affected: AG-02, AG-10, AG-16. `lead-engaged-by-other` tells the owner they own the new prospect; no second "Prospect assigned to you".
- **BUILD-23 — Writer defaults W1–W13 of AG-02 v2.6 D2.1 approved as written (the product owner, 28 Oct: "Approve all").** Home: AG-02 (D2.1).
- **BUILD-24 — "Add to prospect" can be undone; the "Duplicate of another lead" reason is retired (the product owner, 28 Oct: "Yes, Undo"; closes AG-02 D8.93).** Home: AG-02 (AG-2-80). Epics affected: AG-02, AG-14. Undo add to prospect works like Undo combine: the lead reopens and is counted again, and "Also came from" is removed from the prospect. After BUILD-20 nothing sets "Duplicate of another lead", so the reason is removed from the close reasons.
- **BUILD-25 — Unassigned clients is seen by anyone who can edit clients, even with own-only visibility (the product owner, 28 Oct: "Yes, like leads").** Home: AG-07 (AG-7-75 rule 9, AG-7-88). Epics affected: AG-07, SA-06 (note only). Same as Unassigned leads; once taken, the client is the taker's own.
- **BUILD-26 — A firm-service case marked done is reopened with the same key as Mark done (the product owner, 28 Oct: "Same as Mark done").** Home: AG-07 (AG-7-111, AG-7-13). Epics affected: AG-07. Reopening a `FIRM_SERVICE` case needs `agent/case:edit`, a reason and an audit entry; pack cases keep `agent/case:reopen`.
- **BUILD-27 — Notices: AG-02 and AG-07 raise the events now; the notices switch on when AG-11's Notification Doer is built (the product owner, 28 Oct: "Events now, notices later").** Home: AG-11 (Notification Doer), preferences AG-16. Epics affected: AG-02 (AG-2-48a), AG-07 (AG-7-39), AG-11, AG-13, AG-16. Until then the work shows in the queues, counts and Needs you. The `Notification` and `NotificationPreference` tables on the AG-07 branch are not kept as AG-07's own; AG-11 owns them.
